Done (changes `tick-clip-clocks-as-the-original`, `request-locomotion-as-the-original`, `walk-grid-lines`, `find-paths-on-the-grid`, `weight-the-overlay-by-the-latch`; the salvage of the four 2026-10-17 slices left unmerged by the crash): the clip clocks follow the original's posture rates, clock block and sampler pipeline (keys blended, gait overlay, hold, keep), and bodies without a body tick run at 83 Hz. The player's locomotion request feeds the body tick kernel's movement decision (move vector, leg modes, BACK/BRAKE/RUN order, the ready gates in double precision), turn table and alignment, fitted to the bindings, Cursor Look, the crouch, the sprint latch, cap and thrust; the duellist and NPCs stay on `exa_ai::order::follow`. The grid gains the cost and layer at a point and the original's flood for the nearest walkable point (220 cells, stop under 80); the spawn snaps on the third tick, footwork step targets and the soft stop are clamped, and footprints are lifted and stamped every fixed tick (`NavFootprints`). A blocked look-ahead within 100 cm clears run and sprint and keeps the brake instead of stopping the capsule, as the binary does. Review fixes: the Run overlay weight reads the sprint latch alone, and the stop distance's sprint row also needs stance level > 0.9. Not in: the walking drive (the capsule stands in), the kernel's clamped heading, turn countdown, tone ramp and friction calls, the run blend's alignment bleed, the identity sampler slots, the second walk variant and the snap's height. The walk speed, braking distance and spawn snap were not re-measured in game; a `play exanima01` run of the merged wave on the RTX 3090 loaded the level (5900 props, 39 characters with their roles, locale 2, loading screen 28.3 s) and exited 0 (`target/verify/gpu-launch-91.{log,png}`).

Done (change `reproduce-the-character-record-routines`): `exa_formats::characters` reads every field of record versions 5–7 (looks, hands and worn items as id and flags, pools, seed, combat skill, traits, skills, inventory, roles with their weights, relations and per-level tables, saved state, loadouts, the reserved pair, powers) and writes version 7 as the game writes it: 483 of the 485 shipped version 7 records write back byte for byte (two non-canonical voice names aside). The constructor, the creator's reset and the copy through the writer follow the original. `exa_character::record::CharacterData` is the live record: its reference count and staged list, the underwear rule, the equip conflict pass (an item's equip lock above 50 refuses), the inventory, hand, trade, loadout and remap rules, skill levels and unlearning with the prerequisite cascade, and health saved and loaded in double precision; placed characters carry it. At integration the record's item references and profession bits replaced the separate hand flags and profession word of the record-hands change (`exa_items::possession` follows). Not in: the role database's parent roles for legacy records, copying item records between databases, the save package, the arena's loadout forms and the trade window.

Done (change `fit-body-spheres-to-meshes`, C20 of the physics plan): `exa_physics::volume` fits a rigid body's collision spheres, volume and point masses to its mesh as the original's dynamics-volume routines do, on the voxeliser's map in the builders' order: the grid over the stored box (short axes rounded up), near-surface fill, the six-ray solid fill with the original's one-sided triangle tests and approximate reciprocals, volume and centre, corner masks with the original's shift, share and mirror tables, candidate extraction bounded by the cell counts, and point masses from the triple-product barycentric weights. Of the 1503 shipped `Objlib` body nodes 1060 get their stored sphere lists bit for bit (526 with the builders' defaults, the rest with the wide-query flag, the other resolution or depth 8). The rigid body's translate weighs each particle and counts strain only with its restore flag, place-at-matrix keeps contacts and strain, and a settling tick records no velocity. Not in: wiring the fit to the limb, root and weapon builders (characters and factory weapons keep their current spheres; props keep their stored ones, as in the original), the builders' sphere sort and rest tetrahedron, and the settings behind the quality and wide-query flags.

Done (change `reproduce-the-navigation-unit`): the original's navigation unit is in `exa_game::nav_camera` and `exa_game::nav_pick`, checked against the listing: the align wrap about the camera's present yaw, the engine's planar yaw and arc cosine, the orbit rate in double precision, the pitch floor and held pitch, the free zoom per wheel unit, the placement, the preset record (with its double 3.94e-13 retention), the kick and the cut-away segment; the nearest-mesh pick (segment against the bounding sphere then the box, the class word's no-pick bit, the ground-plane fallback) and the kinesis pick, with the physics world's fixed colliders as a stand-in mesh node for the world cursor. `exa_world::scene_nodes` gains the cut-away probe over the drawn sectors (the scene word's candidate and kind bits, the overhead probe, parent and children carry), and the terrain grass scatter takes the cosine for x from the engine's own sine and cosine (an earlier swap reverted). Only the align wrap, held pitch and orbit rate are on the live path; the placement, preset, free zoom, kick and cut-away probe are tested models. Not in: the ground pick, the editor drag, the gamepad zoom, the stepped zoom's per-millisecond easing, CPU mesh triangles for the picks, a retail capture of the camera or the grass points.

Done (changes `run-the-locomotion-routine-as-the-original`, `twist-the-torso-toward-the-look`, `turn-the-head-toward-the-look`; TMotileDynamics kernel lane, wave 3): the body tick's locomotion routine runs as the original's, checked against its instructions: the run blend (√alignment × run × level, the double rise and its 1 − 0.3/0.4·enc cap, −0.015, f(2 − f) braking, −0.005·|v_y|), the gait table scaled from `races.rdb` by the race loader's rules (×1/83, ×(1/83)², the built-in stops, the backpedal negated, the feral sprint blend), the gait columns, the walk start at key 2/4/5/8 or within 600 ms at the kept clock, the boost ramp, the speed and direction on the heading, both stride clocks (round half to even, truncating frac) and the standing branch; the move/stop branches' stance factor, gait reset and leg routine by leg mode; the turn wrapped against the double π with the stance facing. The torso twists toward the look (tone over 0.85, ±1 rad by the engine's arc cosine, 0.02/0.05/handling rates, crouch and cast rules; the lumbar takes half) and the head looks through the head-look routine (1.2566 × 0.8, π/100 a tick, roll × 0.1, the 0.01 weight ramp, the look lerped by 1 − E²), with the engine's acos/asin polynomials, matrix-to-Euler and sign helpers exact. The review wrapped the turn against the double π, sent the stance facing's NaN and the yaw helper's NaN down the original's branches. Not in: applying the computed drive to the carry (C18: the capsule still carries the body and the feet's lag factor is missing), the exertion and head tilt the twist reads (0), the look target and its timer, the gait audio mode, the dead jump branch, the start's callback, held items' static-only collision, the leg routine's golem-suit halving and anchored foot, the reciprocal-square-root estimates (Exarust normalises exactly), femur posture, IK and foot pitch.

Done (changes `take-hold-with-the-hand`, `turn-the-idle-camera`, `dispatch-the-hotkeys`, `light-the-night-vision-helms`, `take-hold-on-the-drag-start`, `order-the-hand-and-the-idle-turn`; player_controls lane, wave 3): the player's hand takes hold as the original's controls do: the press's guards, a Ctrl prop drag at strength 1 in the tick's first two passes, a corpse dragged at 0.5 every pass, refusal and its release, the drag let go past 108 900 cm² (in double) or for a living character, the anchor on the level plane within 200 cm of the hip; the cursor's reach (245/300/350 cm, NaN passes) measured as the request does; cursor classification with the 0.08 mobility and 0.9 strength limits compared in double; the plain iteration's world-drag link in the body (3·√mobility capped at 5 cm). An idle player's camera turns 0.0001 rad a millisecond after 10 s; the key handler dispatches with its permission masks (Tab, R and F wired), and a night-vision helm lights the player (1200, 0.4, the ramp and placement, the equipment rule). The review fixed a schedule cycle that stopped the game at its first frame (now tested by building the lane's plugins), the limits' precision and the reach in combat mode. At integration the hand's reach replaced the cursor's 3 m mesh-pick reach, and the hotkeys read the rebinding screen's virtual-key table. Not in: lifting with an item cursor, door, wheel and cloth drags (level geometry), double-click and Ctrl interactions, item use, gamepad, the walk-to-cursor latch (read as the right button), the light's second off rule; the pick is a rapier ray plus character spheres and the ceiling probe a rapier ball sweep. On the RTX 3090 `play exanima01` loads (5900 props, 39 characters, 3.9 s) and exits 0; its frame-300 screenshot shows the room from another side because the idle turn has run (0.1 rad a second after 10 s, as in the original), so later start-room comparisons should take an early frame (`gpu-launch-98-early.png` at frame 90 matches `gpu-launch-97`); fixed-camera shots at frames 90 and 1800 show the torch pose unchanged (`target/verify/gpu-launch-98{,-early}.{log,png}`, `gpu-torch-98-{90,1800}.png`). A settled `--perf-log 5` run averaged 35.3 ms a frame (median 33.8; fixed ticks 17.2 ms for 2.3 ticks, PostUpdate 8.9 ms) on a host at load 8–12, in line with the 32–35.5 ms of the last measurement (`target/verify/perf/wave3-1021-a.log`).

Done (change `fit-shape-builders-and-the-dynamics-world`; dynamics2 lane): the three rigid-body shape builders fit a body over `exa_physics::volume::fit` (`shape::build_limb`, `build_root`, `build_weapon`): the sphere sort, the limb cut and the mirrored growth, the double-precision clamps, the axis order with every builder's side override, the tetrahedron with all four points scaled, point masses, mobility 10000/V and gravity shares over all four masses, so props fall at g; the factory weapon record is 1.1, even axes, 6 cells, no free corners, tag 2. The dynamics world unit's rules are in `exa_physics::dynamics`: the driver and its backlog drop (to now − (time + step), at most 100 ms of wait), two 600 cm grids per simulation with the original's ×1/600 cells, shift and rebuild, the body-tree helpers (held is 0x400), the affine particle matrix, freeze, the sector purges over three lists with their pair rule, the update flag order before the clock advance, the placement query and the watch list; they are records not yet driven by the game (Bevy's fixed step stands in for the driver). Hung props (lamps, chains, rings) register with their stored links as world links: exanima01 registers 4 713 trees of 6 023 bodies, 122 of them hung. Not in: calling the builders from character generation and the item factory, the refit resync, checking the world-link laws (including the slack the registration stores squared) against the link iteration's listing, and driving the sector grid from the dynamics records.

Done (change `reproduce-the-simulation-sector`; simsector lane): the simulation sector's grid and meet rules follow the original's assembly (`exa_physics::sector`). Cells are the round-down of ×1/600 in single precision. A static span rounds half to even and is inserted with one cell of margin all round. Bodies and sim objects have their own overflow guards. The two-way meet test is the original's slab test of each side's travel against the other's box grown by its radius, with no static threshold; corners count and touching does not. The overlap-only test takes the last overhanging axis, and the placement and static pre-tests are in. The pair stage buckets parts by their mesh box's centre, lets touching spheres through its first look, runs the meet test and offers the all-pairs walk with its two box tests. Registered props carry their node's box and radius. The stage keeps its lists between ticks: 676 → 580 µs per detection of 400 balls (`body_pairs_test::pair_detection_timing`, ignored by default). Not in: the static-collider grid, the overflow reset, placed pairs, sim objects, hit events, collider masks, sphere-against-mesh contacts, and the grid/all-pairs choice. On the merged batch (item windows, gamepad, grid environment, dialogue, dynamics world, simulation sector, mesh tools) the probe took 623 µs a detection on the worker; on the RTX 3090 `play exanima01` loads (5900 props, 229 lights, 4 713 bodies of 6 023 parts, 39 characters, 3.5 s) and its frame-90 screenshot matches the tree before the batch run in the same session (`target/verify/gpu-launch-99-early.png` against `gpu-launch-99-base-early.png`; both open on a turned view against `gpu-launch-98-early`, a session difference); an `--opponent --press W@40-120 --fixed-frames --frames 1800` run logged the duellist entering combat (Defence, then CombatIdle) and ten weapon hits on the player between ticks 976 and 3181 (`gpu-opponent-99.{log,png}`); settled `--perf-log 5 --frames 1800` runs averaged 28.2, 30.6 and 32.1 ms a frame against 28.0, 31.7 and 30.5 ms for the tree before it, interleaved, at load 6–9 (`target/verify/perf/batch11-{new,base}-{a,b,c}.log`).

Done (change `follow-the-world-link-and-repair-laws`; tetra2 lane): a rigid body's world links are one type, `body::WorldLink`, in centimetres and in the original's branch order (horizontal drag, the slider pushed twice, the spring, the plane's 0.8 removal, plain or slack with the step cap), with the disabled and last-pass skips; the soft weld, the limp slack link, the slack reset, the inverted-tetra rebuild and draw interpolation between particle states follow the original. Root bodies' stored links become world links, so hung props use them; the duplicate world-link system is gone. TTetraRBD about 51%. Not in: driving doors, levers and plates through them, the group-mobility blend, the parent-disabled grab removal, sounds, clone sharing and the node-clock draw fraction.

Done (change `walk-in-combat-and-ease-the-pad-aim`; playerctl lane): in combat the right button walks the player as the original's combat branch does (the sprint latch kept, a run beyond 3 m or by the Run latch, a look-ahead of 6·v²+45 cm along the facing that cuts the free walk and, within 100 cm, the run; the chevrons show while it is held); the gamepad aim point (250·|aim|+50 cm) eases by (0.4·AimSpeed+0.1)² in double each behaviour tick; the hand holds a door, wheel or cloth while dragged and leaves the request to the keys; the idle camera keeps the original's blocked-screen, open-window (the in-game menu counts as one) and dialogue or power-bar gates. Tested and not yet called (`exa_game::player_behaviour`, `exa_game::player_controls`): the aim bone lock, the 12 m contact list, the engaged enemy, the screen-nearest character, the attack wind-up and recovery, the item-use values and the timed list, and the play start and end activation centre. The lane's second pass was almost entirely invented and was removed, as were its duplicates of the cursor follower, `exa_combat::stance`, `pick::within_reach` and the click handling (a plain click no longer counts as a double click and no longer walks). TPlayerBehaviour about 46%, SG_PlayerControls about 25%. At integration the player plugin was made to create the aim cursor and play session resources, which only the tests had created (the game stopped on its first frame); in `exanima01` a plain click no longer walks, the right button and the keys walk as before, and in combat mode the right button now walks the player to the cursor. Open: the click and double-click windows and the item cursor; the reach room, portal and mesh-blocker tests; the door, wheel and cloth bodies under the hand; wiring the attack timing, the contacts and the engaged enemy into combat. Follow-ups found on main in review: the `CLASS_LIVING` name looks wrong; the spirit-form permission mask belongs with `exa_game::mind`. The footwork step is taken only past 400 cm² in three dimensions, a side key weighs 0.8 of the facing before the direction is normalised, the step's length is the reach times the legs' extension and √scale again, and a held key keeps the short reach once the crouch promoted to double is at least 0.1; the step is measured from the chest. A released walk's soft stop pulls the goal in when its squared distance, in three dimensions, is over (8·v + 20 cm)².

Done (change `reproduce-the-motile-combat-body`; motiledyn lane): the motile body's combat-side rules were reviewed and corrected: the step phase in double with the engine's epsilon; the step end, full tone, point push and rearmost-foot search in the original's z+(y+x) order with its NaN branches; the invented footstep fallback and ground query removed. The femur posture turns the thighs about the (0,0,1)→(−1,0,0) 0.4 lerp in the combat conditions only (wired in the sampler). Muscled bodies walk by their carry (the capsule asks no velocity; the body's own speed and root step drive the decision), with the head tilt, exertion, the look target, the start notice and the trailing-foot plant wired in the movement stage. The swing rules, strike body, parry trigger, weapon handling, stagger and recoil, burning and effects are ported in the original's float order but not yet called by the combat stage. TMotileDynamics about 37% → 42%. Not in: the start callback, the grip link bodies, the three-bone IK, the rsqrt estimates, the trailing foot's stand-still timers, the balance routine and about half of the body tick kernel. The integration compared long `--opponent --undying` fights (`--trace-all`, 3 100 ticks, an idle player) before and after the change: in both builds the duellist crowds the player to about 0.6 m after tick 2 000 and its blows land early in their arc (readiness 0.1–0.4, 1–2 cm a tick); its first approach varies from run to run (first blow at tick 600–1 400 either way). The crowding is older than this change (the player used to die before it showed) and is open: the duellist's combat spacing.

This phase carries the most risk and defines how the game feels.

- `exa_anim`: skeleton, clip sampling and blending, procedural layers
  (aim, look-at, stance, idle noise) that produce **target poses**.
- `exa_physics`: fixed-step simulation at the original's 83 Hz. Rigid bodies and
  collision for props and level geometry. **Articulated character bodies**
  driven by joint motors toward the target pose (an "active ragdoll"), with
  balance (`Rebalance`, falls, get-ups), momentum and joint limits.
  - Decision: **rapier3d** for level colliders, props and the player
    capsule. The character solver is in-house behind `exa_physics` (done,
    see above), so it can be tuned to match the original.
- `exa_character`: race body plus morphs (done, `morph-the-physique`),
  skinning, hair, apparel meshes fitted to the body (the original uses skin
  pivots and edge splitting, see `TSkinBuilder` and `TEdgSplt`).
- Player control matching the original (done, `original-controls-and-torch`,
  see `docs/PLAYER.md`): W walks the heading and A/D steer it, the right
  button walks to the cursor, Tab toggles combat mode (on at every
  practice and arena fight start) where the body faces the cursor and the
  mouse steers attacks. Camera at a fixed 20 m with a
  field-of-view zoom. The keys come from the original's binding table and
  Cursor Look turns the facing (`bind-the-controls`); X crouches
  (`crouch-the-player`). Still to do: interact.

**M3 "standing man":** a fully dressed character walks around a level under
player control, stumbles when pushed, falls and gets up.

Done (change `port-the-cloth-and-character-view-rules`; charcloth lane): the cloth step runs live with a mesh per cloth node (`ClothPlugin`, the level-mesh cloth flag), with the Catmull-Rom and linear keyframe controllers, clock and slerp exact; the character-view preview turn runs in the viewer, and the pathfinder install (with its level fallback) is modelled. Not wired: the character decomposition passes, the cloth blood/swept/pull/bounds helpers, the pin rule, the triangle binding, the preview pointer and aim source, and the player-control flags.

Done (change `click-and-use-from-play`; playerctl2 lane, reviewed): the player controls' click, double-click interaction, item uses, timed list, request mode, hand drag states, mechanism pulls and box test follow the instructions: a click shows an object with no usable section, opens kinds 0 and 1 and pays 1000 for a lock of subtype 3; the double click (the system's 500 ms) lets only the hand-held item end it and otherwise opens the container, and needs Ctrl in combat mode; a repair marks 0xFF0000 and the elixir's sound plays only with the potion; the box test takes the full affine frame and the wheel wraps in double. A click on the player's own body shows the HUD bars for 5000 ms and a double click on it opens the inventory. Not in: the item cursor and its windows, the GUI item drag, drop and spawn, live door, wheel and cloth bodies, the script registry. SG_PlayerControls about 26% → 40%.

Done (change `reproduce-placed-pairs-and-level-impacts`; simsector2 lane, reviewed): a pair with exactly one placed (settling) body runs the static query only and moves only the placed side, recording the opposite push in its offset array, and never strikes; the pair impact reads the parts' smoothed velocity (a tenth of the way to each tick's motion, so body-on-body impacts are weaker, as in the original); a part's level hits make level-impact records (gates 0.1 and 0.1); every slider link takes the conversion's 0.01 smoothing. Registration kinds, impact scales, level masks, the overflow reset and the scene's grid choice are tested functions. Not in: sphere-against-mesh contacts, the offset array's reader, level-impact sound and damage, the static grid and simulated-object lists. TSimSector about 54% → 60%.

Done (change `review-the-third-body-pass`; motiledyn3 lane, reviewed): the body tick now takes its footwork from the character's step reach, the stance score's side axis from the character frame, a squared, shock-gated step trigger and an imbalance accumulator (read by nothing yet), the original's walking ground weights read from last tick's grounds, the early gait plant, the kick plant and the 0.1 rad pitch rate while stepping. The review fixed a walking ground divided by 100 twice (it would have grown without limit), the hold bones (seven, as the capture keeps), the ease arithmetic, a NaN score's branch, the step target's height and the double-precision constants. Not in: the locomotion leg IK, the sole floor, the footstep sounds, the sampler IK; the queued step, shock hold and limp-body bracket are tested functions with no caller. TMotileDynamics about 46% → 48%.

Done (change `place-saved-corpse-poses`; corpse-pose lane, reviewed): a level's dead are placed as the original's corpse builder places them: every part sits on the world frame saved with the level (the bone tree matched to the nearest child rest offset within 20 cm, else the flat slots by the saved part table), the character frame at the restored root, no muscles and no sampled motion, each body registered at rest so it sleeps after one tick. Corpses lie on their chairs, tables and baths instead of falling into them, and the `LayingOut` stand-in is gone. Both restores re-derive only the local rows, as the original's do, and the solver post-steps a sleeper woken by a contact in that same tick (without it, corpses woke their furniture and fought it for seconds). On the RTX 3090 `exanima01` loads with its corpses in place and no limb impacts. Open: the retail runtime part order for flat poses, the dead body's scale word, saved poses on living placements.

Done (change `review-the-item-cursor-pass`; playerctl3 lane, reviewed): the item cursor is live: a lift without Ctrl holds the prop on the cursor, the pad's move stick (squared length over 0.1) or the bound move keys turn it at the original's frame-time ramp (0.25 to 1.0 over 300 ms, reset after 500 ms), and the cursor's ray places it against the level with the held item left out of the pick; a plain release leaves it where it was put, and a cancelled lift is put back (the review fixed a mouse-driven turn and every lift snapping back on release). Also tested in this pass: the GUI drag claim and next-frame drop queue, the use dispatch (door, character, world item), the used item's removal, the point sound, the wheel's ±π wrap, the door link's stop bit, the corpse role scripts on a double click. Open: the placement's sweep to first contact, the item GUI's slots, the item-record spawner, live door, wheel and cloth bodies, the right-press use cursor. SG_PlayerControls about 40% → 48%.

Done (change `review-the-fourth-body-pass`; motiledyn4 lane, reviewed): the body runs the original's locomotion leg IK while it moves (anchor and contact as the original reads them, the hip sign from the clip foot's stance-local side); the standing leg IK keeps its reviewed anchor targets, now with the double fold compare and the engine's arc cosine (the review reverted a lane rewrite that would have snapped every standing tick). The chest jolt ends steps and re-centres the stance once per episode in the walking condition; walking feet sound their race's step set with the original's two-draw gain and pitch; release races take shocks without a burn. On the RTX 3090 the player stands, walks and duels as before. Still missing: the per-leg lag the feet routine writes while locomoting, the floor-flagged ground query, a caller for the queued step and the muscles-off sampler, the rest of the body tick's timers. TMotileDynamics about 48% → 50%.

Done (change `review-the-playerctl4-pass`; playerctl4 lane, reviewed): the lifted item slides along the cursor ray from 600 cm before the pick to the first contact of its widest collision sphere, a sweep that starts inside the level being ignored. Tested rules: the lift's hover word (a level placement marker gives 0x10, or 0x30 for an `L` link; a free placement gives 1), the GUI drag's spawned record (both drag events alike), the use release (a locked door's key unlocks it or it rattles; a liquid on a character or character view; a kit on a world item with an id; the item window's GUI use), the door drag's stop bit, the Character and Spectate mode ids (table length plus two). The review removed an invented inventory-slot hover that had stopped cancelled lifts going back. Still open: the per-sphere mesh sweep, placement markers, the inventory take, runtime item spawn, the usable-section reader, driven door, wheel and cloth bodies. SG_PlayerControls about 48% → 50%.

Done (change `reproduce-the-camera-drags-and-ground-pick`; navigation2 lane, reviewed): the rest of the navigation unit is in `exa_game::nav_drag`, `nav_pick` and `nav_camera`: the ground pick (a sphere grown by 5 cm when the caller accepts the node and 1 mm when not, asked from the grown top or from the eye, the ground-plane fallback), the absolute free drag with its quarter-turn release snap, the vertical zoom slide and the zoom's cubed pitch offset, the editor grab drag and the mesh grab target, the preset's distance clamp, derive flag, handler set and editor scan, the viewport attachment and the 123-point jittered offset table. The stepped wheel zoom is live in the chase camera (120 units a notch; 24 touchpad pixels a notch is a guess). The review fixed the ground pick's centimetre grow values and zeroed crossing, the slide's product order, the grab's raised point, the scan's height extent, the NaN derive and the kick's sum order. Not in: CPU mesh triangles for the picks, live callers for the picks and the editor drags, the handler installation, the cut-away's callbacks and followed-object focus. RF_Navigation about 55% → 74%.

Done (change `review-the-fifth-body-pass`; motiledyn5 lane, reviewed): the feet routine measures the walking feet's lag (the animated ankle less the contact along the facing, against 60·size) that the carry push reads, and sets the late foot's swing flag (threshold 10·scale·(0.25 − phase), plus the gait's ground difference capped at 25 cm by MINSS, against lead × alignment). The Ground Query is in as a floor-flagged-only point ray (Exarust's colliders carry no floor flag yet, so it misses in game as before); the body tick's adapters, look rate and counters follow the instructions (support and balance, not scale and tone). The placement leg snap is corrected and kept as a tested rule (on the bind pose at spawn it sank the standing player 4 cm). The review unscheduled the muscles-off sampler (the original never samples a dead body; the lane would have snapped every dying body onto its clip) and fixed a doubled backward-lag negation. On the RTX 3090 the player stands, walks and duels as before. Still missing: the floor flag on level colliders, a spawn that samples its first clip before the placement snap, the remaining body tick blocks. TMotileDynamics about 50% → 52%.

Done (change `review-the-plain-body-sounds`; tetra3 lane, reviewed): the plain rigid body's impact sound is in `exa_physics::body::sound` as a tested function (the 0.5 gate, the cooldown or strictly-louder-than-twice rule, MINSS volume·1.5, the light and heavy mobility branches in double, a cooldown of 220 ms less round(170·base) plus the generator's 0–99 draw). Scrape and roll sources step at the head of every post-step: the point is placed by the last tick's matrix, the squared move is in cm² (the lane had plain cm), the curve and smoothing are in double, NaN stops a source, and a body without sources skips the step. A prop's stored sound slots become sources on their parts when it registers. Not in: playing the events through `exa_audio`, calling the impact sound from the pair and level impacts, the audio-name slot filter, the breakable body-link branch, the group-mobility blend, per-root iteration counts. TTetraRBD about 52% → 55%.

Done (change `review-the-sixth-body-pass`; motiledyn6 lane, reviewed): the living sampler folds last tick's knee, hip and thigh-turn corrections into its clip chain before the torso twist, re-places the foot under the bent shin, and the aim puts its clip ankle and hip record into the world after the carry (the lane had left that record in the root's frame, which would have skewed the walking lag and the IK sign away from the origin). The stance score zeroes the feet's height difference and uses the original's general epsilon, and a spawn samples its first clip before the body is inserted. The placement leg snap stays uncalled: even after the clip sample it left the standing player 4.5 cm lower until its frame height reaches the controller. Still open: the placement search and scene-node query, the look-point smoothing, the start capture's upper-body blend, the parry clash point. TMotileDynamics about 52% → 53%.

Done (change `sector-mesh-pairs-and-level-damage`; simsector3 lane, reviewed): the pair stage has the original's sphere-against-mesh query (a sweep on 0.1 cm² travel with no static-only gate, the mesh box and sphere pre-tests, a re-read after each hit), but parts get no triangles yet, so the game runs the sphere fallback, now pre-tested against its own spheres' bound so it can never cull a real contact. A character's level impacts now sound and deal limb impact damage (the limb curve, the 0.25 volume gate, the overhead drop, no striker message). The review restored the pair friction's NaN and at-epsilon gate and removed an invented static-only sweep gate. The static grid and sim-object list exist as tested structures. Still to do: per-part triangles from the loaders, collider masks, the live sector lists and sim-object stage, hit-event damage, the pain voice. TSimSector about 61% → 66%.

Done (change `correct-the-armour-fold-and-build-clamps`; chargen4 lane, reviewed): the armour pass's effective coverage, natural layer precision and fold follow the listing bit for bit (the review formed the fold's outer product first), a worn item of special kind 2 grants the second wind to placed characters and scene bodies (the arena's generated fighters among them), the apparel cache orders garments by the layer level over all twenty occupancy words, the build's feral and age clamps apply where the look reads them, and the slot walk logs the original's stack-overflow message and walks on (the lane's invented stop was removed). Remaining: special kinds 3/5/7, the hit-sound word and body-scale write, the relist and texture hand-off, hair cloth, the rebuild screen, the off-hand torch. SG_CharacterGen about 70% → 72%.

Done (change `review-the-seventh-body-pass`; motiledyn7 lane, reviewed): a clip replacing Walk or Run asks for the trailing foot, planted after the next sample and dropped 500 ms on or whenever the movement decision moves the body (the review added that clear); the thighs take the combat turn only while in combat and neither walking nor off their feet (the review corrected the lane's always-in-combat turn); the stance reference no longer renormalises its facing. The placement leg snap stays a tested, uncalled rule: called at spawn with the controller moved to its ground, it left the player squatting for about a second on the GPU smoke (pelvis 100 cm sinking to 51 cm), so the spawn path was put back. Remaining: the snap's live call, the may-act gate's use of the request, the AI step-end consumer, the start capture's half-lean, the placement resets, weapon bodies. TMotileDynamics stays about 53%.

Done (change `fit-the-body-arithmetic-to-the-original`; tetra4 lane, reviewed): the body kernel restores edges with the original's `(x²+z²)+y²` length and `(len−rest)/(len+len)` correction, adds the step's kept motion and gravity as two sums, and empties a part's motion and smoothed buffers when it falls asleep, live in every level (rounding-level changes plus a woken body's starting smoothed velocity); the volume quicksort is in the original's loop form. The review reverted the lane's 25-neighbour reading (the loops test their index after the body, so the scans keep all 26; the shipped object-library check stays at 1060 of 1503 bit for bit). Remaining: body sound playback, the frozen-body branch, the link-mobility blend, per-root iteration counts, wiring the volume fit into a live builder. TTetraRBD about 55% → 57%.

Done (change `run-the-sector-object-stages`; simsector4 lane, reviewed): the sector's static collider grid is live (filled by the static builder with world boxes and level masks, a thin plate being 1|4) and body detection reads its masks; only a living body's feet carry the foot mask, so a corpse's feet meet thin plates; the level's cloths form the sector's object list and take body spheres and level triangles each fixed tick through the original's pre-tests. The review replaced the lane's centre-to-centre object test with the original's sphere-then-mesh-box test, rewrote an invented object/object stage, replaced an invented AABB static query with the nine-cell scan, and stopped corpses' feet from falling through flat floors (the lane never cleared the foot mask on death). Remaining: the cloth's own contact calls, mechanisms in the body step, dense tables and their overflow reset, oriented static boxes. TSimSector about 66% → 70%.

Done (change `wire-the-worn-specials`; chargen5 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the armour pass's special kinds are dispatched in wearing order and feed a per-body item-effect list that runs once a second of game time, so a worn healing mask heals red health while it holds charge, a null helm hides its wearer from perception and refuses its powers, and a spirit necklace sets the focus bonus; these are live for scene bodies, arena fighters and a level's placed characters, with spawn unchanged. Remaining: the off-hand torch entity, hair as cloth, the dressed-mesh relist and texture hand-off, the viewer's spawn heights, the kind-4/6 light and garment switches. SG_CharacterGen about 72% → 75%.

Done (change `sound-and-freeze-the-rigid-bodies`; tetra5 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): props play their material collision sound on pair and level impacts through the plain body's collision call on each part's own clock, and stored scrape slots drive looping voices (`exa_audio::bodies`, `exa_physics::prop::BodySounds`), live; the step's frozen branch and each root's link-iteration count (2 plus children, 20 living, 10 dead) are modelled without a live caller; physics output is unchanged. Remaining: the group link-mobility blend, removing a broken grab with its parent's chain, a live builder for the volume fit. TTetraRBD about 57% → 64%.

Done (change `capture-half-the-twist-and-reset-placements`; motiledyn8 pass by a Claude agent): a plain start capture keeps half of the twist in the lumbar and thorax, against last tick's twist for starts made earlier in the tick (live, spine only); the AI's step gate holds while the trailing-foot request is pending (live); the struck item's blow sound (chosen by the record's split along the item) and the placement's twist, heading and leg-correction resets are tested functions. Remaining: wiring the struck item sound once item records carry the split, the planted-foot flag's reader, calling the placement resets from a teleport path, the damage routine's remainder and weapon bodies. TMotileDynamics about 53% → 54%.

Done (changes `match-the-cloth-contact-cost`, `count-the-cloth-stage`; simsector5 pass by a Claude agent): the cloth contacts follow the original's cost rules: a cloth skips its own node's mesh, level pushes never wake it and slide it 0.1 cm, its sleep countdown reads the summed particle velocity (the old port read the mean step including gravity, so a hanging cloth never slept), and a sleeping cloth meets only awake bodies whose padded spheres reach its box. On exanima01 the cloth stage fell from about 55 ms to about 12.7 ms a tick, but a few banners next to props still never sleep, so the contacts stay opt-in (`EXA_CLOTH_CONTACTS=1`) with per-cloth counters behind `EXA_DEBUG_CLOTH=1`. Remaining: why those banners stay awake (body spheres of nearby props over their rest pose), the per-body cloth-triangle candidate list, mechanisms in the body step, dense tables, oriented static boxes. TSimSector about 70% → 72%, TVCCloth 84% → 88%.

Done (change `push-the-cloth-off-bodies`; simsector6 pass by a Claude agent): the cloth's body contact follows the original: triangles are pushed off each body sphere into a de-duplicated buffer applied by a post-body push pass with three plane-holding constraint passes, the countdown trade keeps sleeping props from holding a cloth awake, a cloth sleeps under 2 at the tick's end, and a per-particle level-triangle grid cut the level tests on exanima01 from about 200 000 to about 80 000 a tick. Five banners still stay awake at countdown 32 under 11 nearby bodies (about 8 ms a tick), so the contacts stay opt-in. Remaining: why those banners keep moving, the body side of a contact, mechanisms in the body step. TVCCloth about 88% → 91%, TSimSector 72% → 74%.

Done (change `place-the-armed-parry-point`; motiledyn10 pass by a Claude agent): TMotileDynamics was audited routine by routine (`docs/re/motiledyn-map.md`): about 72% of its bytes are reproduced and 63% are in code the running game calls; the earlier rating undercounted ports that are tested but not wired (bite, struck-item sound, weapon handling, Break Tangle, the two-hand group, swing pushes, the clinch clear). The armed parry's point geometry and grip arithmetic were ported and the AI's stance-by-energy compares fixed to double. Next: wire what is already ported into the hit path, the body tick kernel's remaining blocks, the character init's presets, then the pose-only sampler and re-placement routines. TMotileDynamics rated about 55% → 63% (live code).

Done (change `read-the-captured-planted-feet`; motiledyn9 pass by a Claude agent): every capture records whether each foot touched (live, data only); the muscles-off sampler leads in from its capture without ease, reads the capture's flag as its source, and puts its feet down, lifts or plants them as the original does; the swing voice's start, the start's clothing rustle, the per-tick fade of held voices and the walking plant's clothing rustle are tested functions. Remaining: playing the swing voice and rustles through the audio instead of the window-open whoosh, the foot's water splash, scheduling the muscles-off sampler, the damage routine's remainder and weapon bodies, and a full audit of the body tick's large blocks. TMotileDynamics about 54% → 55%.

Done (changes `pin-the-cloth-by-its-face-flags`, `start-placed-cloths-asleep`, `turn-on-the-cloth-contacts`; simsector7 pass by a Claude agent): the cloth is pinned as the original's controller lists it from the mesh's face flags (every shipped cloth marks only its hem, so the hem holds and the rest hangs free; the old guess held every vertex at 0.1 toward its rest pose), one particle per position element, and a placed cloth takes its saved positions from the level's frame and starts asleep from its record's countdown. The five exanima01 banners that never slept had been read as node-local and heaped near the world origin with equally misplaced wall rings. With these fixes all 14 cloths sleep from the load (the stage about 1.6 ms a tick, 59 FPS), so the cloth contacts are on by default. Known divergence found: the node transform records are level frames too (all 1433 in exanima01 match within 1 cm) and Exarust still applies them as local, so sacks, buckets, carts, wall rings and chains sit 45–90 m away; fixing that needs the navigation's handling of moving props first. TVCCloth about 91% → 94%.

Done (change `port-character-init-presets`; motileinit pass by a Claude agent): the character init's race presets that survive its later overwrite from the race record (balance bonus, arm relaxation, footstep volume, golem footsteps) are a table in `CharacterStats::derive` and drive the body's race muscles and the footstep gain live (humans unchanged but for the gain by height); the body tick's grip relax, weapon flip and block stamina turned out already ported and live (the relax now steps in double as the original); the balance routine's foot sounds are tested rules. TMotileDynamics about 63% → 65% (live code, per the audit map).

Done (change `port-motile-kernel-remainders`; motilekernel pass by a Claude agent): a standing body now has no locomotion drive after the alignment, as the original zeroes it every tick (before, a stop after a walk kept pushing recovery steps forward); golem links are smoothed at 0.995; the damage routine's pain sounds and struck-player jolt, the feet routine's flat-floor and hovering modes and the init's leg order are tested rules; the muscles-off clock counter and the jump phase are dead code in the original. Next: the pose-only sampler, the down body's ground probe, voice playback for the pain lines, the camera jolt and pad rumble feed, the cross-body bite grab. TMotileDynamics about 65% → 66% (live code, per the audit map).

Done (change `hang-hair-as-cloth`; chargen6 pass by a Claude agent): long hair hangs as cloth as the original's character build does: each cloth-marked node of a style becomes a cloth pinned by its face flags, the roots take the hair's built-in cloth record (particle mode, 0.2 drag, no padding or slide, two passes) while the level's cloths keep theirs; the particle mode makes all its passes in the step and pushes particles off body spheres (1 cm padding, none on the owning head) with the original's stick-or-slide friction; a hair cloth never sleeps below its head's countdown and keeps its world motion when the head moves. It is opt-in (`EXA_HAIR_CLOTH=1`, change `gate-the-hair-cloth`): on exanima01 every awake hair was offered every body part of the level and the game fell to 16 FPS, so the stage must cull the bodies it offers an owned cloth first. SG_CharacterGen about 75% → 77%, TVCCloth 94% → 96%.

Done (change `step-cloths-at-once`; haircost pass by a Claude agent): the cloth stage steps its cloths in parallel and writes their meshes once a frame after the fixed ticks (bit-identical results), and `EXA_DEBUG_CLOTH` logs the stage split, the write-back and the whole fixed tick. On exanima01 with hair on (`EXA_HAIR_CLOTH=1`) the fixed schedule fell to about 7.6 ms a tick at 1.4 ticks a frame and the level runs at 54–59 FPS instead of 16; most of the remaining stage cost is bucketing the level's body parts (about 1.6 ms a tick), so the hair cloth stays opt-in until that is cheaper.

Done (changes `cull-the-bodies-a-cloth-is-offered`, `keep-the-hair-cloth-opt-in`; haircull pass by a Claude agent): the cloth stage offers each sim object only the body parts linked in the nine 6 m body-grid cells around its cell, as the original's grid sector walks them, and the cloth object list's origin no longer parks every object; on exanima01 with hair on, parts offered fell from about 108 300 to about 1 600 a tick and the stage from 8 to 3.8 ms, but the level still ran at 16 FPS (the fixed schedule 12.5 ms a tick), so the hair cloth stays opt-in until the rest of its cost (outside the counted stage) is found. TSimSector about 74% → 75%.

Done (change `port-motile-samplers-and-placement`; motilesampler pass by a Claude agent): the samplers' heights (stand height, leg reach, the placement's and living pelvis targets), the pose-only key pick and root source, the queued-clip switch, the placement routines' arithmetic and the down body's ground probe (its offset is the zone origin, zero in Exarust) are tested rules; the living sampler's hunch now acts on the thorax's own placement after the twist (live, feral characters only). TMotileDynamics is about 78% reproduced, 66% live. Remaining: wiring the placement arithmetic with the leg snap's frame-height write-back (the cause of the round-23 squat), the probe's ground field, the pose-only per-part routing, exertion.

Done (change `index-the-cloth-body-parts`; hairon pass by a Claude agent): the cloth stage keeps a persistent cell index of the body parts, re-buckets only bodies whose ragdoll changed, reads only the cells around its cloths in the order a full re-bucket gives and finds hair owners through the index (offers and particles bit-identical; the original offers sleeping bodies to awake objects, so they are not skipped). On exanima01 on the RTX 3090 the parts split fell to about 0.5 ms a tick (about 19 bodies re-bucketed), the fixed schedule to about 6.4 ms a tick, and the level holds 60 FPS with long hair hanging as cloth, which is now on by default (`EXA_HAIR_CLOTH=0` turns it off).

Done (change `give-prop-parts-their-mesh-triangles`; simsector7 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): prop parts carry their node mesh's triangles, so a prop collides by its mesh (one-sided, as the original's sphere-against-mesh queries) whenever it is the less mobile side of a pair; the review stopped the lane copying every mesh node's triangles at load and fixed a clippy failure the lane had reported as passing. exanima01: 60 FPS. TSimSector 75% → 76%.

Done (objdb3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the object database's editor look roll, container accessors, old-layout paint seed and the migration's factory step follow the original. SG_ObjectDB2 90% → 92%.

Round 39 motiledyn9: the recovery step's end picks its foot as the original does (skips anchored feet not yet reached, the just-under-maximum sentinel so a NaN never wins, first leg when none wins), live from the balance routine; the cadence target rounds through a 64-bit conversion (NaN gives 0) and the pitch impulse uses the engine's sign, both still tested functions.

Round 39 playerctl10: the lift refuses an overlapping placement marker; review fixed the action word width and made the overlap spheres lazy.

Round 39 simsector8: cloth level contacts gathered after the step and the placement overlap query; review made its boxes oriented and let a NaN distance pass.

Round 40 tetra9: the body's clone slot and shared sphere arrays; review fixed a rebase build break and the clone's link range remap.

Round 40 wireui: the use cursor, the GUI-to-world drop and the scripts' GameEvent background unlock run live; the light toggle reads the held part's lit mark, so a lit torch dropped on the doll goes out on the first press.

Round 41 navigation4: the camera's integer tick clock, the orbit release snap and the exact grass scatter live; review fixed the cell side precision, the node-frame transform order, the callback slot and the redraw byte.

Round 42 motiledyn10: the body tick's link-iteration ramp (5 while dormant, else one a tick toward round((1 - 0.25 feral) 1660/83), half to even) wired into the body timers, and the default spawn samples its first clip by the pose-only sampler's key pick; review removed three duplicate blocks the lane had ported with errors.

Round 42 playerctl11: the lift's placement limit and last frame, the world use cursor and drop record wiring; review fixed nine rules including the wheel NaN, the timed list order, the repair copies' offsets (now sharing the refresh passes) and the tagged use id.

Round 46 checkfig (P3, G6): the checkpoint is a level trigger volume in exanima01 whose routine calls CheckpointSave; the exe never calls it. Also corrected the npc-manager spec: zone kind 2 is the golem alarm and 0x20/0x21 the level exit.

### Phase 4 — Combat and items (started)

Done (change `add-combat-prototype`): held weapons, attacks steered by the
mouse, hits between blade and body parts, knock-downs and a duellist.
Done (change `add-weapon-factories`): weapons assembled from the factory
recipes with the original's seeded choices, deforms and painted parts.
Done (change `add-damage-model`): weapon stats, apparel protection and
races' natural armour decoded, and every blow resolved by the original's
model: the zone along the weapon, the speed of the struck point, two armour
layers per part with the coverage roll, impact, crush, slash and pierce.
Done (change `start-no-clip-on-a-wound`): a blow that does not kill starts
no clip on the struck fighter, as in the original (Exarust used to play
Head Pain, a story clip, for any wound to the chest, head or arms, and
Rebalance below); a fighter struck mid-attack stopped it and returned to
its stance at once, Exarust's stand-in until the swing stall
(`run-the-swing-clock`); the
shipped elbows and knees are shown to be hinges bounded by their links
(elbow straight to 120°, knee 10° to 130°)
([formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md), Damage;
[formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md)).
Done (change `choose-the-moveset-from-held-items`): what a character holds
picks its moveset, stance class and weapon class as the original's rule
does, in and out of combat mode (the fists the race's own moveset, a
one-handed weapon "One Handed", two-handers by their flags, polearms,
shields, a torch in either hand), for the player and the duellist; the
player's left click picks the original's attack (the main side at random
without repeats, the other side with the cursor more than 20° left of the
facing, an overhead on a double click), so unarmed it throws the "Default"
punches, which cannot land yet; in combat mode the heading turns to the
cursor beyond 30° and the body turns toward it
([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), Combat mode and Moveset from held items).
Done (change `take-the-combat-stance`): every fighter keeps the
original's stance, the looping clip it returns to; in combat mode the
player stands in the Defence stance (unarmed, the fists-up guard of the
reference shots) and drops to Cmbt - Idle with its target within 3.5 m or
for 3 s after its attack, and stays there when the target leaves, as the
original's body tick never switches away from Cmbt - Idle; practice and
arena fights start in combat mode once the level shows; the duellist
enters combat in its guard and guards at four reaches or more; standing
3 s in Cmbt - Idle halves a blow's wound (readiness); `--still-opponent`
shows the stances at a chosen distance ([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), The
combat stance).
Done (change `draw-world-cursor`): the original's world cursor in place of
the system pointer, from `cursors.rfi` at the GUI scale's size (30 px in
a 900-pixel window): the red ring in combat mode, the arrowhead after
death or with Ctrl, amber chevrons walking to the cursor and the
arrowhead or cross outside combat; the cursor's point on the 80 cm aim
plane, found through the supersampled camera's viewport
([UI.md](UI.md), Interface scale and World cursor).
Done (change `classify-a-click`): a free hinge is a door, a dead character a corpse and a living one a person. A prop lighter than 0.08 is ignored, and a heavy one is ignored below strength 0.9. A point past 70 cm is turned toward first. Reach is 245 cm high and 300 cm away, or 300 cm high and 350 cm away for a door.
Done (change `limit-a-drag`): a drag pulls along the cursor direction by the nearest hit inside 1000 cm. Hits of 1200 cm and 400 cm pull 400 cm. A box centred at 270 cm with 10 cm of half-height is held, and 20 cm of half-height is refused because a corner passes 280 cm. A corner on 280 cm or on −1000 cm is kept.
Done (change `draw-the-health-bars`): the HUD's health bar (yellow, brown
for damage that will heal, red for lasting damage) and focus bar (full blue:
no powers yet) at the bottom centre, painted as the original draws them
(gradients, inner and drop shadows, frames tinted by `guitex.rfi`) and
fading in with combat mode and out 200 ms + 2 s after; the stamina gauge's
rule (no shields yet); the low-health heartbeat (a pulsing dark red border
and its sound, story levels only); the interface laid over the game's view
premultiplied, which fixed every translucent interface pixel
([UI.md](UI.md), Health bars, Heartbeat, Compositing).
Done (change `complete-the-weapon-record`): a held weapon's whole record
as the original builds it: swing speed and shaping, recoil, stance
blend, arm stiffness, second-hand place, swing sound and AI turn factors,
reach and mass, held by the holder's encumbrance and size and refreshed
on equipping with the race's factors; its length along the model's
rigid parts; the built-in fists and claws by race; the shield record and
its factors; the step reach. Held weapons and the duellist's reach use it;
fists that strike, the swing clock and weapons as bodies build on it
([formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md), Weapon record).
Done (change `keep-the-combat-idle-until-the-player-walks`): the
user's reference shots of the original's torch arm (a compact carry before
the chest) are its combat mode, the "Torch" Cmbt - Idle; exploring, both
games raise the torch beside the head with "Torch" Idle, and the grip,
overlay, arm muscles and tracking were measured equal. The exploration
steering now starts only the gaits, as the original's, so a player that
turns combat mode off in Cmbt - Idle keeps that carry until it walks;
tests pin both carries to the installed clips ([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), The
carried torch). Next for the torch's look: the torso twist and head look
toward the cursor (M8) and the open hand.
Done (change `strike-with-fists`): the fists strike, as the original's
empty hands are held items with the race's fist or claw records, each a
striker across its hand's box, the motion's hand striking (the right
straight, the left straight, the right overhand); the original's attack
executor for the player and the duellist: 80 % of the most energy, the
cooldown from the last attack tick on the 12 ms game clock (400 ms with no
primary striker, else 300 ms × its recoil + 300 ms: 330 ms for the fists);
the player's left button kept as a standing request each behaviour tick,
so holding it repeats the attacks, a held double click's overhead follows
the attack, and Alt stabs with a held weapon; the race's weapon fields
from its decoded stats; `--pause-on-hit` for pictures of a landed blow
([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), Combat mode; [formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md),
Weapon record).
Done (change `blend-stance-and-daze-starts`, after C10 and L2/L3 met):
every stance change starts its clip with the original's blend and ease
(the set-stance helper 30 frames smooth, the posture switch 30 linear, a
duellist's stance 40 or twice its primary's stance blend smooth); a dazed
fighter (energy more than 0.05 under its most, in double precision) takes
the Defence stance, its clip starting with round((1.05 − E) × 300) frames
of blend unless it is down or attacking, and the player's stance rule
gives Cmbt - Idle only out of the daze, so a dazed body's targets blend
back instead of jumping to the clip when its energy returns; the other
side's attacks start with round((20 − W19) × 0.6) frames
([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), The combat stance;
[formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md), Motion playback).
Done (change `sum-armour-encumbrance`, M42 of the master plan): armour
weighs. Every character spawns with the original's encumbrance, summed as
its armour pass does from what it wears (0.0025 per unit of each item's
byte, less 0.025 for each articulated plate piece touching another, less
0.3 per Manoeuvring in Armour tier, an unpowered golem suit 1) and the
shield it holds (its bulk, less with Mobility), at most 1: the duellist's
jacket and helm 0.175, a tourney plate set 0.625, Elestan of the story
0.77; its step reach shrinks 20 % per unit and the player's run blend tops
at 1 − 0.3 × it (3.2 m/s in the plate set instead of 3.6). The armour
layers read each item's protection as stored (made items store it scaled
by their condition; Exarust scaled it twice) and Deflection widens
coverage. The weapon record's holding pass reads it too (in the plate
set a sword's recoil and swing shaping rise, its blend by a frame); the
turn, sprint, steps and get-up clock read the same value when they land;
`exa-tool characters` prints it ([formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md),
Armour and Encumbrance).
Done (change `face-the-practice-duellist`): in `play arena`, which has no
opponent's start, the duellist stands 2.5 m in front of the player, facing
it, instead of behind it, where its blows fell 15–21 cm short of the
player's back and none landed. A duellist behind a player who turns away
still falls short: its striking distance (1.15 m between the characters)
stands in for the original's reach and footwork (C16, L11).
Done (change `assemble-movesets-by-the-tree`): every clip comes from the
character's clip table as the original builds it from `humanoid.rsq`'s
moveset tree (parents are the nearest earlier moveset of their name): the
race's own moveset chain from the root, the weapon moveset the held items
ask for found under each of its ancestors, then the "Torch" overlay. The
held items give the original's config word instead of a moveset name, so
a torch beside a shield and the creatures' own weapon movesets (the
golem's, the ogre's, the demon's "One Handed") now resolve; a human's
clips are unchanged. Tables are built once per race moveset and config;
`exa-tool clips` prints one ([formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md), A
character's clip table; [PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), Moveset from held items).

Done (change `collapse-before-death`, L5 of the master plan): a lethal
wound no longer kills at once. The fighter collapses as in the original:
its condition collapsing, Fall Backward from where it is with
round((1.2 − E) × 300) frames of blend, off its controller; each tick its
heading wanders, its tone falls 0.003 and its pins' gain 0.0003, its frame
follows its pelvis, its lift fades by the tone squared and its arms go
loose (0.2); it throws its arms wide with Rebalance once its balance is
under 0.8; at a tone of 0.25, 250 ticks (3.0 s) after the blow, it dies a
plain ragdoll that keeps its motion (no invented velocity). The cursor
keeps the ring through it ([formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md), The
collapse); `--pause-after-collapse` for pictures of it. The corpse lies
as the original's (on its back, arms out, a knee up), but the body
squats on its feet longer before it falls (Rebalance 1.6–1.9 s after the
blow; live 0.9–1.4 s at like energies). The stagger step's length and
target are in. The footwork that walks that target is in, and the
dying tick calls it. The held weapons are dropped when the collapse
starts and again on the first dying tick if they are still held. Death's
own write (`settle_death`) clears the may-rise rule once the fall counter
is past 1, or when red health is below 0.01 and the race's first flag word
lacks red regeneration, and leaves a body that may still rise a 10000 ms
clock when red regeneration is not blocked. Each lower leg then takes
collision layer 0 and its foot child 0x30, and only that foot child loses
its strike-chain bit; no other part's word changes.
The dying tick and a fall that has reached its death call that write and
stamp both clocks. The f32 value 0.01 promotes to a double just under the
threshold, so it clears the rule. A fatal fall zeroes both bars before
the call.
Done (change `regenerate-health`, M43 of the master plan): wounds heal as
in the original. Each fixed tick yellow health regains √red × a rate that
ramps up over 40 s to 0.01/83 a tick (0.005 a second for a human with
full red), held at 0 while the fighter attacks or Cmbt - Idle plays and
set back to 0 by every blow and weapon contact; a heal pool (nothing
fills it yet) and, for the ogre, Sir, the harvester, the demon and a
cored golem suit, red regeneration (1.25e-5 a tick, dead or alive) refill
red; yellow stays within red, red within the maximum, and death empties
yellow. The rule fits a retail trace to 0.0012 over 46 s; in game, the
player's yellow refills on the HUD once combat mode is off, the bars
showing while it rises ([formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md),
Regeneration; [UI.md](UI.md), Health bars). Not yet: bandages, potions
and the second wind (M46), burning (M45), the saved heal pool (M47).
Done (change `apply-damage-as-the-original`, M29 of the master plan):
blows take health by the original's damage call, its order and its
double-precision arithmetic: yellow −0.1·Y (half that under a potion),
then red −0.2·R with yellow scaled by the red left, except that feral
humans, skeletons and ancients (rule bit 0x8: 248 of the story levels'
451 living characters) keep their red and lose the red share from
yellow; only vital damage (every weapon blow; impacts once C22 lands)
kills, when it leaves yellow under 0.01 in double precision
(so 0.01 in single precision dies), costing red 0.1 × the maximum in the
story levels; anything else that leaves yellow under 0.05 runs the
low-health check, which now starts the player's heartbeat (and later the
second wind, M46). The "no death" byte and the player hook the original
also tests are never set in its executable (RB48), so Exarust has
neither. `--feral f` makes the player feral, for a duel in which its red
stays full ([formats/OTHER.md](formats/OTHER.md), Applying damage;
[UI.md](UI.md), Heartbeat).
Done (change `fall-to-death`, M44 of the master plan): a character that
falls into a pit dies as in the original. Each tick a living body off its
balance and still moving checks its fall: sinking faster than 5 cm a tick
it screams once (a heavy groan); with no ground within reach under its
pelvis and its feet (25 m) it is lost; the player hears the pit 1 s later,
its screen fades to black over 3 s and it dies at 4 s, anyone else at 3 s,
and the character leaves the world with what it carries. Unsupported
bodies now measure the ground under their pelvis for it. Dead bodies that
fall below −50 m are taken out, the player's kept
([formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md), Falling to death; [UI.md](UI.md),
Death fade). A drop from 200 m in the arena: lost about half a second
after the body loses its balance, the pit 84 ticks later, gone 334 ticks
after the loss. Not yet: the pause and a death menu at the fade's end,
the voice sets (the scream picks a human voice), the retail chasm
capture (RR18).
Done (change `run-the-swing-clock`, M22 of the master plan): every
attack tick runs the original's swing routine. The clip clock runs at the
primary striker's rate (now with the feral level's ramp); a swing
strength scales it while the swing stalls, and the stance returns when
the stall ends: a dropped request releases the swing (a click checks a
punch in 5 ticks, a held button swings through: 67 ticks for a punch), and
a blow dazing an attacker stalls its swing, replacing the stop-at-once
stand-in. The strike window opens at 0.3 of the clip (stab 0.4, overhead
0.5) with the motion's hand, the overhead's and stab's close late; a
released swing's next press within 300 ms throws the overhead at once;
the record's perks reach the fighters: Remise chains a held side attack
past 0.8 (a punch after 55 ticks, a greatsword's Attack Mid after 78: the
same rule gives the live trace's two-hander its 828 ms within its weapon's
spread), Feint renews a released swing; the stab's and overhead's start
blends; the attack turn by the weapon's handling. `--perks` and the
trace's swing columns ([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), Combat mode;
[formats/RSQ.md](formats/RSQ.md), Motion playback). Still missing from
it: the swinging arm's stiffness (needs per-arm stiffness in the body
controller), the swing sounds, the AI's own release.
Done (change `hold-weapons-by-the-grip-table`, L12 of the master plan):
every held item sits under its hand by the original's grip table,
replacing Exarust's invented 90° turn: the hand class from the held slot
and the weapon class (fists, one-handed and the first of two weapons,
two-handed, polearm, claws, the off-hand item and the second of two
weapons), the grip's hand (a polearm in the left), its offset times the
character's size (the ogre's own, unscaled) and the race's held offset
(the demon's 9 cm), its Euler angles (about X, then Z, then Y of the
hand) and a flipped item's half turn; all 208 shipped weapons have a
grip. The one-handed sword is held low, forward and across the hips; the
two-hander upright before the chest as in the live retail capture; the
torch keeps its grips (now shared) ([PLAYER.md](PLAYER.md), Held items
and their grips). Still missing from it: the grip links and modes (with
weapons as bodies, M24/M25), the record's flip bit, second weapons.
Done (change `equip-and-block`): a worn item takes off whatever overlaps
its occupancy; displaced braies are dropped and return when the groin is
bare; a container and a pack exchange an item by moving its id. A parry
stops the blow. A shield that is not parrying deals yellow
0.05·(1 − stamina)·(0.4 + 0.4·impact)·leak and no lasting wound.
Done (change `place-items-in-a-pane`): an inventory pane places icons at
pixels. A drop from outside adds the icon and its bulk and clamps it; a
drop from the same pane or the linked pane only moves it, and a linked
move is not refused for capacity. Ending the drag removes the icon. The
hit is the top-most rectangle.
Done (change `mark-a-container-drop`): a shared reject bit is refused. A required mask must match. An own-only pane takes only an id it already lists with the low bit set. A limit of 0 does not refuse for size, and a capacity of 0 does not refuse for bulk.
Done (change `choose-a-card-page`): an id below 1, a container, a missing model, and a mark of 0 have no card. A map has no stats page. Seven coins print 1, and the high bit keeps 7. A withheld weapon of armour 6 is apparel, armour 0 is a shield, and armour 7 is plain. Three coins print 0.
Done (change `show-a-weapon-bar`): slash 5 and pierce 0 are absent. Crush 6 is 0.3. Thrust 20 is 1.
Done (change `show-an-apparel-bar`): coverage 5 is 0.25. Impact 5, crush 0, and encumbrance 5 are absent. Slash 6 is 0.3. Pierce 20 is 1.
Done (change `place-a-card-icon`): a plain card of span 268 and content 160 places an icon of 160 at 54, 16 with a unit of 4. Content 100 on a span of 269 places it at 54, −14. A stats picture of 40 or 44 stays at −28, 4. A picture of 45 is −27, 4. A picture of 160 is 88, 4.
Done (change `show-a-shield-bar`): weight 5 is 0.05 and impact 20 is 1. Encumbrance 0 is absent. Encumbrance 1 is 0.05. Flags 0x02000000 store weight 1, impact 0, and encumbrance 8.
Done (change `show-rank-in-the-arena`): menu 0 entering arena 8 turns rank and points on. Arena entering story turns them off. Story entering practice turns them off. Staying in practice, leaving mode 6 for practice, and leaving arena for mode 1 keep them on. Skill 0x21 is Aspirant and 0.5. Skill 0xc0 is a dash. Skill 0xe0 has no name. Skill 0x1f is 15.5. A closed gate shows neither.
Done (change `pick-a-description-phrase`): value 11 misses a row of 0 to 10 and keeps roll 7. Value 5 turns "fine" into " fine." and the roll into 1. Roll 16 chooses " beta." and becomes 0x80884051. "fine x" and "Fine x" add nothing before "fine work". "fin x" and a bare "fine" become " fine work.". A comma phrase becomes ", but torn.". "the x" becomes " the item here.".
Done (change `wrap-a-weapon-sentence`): "ab" stays "ab". "sword" is "A well sword sharp." at roll 0x08088406. "edge." is "An edge edge sharp.". Quality 0 on "Sword [x]" is "SwordIt is well, but sharp.". Quality 131 is "SwordIt is well and sharp.". "Blade [c]" is "BladeIt is sharp." at roll 1. "-blade" is "A prime blade keen.". A missed flawless row uses "ok".
Done (change `edit-a-fixed-name`): "ab" stays "ab". "Sword" stays "Sword". "axe" is "An axe". "Zed" is "A Zed". "zed" is "A zed". A mark between "ab" and "xy" becomes "An aby". A mark at the end of "ab" becomes "An ab". A leading mark before "cde" becomes "A de". "Za" and a mark before "bc" becomes "A Zac". The roll does not step.
Done (change `wrap-an-apparel-sentence`): "ab" stays "ab". "axe" is "An axe". "coat" is "A well coat sharp." at roll 0x08088406, and material 1 names the side "Wool line" without changing that text. "edge" is "An edge coat sharp.". Quality 0 on "Coat[p!]" is "CoatThey are well, but sharp.". Quality 131 is "CoatThey are well and sharp.". Both high marks stay ", but". "Coat[x!]" is "CoatIt is well, but sharp.". "ax" plus a mark plus "e[pc]" is "ax" plus a mark plus "eIt is sharp." at roll 1, and the side stays. "a coat" is "A well a coat sharp.". "coat." is "A well coat sharp.". "A coat. lined" is "A well coat sharp. lined". Material 9 on a mark between "ab" and "xy" is "A well aby sharp.". Material "wool" is "A well abwool y sharp." with side "Wool line". "oak" before "cde" is "A well oak de sharp.". Fourteen missed rows hide a fifteenth "yes", leaving "A  coat ok." at roll 1.
Done (change `stick-a-weapon`): an unarmed bite sticks nothing, and a depth of 0.1 sticks nothing. A depth of 0.25 uses flags 0x11000, strength 3, smoothing 0.6, count 60 and span 41.5. A float depth of 0.4 uses flags 0x211000, count 99 and span bits 0x4284cccd. A depth of 1 uses count 249 and span 166. A depth of 3 matches that count, span and strength, with smoothing 1. A unit of 2 at depth 0.11 uses count 54. The weapon layer is 0xC0. A part layer of 3 with a parent of 0 and children 1 and 0x40 becomes 0x43, 0x40, 0x41 and 0x40.
Done (change `parry-unarmed`): a mid swing at clock 0.5, in front and within reach, is parried left-down with blend 20. Clock 0.2 is refused unless the combo flag is set. Yellow 0.009 is refused. Size 1.3 is refused unless the defender has a shield. A blow behind, and one past reach plus 150 cm, are refused. A down swing whose table height is at or below the chest difference is left-down, and one above it is left. A bite of 0.25 holds the two parts together in the solver. A bite of 0.1 opens no grab and the parts stay apart.
Done (change `arm-a-parry-grip`): a cleave is refused. Handling 0.5 inside 80 cm has speed 0.5, blend 10, and strength bits 0x3ecf5c29. At 165 cm the strength bits are 0x3e4f5c29. At 250 cm and beyond the strength is 0. A passed blow has speed 0.25, blend 5, and flags 0x100000. A shield of weight 0.8 and stamina 0.5 has speed bits 0x3f4ccccd, blend 16, strength bits 0x3df5c290, and flags 0x800. Slot 1 is used only while attacking outside motions 8 to 10 with two weapons held.
Done (change `recoil-a-striker`): a recoil sets strength and the stall rate to 0.5 and clears the released bit. A class word of 0x90 with the continuation bit, or with clock 0.4 and a counter, stops there. A released swing with the window bit and drive 1 sets flags 0x27, ends at 1240 from a clock of 1000, uses blend 40, and sets both arm stiffnesses to bits 0x3e4ccccd. Drive 0.013 ends at 1200. A follow-through with that same skip writes nothing. Gain, recoil and speed 1 at zone 0.25 set strength bits 0x3d99999a and end at 1060. An edge sets strength bits 0x3eb9999a. Strength caps at 1. A negative gain ends at the clock. Speed 0.009 ends at the clock.
Done (change `stagger-a-collapse`): a body that is already stepping takes no stagger. Reach 80 at balance 1, tone 1 and scale root 1, with chest velocity 3 and 4, has length bits 0x42a00000 and target bits 0x42d7faf0, 0x42be0000 and 0x430ffca0. Reach 0.01 at balance 0.4 has length bits 0x3b83126f. The next lower balance has length bits 0x3b83126e. A zero velocity leaves the chest. Landings and the step kind are 0.
Done (change `walk-a-stagger`): a target 2 m ahead pulls the stance origin to z bits 0x3f6147ae and anchors the first foot there, state 4. A target at 0.5 m stays at z bits 0x3f000000. A target on the frame moves no foot. The stagger target from reach 80, balance 1 and velocity 3 and 4 pulls the origin to bits 0x3f072b02 and 0x3f343958 and anchors the first foot at bits 0x3f53f7cf, 0xbe4ccccc and 0x3f343958.
Done (change `walk-the-dying-tick`): the dying tick walks that stagger from the measured chest. A chest recorded at the origin, then a metre along z, then there again, anchors the first foot on the first tick and leaves it on the second.
Done (change `drop-weapons-at-death`): the dying tick drops both held weapons. Mobility 1.25 becomes drag bits 0x3e600000, friction bits 0x3f400000, and the striker flags clear. A velocity of 1.5 stays. The hands, the class word and the primary are empty. A second tick adds nothing. An empty pair stays empty.
Done (change `own-a-dropped-weapon`): the dying tick makes weapon 3 its own owner and copies that owner onto child 7 and grandchild 8. The parent list 5, 3, 8 becomes 5, 8. Roots are 3, iterations are 4, links are 0, grip 4 becomes -1, and the awake byte is 0. Child 7's mark is -2. The child's iterations stay 11. Weapon 4 keeps grip -3, mark 4, parent list 9 and iterations 2.
Done (change `step-a-dropped-weapon`): the dying tick puts a solid weapon of mobility 1.25 into the solver, awake at 256, with friction bits 0x3f400000 and its sphere. Velocity 1.5 is kept. One solver tick leaves the first particle at bits 0xbe3a7bb7, 0x3f7fa2b5 and 0xbe4ccccd. A flat shape spawns nothing. A second tick adds no body.
Done (change `meet-a-dropped-weapon`): that weapon's sphere, starting on another body's sphere, meets it in one shipped body step. The contact names both bodies, has one hit, and its deepest bits are 0x3ec39cd9. The weapon's first particle is at bits 0xbda3ae15, 0x3f7e43be and 0xbe492d0c, not the place one step alone leaves.
Done (change `notice-a-deep-wound`): a lasting wound of bits 0x3daaaaab is not noticed. The next float is. A non-number is not. A chest cut through the hit path records the notice.
Done (change `grant-a-second-wind`): a granted wind whose ready clock is 11, at game clock 12, fires, waits until 180012, and writes no heartbeat message. Volume bits are 0x3dcccccd and pitch bits are 0x3f800000. Ready clock 12 waits, and the heartbeat message is written.
Done (change `heal-a-second-wind`): an attacking fighter with yellow bits 0x3d000000 and an active wind ready at 180012 gains yellow bits 0x3d013a93 at game clock 12 and at 3012, and stays active. At 3024 it gains the same bits and the wind ends. A quiet wind and a dead fighter keep bits 0x3d000000.
Done (change `name-a-practice-partner`): a novice fight at count 0 becomes count 1 named opponent01 and is not last. Red bits 0x3e4ccccd with maximum 0.25 become 0x3e666667, and yellow copies them. Count 7 becomes 8, named opponent08, and is last. Count 8 stays. An expert count 0 is named opponent09. An expert count 7 is count 8, named opponent16, and is last, and red bits 0x3e75c28f become 0x3e800000.
Done (change `knock-a-fighter-out`): flags 0x80000150 at clock 1000 with delay 40 wake at 1040, are down, and have flags 0x80000130. The statue clears, gait is 0, tone is 0, and the pin bits are 0x3da3d70a. A countdown of 300 stays. A lower countdown rises to 256. The maximum clock plus delay 1 wakes at the minimum clock. A blow does not call this. No limb stores an impairment. Still to

Done (changes `add-item-possession-and-gui-rules`, `round-the-description-marks`, `read-the-record-hands`, `correct-the-swap-and-the-worn-panel`): characters keep their record's possessions (two weapon sets, worn and carried lists with held marks 0x100, set swaps on R through `ItemsPlugin` and an objdb catalogue, trading the sets only when a hand changes; giving, HasItem through nested containers, take-into-hand and take-out, loose items, golem cores, the AI's planned arms). The item windows' rules are in without drawing: the inventory geometry and callbacks, the arsenal layout and checked-out items, the ref-counted icon cache, sockets, rating dots, slider, coins, popup, framing and halo, the panel tint, clamps, held entries and the looted worn panel (items without an icon slot left out). Item copies roll condition with the six armour variants (the live factory-weapon pass shares the exact factor and byte wrap), price, desirability, protection, palettes, the apparel roll, template refresh, grades and the random-table fill (signed count span); objdb's container and random-table sections parse on the shipped data (137 containers, 24 tables). Not in: drawing icons and windows, re-attaching held models after a swap, instantiating table picks at level load, item-card text templates.

Done (change `correct-the-factory-roll-and-paint`): the factory roll, deforms, placement, stat pass and paint job follow the original: the colour roll by material class (shared with the item palette), quality in double, the stored flag after a full roll, the engine's packed sine and cosine in the bend, the parent's recipe scale at the attach point; the stat pass's own second curve and precision; every procedural material family painted with its program's terms, tile rules, per-layer tint and wear weight, the specular pass reading the painted normal map, and the plates' own rust grain. All 106 shipped factories parse, roll, match their stored choices and assemble with a mesh and a painted material per part. Not in: the shared atlas, texture-only overlay parts, emblem layers. In the character view a factory falchion and flanged mace are painted (steel blade, wrapped grip, metal head), not black or missing, and closer to the original than before (`target/verify/gpu-factory-95{,-mace}{,-before}.png`, `gpu-factory-95{,-mace}-compare.png`); the duellist's held factory weapon is assembled and painted (`target/verify/gpu-opponent-95{,-factory}.{log,png}`).
