Done (changes `finish-the-damage-routine`, `block-on-the-shield-arm`, `review-the-damage-routine`, `strike-back-per-contact`; dyn_damage lane, wave 3): every blow is resolved by the original's damage routine in its order and precision (`exa_combat::damage::resolve`): the speed cap and softening in double, the swing impact by the true ln/exp, the stab impact, p and its rejection, all four zone paths, unarmed 0.6·√(3I), the volume, pitch and per-layer sound weights, the coverage roll over both layers, J/T padding, β, the stab pierce, crush/chop/draw/pierce by the fast ln/exp, τ, penetration, readiness, the daze with Security, potion and steadfast, the bars and the blow sag. Each contact deals its struck side, then its striker (the follow-through by size, the wound bits, the recoil under 1.4), as the routine runs per contact. A shield takes a blow on its arm (stamina, leak, daze, the parry's lock and sag), a parried blow lands on nothing (600 ms lock unless Riposte), a body with state flag 0x400 turns any striker, and a clash or a block rings the swing once with the shield's generator step. Not in: the clash's counter branch and parry end, script hooks, sound playback and pain voices, Force Strikes/Shell (inputs 0), shield stamina regeneration, the bite link, the tracker-based struck-point speed (tip speed × grip share stands in); nothing in the running game raises a guard yet, so the block and parry paths run only in tests.

Done (change `correct-the-item-windows-and-character-view`; itemgui lane): the item panel's grid, placement, scatter and worn passes, its list and record codecs, drops, accept, hit test, mouse and card request follow the original instruction by instruction (single-precision grid shape with the round-up, the first slot to the last entry, the clamp after each pass, a signed placement compare, the shared generator, the worn push on the horizontal half extents, half-to-even saves with the low word, the record's id and save gate, drops matched by icon with no bulk from the linked panel and an icon-extent clamp top before bottom, the 200 ms delayed card by use type). The card text picks its condition block and names its pronouns (HeShe, HimHer, HisHer, HisHers); the weapon card's figures and striking rows use the original's mixed precision; a socket row commits to the weapon, else the apparel, section and keeps its charge; a gift an open pack refuses is lost. The character view's camera position, sRGB-decoded background, race-share easing, drag acceptance, drag start and release (hand, shown or dragged item with the pack's bulk) are in. Not in: drawing any item window, card or the 3D figure, a GUI host form, the record calls behind a figure drop, held-model attachment and trade prices.

Done (change `match-the-motile-part-rules`, new spec `motile-part`; motilepart lane): the body part's rules (`exa_physics::part`, `exa_physics::grip`, `exa_combat::{sounds, voice, condition}`, `exa_audio`): the part state word with the strike window, chain and mark bits, the sound cooldown stamp over the first child chain and the weapon's ancestors, the impact-sound material tree, the hard-impact reaction, the wound tally, the voice request and its spacing, the wake and status effects, the part-material hit sound and the foley tables, the grip link group and the attack reach, in double with the original's float order, MINSS/MAXSS and NaN behaviour, round-half-to-even and draw order. The review corrected the voice gains and unwrapped the scream wait, summed the wake in double with the grace sums in 64 bits, let a NaN armour layer count as covering, fixed the attack reach's double association and negative-age branch and modelled the strike mark bit. Only the part-material hit sound, the wound tally and the strike window run in the game. TMotilePart about 38%. Not in: callers for the equipment runtime and the grip builder; the grip modes, flip and shield links (ported, not verified); the clash branches, shield stamina, the status pass and the swing whoosh trigger; the contact-impact scalar on `Hit`; the held-item refresh and drop masks; the cross-body solver stage.

do: spawning the practice partner.

- `exa_items`: item database, item instances, inventory and containers,
  equip and wield, weapon assembly from factories, mass and centre of
  balance from geometry, procedural material grades and their textures.
- `exa_combat`: hits from contacts between weapon and body parts, impact
  energy and contact area (edge, point, blunt), armour layer coverage per
  body part, cut/pierce/blunt penetration through layers, injuries
  (bleeding, limb impairment, stun, knock-down, unconsciousness, death),
  blocking and parrying with weapons and shields, grabs and tangles.
- Practice mode: a dummy and a sparring partner.
- Minimal UI: inventory, equipment, health.

**M4 "first duel":** fight a passive or scripted opponent in an arena.
Hits land where the weapon actually touched, and armour visibly matters.

Done (change `hold-a-weapon-part-tree`; motilepart3 lane): the equip pass builds a held weapon's part tree from its model's rigid nodes, runs the original's two mass redistribution passes with the record's table mass and divides the root's mass word by the holder's size (kept as `WeaponMass`, which no solver stage reads yet; the initial mass is a stand-in, the reciprocal of the node's mobility). The blade length walk stops at the first non-rigid node, the handling element narrows once from double, parts joining the world take the original's class bits, the recent-impact counter decays before the tick's contacts, and a leaving non-limb part's blood-spot release is a tested rule with no caller. Not in: held weapons as bodies, the world join's second flag word and collision layers, the weapon-body impact branch. TMotilePart about 41% → 43%.

Done (change `correct-item-gui-second-pass`; itemgui2 lane, reviewed): the icon render plan (projected vertex and box extents, depth bounds, 20/30/70 framing, screen scale, the alpha-global screen pass versus the Kaiser downsample and halo, resize, stream wait), the weapon, apparel and shield card rows with their gates, title tint, value gate and refresh slots, the card window on the card timer, the panel's mouse order, the item control classes, the sprite-sheet managers and crops follow the instructions, and the item panel's `HltCol` icon layer is drawn in the inventory's pack panel (empty until an item renderer fills the atlas). Still open: the off-screen item renderer, the card, popup and parchment forms, binding the player's carried list to the pack, the per-level socket cell, the character view's 3-D scene. SG_ItemGUI about 48% → 60%, TGUIItemContainer 64% → 75%.

Done (change `open-the-loot-window-and-draw-power-slots`; gamegui2 lane, reviewed): double-clicking a dead body opens its loot window ("Body", 244×440 at x 400, one per body, pack and equipment panels drawn through the GUI path, registered as an open container), and a right-button click release closes the focused inventory or loot window. The power slot's draw (the inks, the empty slot's shadow and kind icon), the inventory preview's settings, build callback (camera at height × 10) and bars, the HUD reset with the GUI hold count, the status draw values, the form tree's eager layout and messages by control id follow the instructions as tested rules. Next: the character view (the preview bars and doll), body inventories for NPCs so the loot panels fill, the power bar's slots on the HUD, the deferred-close hook. TGUIForm about 76% → 79%, SG_GameGUI 60% → 65%.

Done (change `review-held-item-bodies`; motilepart4 lane, reviewed): an equipped weapon or torch whose model has a rigid root is its own solver body, tied to the hand, second hand and arm by grip links in a cross-body solver stage (`exa_physics::grip_solve`) that uses the body's own limp link law; it never collides with its holder or the holder's other items, keeps its links' smoothed lengths between ticks, and falls free when the holder goes limp. The weapon-body impact branch (hard impact, stab strength and stall, a sound gated on the item's cooldown) runs for contacts with anything but a limb, whose hits the blade sweep already answers. Factory weapons have no rigid node and stay on the bone. Still missing: factory and multi-node held bodies, weld gates and enabling them on death, callers for grip modes, flip and shields, the camera kick and base sound. TMotilePart about 43% → 47%.

Done (change `wire-body-inventories`; wire-body-inventory lane, reviewed): a dead body's loot window fills from its record: the pack shows its carried items at their saved places and the equipment panel its worn items plus any shields in its hands, laid out by the original's scatter pass. The player's inventory pack shows its own carried items, and a left drag between open packs moves items between body and player through the item panel's accept, drop and drag-end rules. The show rule reads the body's profession word (race 3 is refused), and the window closes once its body is no longer dead. Review fixes: held weapons no longer appear on the equipment panel, the show rule reads the profession word instead of the condition word, and the scatter's seed is labelled a stand-in. Next: the off-screen item renderer so the panels draw icons, the character view's equip drop, re-dressing a body after a worn item is taken, writing possessions back and saving them, copying level-local items into the item database at load. TGUIItemContainer about 75% → 77%, SG_ItemGUI 60% → 61%.

Done (change `model-the-character-doll-equipment`; charview lane, reviewed): the character view's equipment cluster is record-side rules in `exa_items::doll` (plan, apply, take-in, take-out by place with the first-weapon promotion, the shield take, redress and finalisers), and its pointer handlers are in `exa_ui::character_view` (hover pick, drag-start refund, drop, release). Weapon-set swaps now dress the moveset's hands (`exa_game::equip`, keeping the carried torch). The review removed an invented take-in permission gate (it is a wait for the item's node to load), fixed a shield take-out that did nothing, and corrected the apparel drop's hand-over and bulk, the locked-flagged release (answer −1, not a cancel), the shown-item release mark and the drag-start section order. Still unwired: the doll's off-screen render and scene, item nodes, parts and physics, the deferred one-step apply, the inventory and character forms that would call the drop and release plans. TCharacterView about 40% → 56%, its equipment routines 43% → 58%.

Done (change `run-the-grip-modes-and-flip`; motilepart5 lane, reviewed): the holder's clip runs the grip mode callback and relax pass every tick; a flip request runs the flip with the original's double compares and rebuilds the weapon record when it lands, and F lights the torch and then asks for the flip, as the original's branch does (Exarust still gates it on the light, not the section's flip-side kind). Every stored rigid node of a held item is a body part, the top one moving the item's root (blade, grip, fire); the soft (parry) world weld follows the auto-parry gate and sits at the grip for polearms; a non-striking held item rings the base collision sound, and a big attacker's hard overhead kicks the chase camera. The factory weapon's fitted body is tested but not attached, and shield links are tested with no equip caller. Not in: held shields, second weapons and starting flips, the death and go-limp weld changes in the game, the torch's soft weld, the equip/swap refresh, limit links. TMotilePart about 47% → 50%.

Done (change `attach-the-off-hand-and-torch-links`; motilepart6 lane, reviewed): the spawn's attach plan hangs an off-hand shield (its back pivot to its forearm, its face pivots to the left hand, a soft weld) or a second weapon as its own body; the torch holds by its own two pivots to its holding hand plus its soft weld; the flip needs the section's flipped-side kind byte and a non-switching holder and turns slot 0 only; a dead holder's soft weld stiffens and the corpse keeps its items, and a collapse drops weapons and torch and keeps the shield. The review removed the lane's limit-link wiring, which would have stopped the living muscle law for every character (the shipped skeleton link words are 0 and the registration makes every parented link a pivot), a duplicate equip refresh that doubled non-human races' blend and turn, and a misread collapse slack, and fixed a torch and a shield tied to the wrong hand. Not in: the shield guard, the gobbler collapse slack, the flipped record of a weapon that starts turned over, off-hand torch placement. TMotilePart about 50% → 53%.

Done (change `review-the-playerctl5-pass`; playerctl5 lane, reviewed): the movement word now follows the key reader (A and D bits, opposing keys cancel, crouch-gated tap) and keeps a tap moving for 200 ms in the running game; the lifted item's spheres sweep from the placed grab point, and a scene cloth vertex can be grabbed with a drag plane facing the camera. The review removed the lane's invented attack gate and 2000 ms door hold (that stamp is the last pusher's timer) and corrected, still without callers, the bone aim lock, the camera follow step, the look snap, the auto-target, hex-named placement markers with the engine's segment-sphere test, the deferred interaction list and the drop spawn run. Remaining: register level placement markers and object ids, door and wheel bodies, the movement word's reset path and 300 ms branch, combat footwork. TPlayerBehaviour about 50% → 56%, SG_PlayerControls 50% → 53%.

Done (change `equip-the-shield-flip-and-swap`; motilepart7 lane, reviewed): a shield's equip derives the holder's parry factor, leak and cost from its record and the Mobility and Endure techniques, kept beside the shield; a weapon that starts flipped is placed and built from its flipped side, the second weapon turning with the character's flip; a weapon switch past 60% of its clip trades the two weapons' blade slots and the primary; and the held refresh (weapon count, reach, aim height, flags, the parry gate) is recomputed every tick. The review removed the lane's standing shield guard, which would have turned every blow on a shield holder's limbs into a block and barred its weapon parry, tied the off-hand flip to the character's flip and limited the refresh count to weapon slots. Remaining: blocking with a shield body and the shield parry, the refresh's aim point, the moveset on a swap, a reader of the refresh. TMotilePart about 53% → 54%.

Done (change `review-the-playerctl6-pass`; playerctl6 lane, reviewed): the combat aim takes the cursor's point only from 50 cm out across the ground, each combat tick stores the auto-target (scored within 5 m with a level line of sight), the aim point locks its height onto that target's head, belly and pelvis (a stand-in for the original's own points) or rises onto a picked surface under the ground + 200 cm, and past half a crouch the footwork steps 0.7 of the reach, all live. The review corrected the crouched step's order (0.7 before the legs' extension), the step vector's unscaled side vector and flattening, and the goal's flattening and tapped stride. Remaining: the behaviour tick's mode word, the goal's navigation write, door and wheel bodies, placement markers. TPlayerBehaviour about 56% → 60%.

Done (change `read-the-description-phrases`; descphrase pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the description phrase tables and the eight armour-variant words are read from the user's own Exanima.exe by `exa_formats::desc_phrases` (no game text or addresses ship in the code), and the item card rewrites its description into the generated sentence through `exa_items::desc_phrase`, choosing the rule as the original builder does and putting the variant word in front of apparel titles; the spec now reads 15 "made" rows for the apparel sentence. Remaining: the engine's shared generator for the card's roll, the character view, the container popup and the card's socket controls. Item description phrases about 66% → 71%, TGUIItemContainer 79% → 80%.

Done (change `paint-factory-emblems-and-blade-stretch`; matproc pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): factory weapons paint their emblem layers (repeating their area's mask, material and wear in the emblem colour, colour pass only, through the emblem background picture for painted-pattern materials), placed by the assembler's pattern position in the original's double/float mix, and blade regions apply the last type-6 deform's texture-only stretch, read only by the blade programs. Remaining: the shared atlas and its low-quality half size, texture-only overlay parts and the mask-gated shade pass, the region's width crop, the alternate normal program, the gemstone passes. FLX_Materials_Procedural about 58% → 62%, TProcMaterial 45% → 52%, FLX_ItemFactory 87% → 89%.

Done (change `weigh-the-held-aim-point`; motilepart8 pass by a Claude agent): the equip and swap refresh weighs the first held item's aim point on its body's rest particles with the engine's exact barycentric routine and keeps the weights for the body dynamics' aim drive; the refresh's held reach is an intermediate the equip pass overwrites with the longest record reach; a weapon switch trades the main and second hands so the moveset is chosen again from the new main weapon; the unarmed hand share (0.5, or 0.25 with heavy claws) is a tested rule. Remaining: shields struck as bodies (block and parry), corpse collision words, hand-part mass and mobility words. TMotilePart about 54% → 55%.

Done (change `step-the-combat-footwork`; playerctl7 pass by a Claude agent): the original's combat step machine is live: the movement word feeds a dispatcher that starts, re-lays and queues steps under the original's gate (condition bits, 0.7 energy, no swing), a landing takes the queue through the body's queue rule, the movement goal makes its navigation write (line walk, 400 cm² test, grid snap) without the deep-crouch 0.7 its routine overwrites, the combat mode ladder decides when the right button walks, and the 200 ms word persistence moved out of exploration. Remaining: real feet carrying the steps (an interim capsule speed and landing rule stand in), the target-approach bend, the pad's analog word in combat, door and wheel bodies, placement markers. TPlayerBehaviour about 60% → 65%.

Done (change `snap-items-to-placement-markers`; playerctl8 pass by a Claude agent): placement markers are live: the level loader registers `[`-named model nodes (torch holders, the teleporter and the resurrection device, the last two locking), placed objects keep their template, and a lifted object snaps onto a matching marker's frame; the lift holds a placement only when its box fits and it is in reach, the release follows the original (last fit, stays on the marker, frozen for a locking marker, or put back), a fall ends a lift and a press thaws a frozen object. In combat the pad's stick is the footwork's analog word and a lone forward step bends at the target's circle. Remaining: door and wheel bodies, the GUI item layer, the deferred list's trigger. SG_PlayerControls about 53% → 56%, TPlayerBehaviour 65% → 67%.

Done (change `wire-swing-voice-and-tangle-break`; motilewire pass by a Claude agent): the swing tick's voice now plays through the mixer as the original's does (the first held item's voice restarts silent in the swing's region, follows the tracked speed, fades while not attacking and rustles the clothing), replacing the window-open whoosh, and the body tick's Break Tangle runs in the readiness stage on the hands' place in the character frame. The swing pushes, the bite link, the clinch clear, the armed parry and the struck-item sound stay tested only, each waiting on a prerequisite (a push contract on gripped item bodies, a grab across bodies, the armed parry in the game, the shield as an item entity). TMotileDynamics' live share rises by about 920 bytes (credited with the init-presets lane's rating).

Done (change `pull-doors-as-bodies`; doorbodies pass by a Claude agent): a placed door's hinged leaves register as hung bodies (exanima01: 109) and run the original's door set-up and step, so shut leaves stay shut and a left-button drag adds the original's door link (1 cm slack, strength 0.003, iteration limit, three passes — the plain slack law, not the horizontal drag the round-27 note feared) pulling 100 cm from the door's origin toward the cursor; a latched leaf refuses and the link is disabled past 350 cm from the hip. A door leaf's ~1 900 spheres are bucketed in a sphere grid with unchanged results, and exanima01 and arenahub hold 60 FPS with the doors live (`--static-doors` keeps the old static doors). Remaining: door sounds, the navigation passage report, sliding doors, levers (the wheel drag sets the lever's target) and plates as bodies. TRBDDoor about 70% → 80%, SG_PlayerControls 57% → 59%.

Done (change `review-the-plant-ground-grass-hooks-and-node-visibility`; gridenv4 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the plant ground's diagonal normal correction, the grass chunk's draw and shadow tests and the importer's visibility walks are ported from the assembly as tested rules (the review moved the grass sun-shadow bit onto the node's mask word); none is wired, Exarust having no block or node draw words and no fade value at load. TTerrainGrass 72% → 75%, RF_Import_RFC2 73% → 74%.

Done (navigation3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the navigation camera's editor scan, cut-away cascade and focus, the viewport attachment and the gamepad zoom follow the original; the review fixed four lane errors. RF_Navigation 74% → 77%.

Done (sceneenv3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the scene environment now ticks live (rain, lightning, fog, ambient and sun republished); the review fixed four lane errors against the assembly. A dark outdoor shot was a drawn night (outdoor levels draw their day per load; compare with EXA_SCENE_SEED), not a regression. TSceneEnvironment 65% → 82%, RFE_GridEnvs 61% → 68%.

Round 38 chardata3: the character data unit's record copies, entry walks, item import and armour/equip weights; review fixed the entry walk, added the import walk and migrate-first, and the short weapon section rank.

Round 38 tetra8: body run-time links, tick take, disabled state and the two-step transform; review reverted a shared rest-point array the original does not have.

Round 38 undead: dead bodies rise live through the AI manager's rise rule; the revive keeps race and stats and undoes go-limp (clips and controller back). Flag --undead-opponent shows it.

Round 39 chargen7: the armour pass's head extra height as a tested function; review removed an invented coverage gate.

Round 39 motilepart10: held item weld changes at collapse and death, and the collapse groan, live; review limited the collapse to the slack word and applied the changes on every condition change.

Round 39 risehands: a collapse now empties every hand except a shield's in the character's hands and the record's out set, as the original's came-loose hook does, and the rise reads the record's out set, so the unarmed set-up runs (confirmed on the GPU).

Round 40 charcreate6: the creator's starting kit, equipment panels and continue page as tested rules, the voice pick live; review reworked the kit entry layout and bound the apparel panel.

Round 40 charview2: the doll's hand scene and the figure's drag start as tested functions, the masked take-in flags and Insight's off-hand drop live; review restored two readings the lane had changed and split out the grip recompute.

Round 40 itemgui5: the icon atlas follows the interface scale live and the model-attach decisions are tested; review read the take-in gate as a wait and the 0x100 attach mark as shadow casting.

Round 42 motilepart11: held item grip laws and drop and torch words; review reverted a wrong limit-link law and removed round 39's invented held-weld writes at death and collapse (those routines write the legs' limit links).

Round 44 sweep-items: the golem core drop accepts both crystals live; review restored the palette precision and paint units and reverted a misread item-manager model and a duplicated apparel walk.

### Phase 5 — Visual and audio fidelity (started)

Done: the original's render pipeline (legacy and PBR programs, the
environment cube, parallax, supersampling without MSAA, sharpening),
voxel GI with the voxel lights of glowing items, emissive surfaces drawn
as the original's (additive glows, opacity fall-off), uncompressed
textures in the original's channel order (red embers, green foliage, RGB8
normal maps), fires and their particles, point lights that stop at walls
with cached cube shadows, and basic audio (music, ambience, impacts,
footsteps).
Done (change `shade-and-paint-blood`): wound blood as the original keeps
it, one byte per vertex in the colour stream's alpha, painted by the
original's wound painter (a ball behind the centre, a smear along the
blow's follow-through, speckled edges) on a character's skinned meshes and
the meshes under its head, each shared mesh copied for the struck
character on its first wound; dirt mode 4 shading against the speckle
`spatter01` for the human skin, painted apparel, factory items and a
bleeding race's skin (`races.rdb` flag bit 0), and `--debug-wound` to paint
test wounds.
Done (change `bleed-from-wounds`): blows bleed as the original's do: on a
race that bleeds, when the wound's size R (the lasting damage on the part
before the red bar's 0.75) exceeds 0.05, the wound waits on the victim
(seven at most) anchored to the striking blade and is painted seven body
ticks later, smeared along the blade's follow-through, with radius
30·R + 10 cm, on corpses too.
Done (change `stain-weapons-with-blood`): weapons stain as the original's
do: the weapon that deals a bleeding blow draws half the wound's R of
blood (up to 1, never washed off; fists none), and once it holds more
than 0.01 a second, multiplying pass draws the blood gradient `BldGr02D`
over its model's first mesh (every part of an assembled weapon), keyed on
the height up the blade so that the stain runs down √(80·L·blood) cm from
the tip, densest at the tip and speckled at its edge; `--weapon-blood`
shows one without a fight.
Done (change `spray-blood-from-wounds`): bleeding blows spray as the
original's do: one jet per bleeding blow, fixed to the struck part at the
contact point and pointing against the blade's swing, sending 36·R
droplets (60 at most) at 40 a second; each droplet flies off at up to
twice its jet's velocity, falls at a quarter of gravity for its 62 ticks
and never touches the floor, drawn as a camera-facing diamond of the
`BldPrt02` spatter that multiplies the frame (white leaves it unchanged);
`--debug-spray` fires test jets and `--pause-after-jet` stops a fight just
after the first. Next: blood across saves and respawns, creatures' own
blood (their striking body parts), the 8-bit clamp before the multiplies.
See `docs/RENDERING.md` and `docs/PERFORMANCE.md`.

Done (change `read-shipped-shadow-levels`): meshes cast and receive at the
shadow-bias level their file stores (bits 16–18 of the word before a mesh
body's streams, misread as skinning flags until now; 3 317 shipped meshes
carry one). Read as level 0, the dungeon's broken-floor slabs, which lie
1–2 cm under the painted dirt, shadowed the terrain over them in soft dark
bands around every broken-floor patch; at their shipped level 2 they no
longer do. The bit-19 variant (one mesh) is not reproduced.
`--debug-column` and `exa-tool corpus render-words` find such cases.

- Materials: one `ExaMaterial` whose parameters follow
  [OpenPBR](https://github.com/AcademySoftwareFoundation/OpenPBR) names
  (mapping in `research/openpbr.md`), with two shading modes: **Original**
  (default, reproduces the captured GLSL lighting model) and **Enhanced**
  (an OpenPBR subset in WGSL: base, specular, coat, fuzz, approximate
  subsurface).
- Lighting: cube-map shadows for many point lights (torches, candles),
  and the original's voxel light-propagation GI rebuilt as a compute pass
  (done: one block per level; see `docs/RENDERING.md`).
  Ambient occlusion, bloom, tone mapping, colour grading against reference
  screenshots.
- Effects: particles (fire, sparks), blood sprays and spatter decals,
  bloody weapons, thaumaturgy effects (force waves, barriers, tendrils).
- Character surface: GPU generation of procedural skin, injury and decal
  textures (`chardecomp_*`, `chargen_*`).
- Cloth on capes, tabards and padded armour (simplified, physics-driven).
- `exa_audio`: positional playback of `Sound.rpk`, reverb environments
  per area, sound sets per material and event (impact, footstep, voice), and
  music. Its modules (`bank` with the one sound-set picker every event
  uses, `music`, `hits`, `footsteps`) take each lane's new sounds as their
  own module; `environment`, `ambience`, `reverb` and `playback` carry the
  original's audio environment, with set probing (01 to 16, no repeat).

Done (change `fit-the-smallest-sphere`): `exa_render::bound_sphere` holds the original's smallest-enclosing-sphere solver (RFT_BoundSphere, move-to-front over 1–4 support points in f64) and its f32/f64 helpers, bit-exact against the assembly and a separate port on 600 point sets, but nothing calls it yet. Its only caller in the original is TShadowMapDirect's fitted sun map (at least 2 clipped corners, sphere ×100 to cm, radius in 200 cm steps with +0.3/−0.1 hysteresis, centre snapped to texels, ±8000 cm depth); that map and the RF_Intersection convex clipping are a separate lane. RFT_BoundSphere 80%.

Done (change `match-the-noise-and-pixel-formats`, power effects part): the force and spirit powers' animated noise field is modelled as `exa_render::image_noise::NoiseField` (256×256, 4 octaves, amplitude 1, keep/add 0.5, period 0.8 s for the force) but not drawn; the three-plane weighted sum p2·w2 + (p1·w1 + p0·w0) of RF_WorkerThreads is inside it.

Done (change `voxelise-cell-floors`): the original's mesh voxeliser is in `exa_physics::voxel`, checked against the assembly: the floor heightfield (ray plane distances in the original's lane order, cell edges snapped in whole 20 cm steps, the downsample's odd-row drift), the surface generator (0.2·p step and s·√2/2 near radius in double precision, near-cell clearing, greedy 40/4/0.4 cover) and the rigid-body voxel map's layout. The GI volume now merges each grid cell's floor meshes and runs the heightfield once per cell, in the cell's space, at level load; a flat 6 m cell still gives 900 points 10 cm under its floor. Not in: the node's kd-tree sphere query (closest-point stand-in), the generator's callers for floor-set props and character bodies, floor collision triangles.

Done (change `mix-the-audio-environment`): exa_audio carries the original's audio environment: 512 virtual voices ranked by audibility onto 64 source slots with the original's scan, sort, bind, refresh and eviction; every source heard through the OpenAL model the original sets up (linear clamped, 4–30 m, rolloff 1); the listener 3 m along its up vector from the player + 50 cm; a name-keyed, reference-counted buffer cache with sound sets and the no-repeat picker; level music cut at level change, the main-menu crossfade, overlays with mirrored interruptions and the Music setting; area ambience beds and scheduled one-shots; the stored EAX reverb block and per-level decay offset, computed but not applied (rodio has no EFX). Still open: the in-game listener orientation writer, weather/time-of-day/story ambience callers, the menu theme alternatives, an options screen for Music, the remaining one-off sounds on the mixer. A `play exanima01` run on the RTX 3090 loaded the level (5900 props, 39 characters, loading screen 22.1 s) and exited 0 (`target/verify/gpu-launch-87.{log,png}`).

Done (change `reproduce-the-small-effect-nodes`): the small effect nodes run on the original's game clock in whole milliseconds with signed spans: the domain wheel (six domains Mind, Force, Body, Energy, Light, Displacement clockwise from the top, label fade, (3−2x)x² open ease, confirm with a 256 ms hold), the displacement shell (400/300 ms, fade (1−shown²)², fade plane along the body's up axis), the teleporter scan, the teleport bubble, the flame row (double-precision fades and walks, lag 20 cm above its ends) and the portal dissolve; a loaded portal marker (the `uwportal01` emitter) gets its blue 0.75, 500 cm light. Not in yet: the callers of the shell, scan, bubble, wheel and flame row, every draw path, the portal's particle simulation. Frame time in `play exanima01` did not regress: with `--perf-log 5` over about 40 s after loading, the merged tree averaged 23.1 and 25.0 ms a frame against 28.9 and 30.3 ms for the tree before the scene environment (commit 3b27a9dc), both main-thread bound (fixed ticks and PostUpdate) on a loaded host; the 59.4 and 27.9 FPS readouts of `gpu-launch-87`/`-88` are single readings 90 frames after loading, which gave 43.5 and 22.6 FPS for the same binary in `gpu-launch-89`/`-90` (logs in `target/verify/perf/{effects-merge,pre-scene-env}-{a,b}.log`).

Done (change `smooth-the-meshes`): run-time mesh smoothing as the original's MeshSmooth does it. A decoded mesh becomes the editable form (one element index per corner and channel) and converts back by smoothing groups, material runs, texture seams and mirrored winding, then the vertex-cache order with the original's fast ln/exp bit for bit. The Loop subdivision follows the original's crease, pairing, fold and weight rules and output order, splits a shared texture coordinate that lands more than 1e-6 (squared) away, and refits the bounds. `exarust model <pack> <entry> --msm` stands in for the original's `msm` command; `exa_assets::polyhedra` builds the icosahedron, its cap and subdivisions for the effect shells. Not in: the hair the creation scene smooths, the scene-wide and item-preview passes, the mind effects' UV sphere and the effects that draw the shells.

Done (change `reproduce-the-particle-emitter`): fires run the original's particle emitter (`exa_world::particles`): template scaling, per-tick values in double precision, the Verlet ring, the fire's ageing and distance cap, the two-thirds accumulator, emission from one source or a ring, the light walk and density, the three quad builders and the colour keys baked as the load-time scaling rounds them (half to even), drawn with the engine's LCG and the run-time library's MT19937. `exa_world::fire` builds the six templates, three beds and four size tiers (large fires one-shot, removed when done); torch particles spawn at rest, flames face the camera in centimetres, the held torch's fire rides the hand, hidden flames build no quads and a slow frame catches up four ticks at most. Electric arcs (`exa_render::electricity`, `exa_world::arc`) have their presets, 84-byte record, charge step and `_ElecMat01` shader, but nothing spawns them yet. Integration fix: a flame hidden from its first frame kept a mesh without UVs and failed the flame shader's validation in `play exanima01`; particle meshes now start with their attributes. Not in: the tendril emitter's tick, arc callers, the fire's emissive scale on its parent mesh, the looping fire and arc sounds.

Done (change `fit-the-sun-shadow-map`): in a level with a terrain the sun has the original's one fitted orthographic map (4096², the top shadow detail): each frame every active camera's frustum, out to the original camera's 125 m far plane, is clipped by the planes its view direction picks at the level's box and the environment record's floor and ceiling (`exa_physics::intersect::ConvexVolume`), fitted with the smallest enclosing sphere, quantised in 200 cm steps with the original's hysteresis, snapped to the texel grid and given ±8000 cm of depth (`exa_render::sun_fit`, `exa_world::sun_shadow`); it replaces Bevy's cascades there. Levels without a terrain keep two cascades over 60 m, standing in for the original's whole-scene map, as does every level with `--bevy-sun-cascades`. Not in: the whole-scene mode, the original's softness and 8-tap filter, the caster polygon offset. The `arenao3` check under Phase 2 shows the fitted map.

Done (change `match-the-mip-and-mesh-units`): generated mips follow the original's resampling unit: the uniform bank (2^L taps of 2^−L) for data words and the Kaiser bank for colour, each axis wrapped or clamped by the image's own flags, normal maps (three- and four-channel images flagged `0x4000000`) renormalised with the engine's normaliser, the Kaiser weights summed in double precision, chains started at their requested first level with the final 1×1 kept and uploaded sides floored at one texel, and the resample to an arbitrary size. Mesh normals weight unit face normals by the original's corner-angle approximation (faces with the fallback flag shape a smoothing group only alone); tangents, bounds, barycentric coordinates, the index type and the material regroup are computed as the mesh unit does, with the sub-image blits, sharpen kernel and icon canvas placement. All 8839 shipped images convert (151 generate a chain: 33 with the uniform bank, 58 clamped on an axis, 30 renormalised) and all 16 640 shipped meshes get finite unit normals. Not in: filtering in the native channel count, the 12-thread float-image driver and the icon manager, terrain and cloth per-face normals, byte index buffers.

Done (change `expand-the-original-shader-templates`): `exa_formats::flb` decodes the `.flb` shader libraries (pack table, per-entry rotation cipher, NUL padding); `exa_render::shader_template` expands a template as the original's builder does (word-group conditions, the `&` chain, else rules, unrolled loops, integer expressions, the builder's number printing, includes, the generated vertex/fragment interface); `shader_program` builds the material and shader words, the program choice, the shadow and displacement sample splits and the 29 warm-up keys, and `shader_uniforms` the Global, Material and light blocks. `exa-tool shader` prints a material's original GLSL from `shading.flb` (all 54 entries decode byte for byte against an independent decode and expand with no leftover markers). The WESL surface program now follows the decoded templates where it differed (unlit emissive alpha, two-sided normal flip, PBR f0 without a diffuse map, skin alpha, N·H clamp, specular GI without a reflection layer inside the GI volume, legacy diffuse ×1.5, specular ×3, parallax texel rounding). Not in: the procedural texture programs of `textures.flb`, a runtime program cache (Exarust keeps its WESL), hair and translucent programs, wetness, dirt modes 1–3. The branch's WESL had never been compiled: at integration every material pipeline failed (`} else` after an `@if` block, exa.wesl:248), fixed with an `@else` block (29dcfaff). On the RTX 3090 `play exanima01` then compiles every pipeline and its start room matches `gpu-launch-95` (mean colour (30.8, 19.4, 12.3) against (28.6, 18.4, 11.9); no black, missing or magenta surfaces; `target/verify/gpu-launch-96.{log,png}`), and `play arenao3` matches `gpu-launch-93` to a mean difference of 1.5 levels (`target/verify/gpu-arenao3-96.{log,png}`; the grass lies outside the arena view).

Done (change `weld-and-sphere-the-meshes`; meshtools2 lane): the mesh tools' weld, influence merge, winding flip and generic incidence, plus the mind effects' UV sphere, work as the original's do. The weld sorts each owning stream by its first component with the original's quicksort, merges later elements within 0.01 cm (positions) or √1e-9 (texture coordinates) into the earliest live one, splicing the incidence rings in the original's order, and renumbers survivors in old order; shared streams follow. The character pass folds a welded-away position's influences into its survivor (larger weight kept) and normalises them. The sphere's 4-segment strips are (0,1,2,3), (4,7,2,5), (6,7,8,9), (10,11,8,12); its fast sine rounds the quadrant to nearest, so sin 0.5 has bits 0x3EF57746. The review restored the 65,535-face subdivision limit (16 383 faces divide) and the edge-point search's stop, and made the texture fold test ordered, so all 24 CharExt meshes smooth to finite values. Not in: a loader stage that calls the weld (skinning, the item factory and grid environments have none), drawing the mind effects, the voxeliser wrappers and the live-scene `msm` walk.

Done (change `reproduce-the-force-effect-nodes`; fxnodes lane): the force and environment effect nodes are in as plain rules under `exa_game::effects`, checked against the instruction listing: the rain (300 cm box 450 cm ahead of the eye, the tilt-dependent tail streak, the sign-bit wrap, the colour and draw count), the portal spray, the flame node's light, the force domes (ripples, Barrier, Bubble, keep-outs, contact, double-sided meshes), the Force Wave's ring and thrown step, the vanish, the AI's sight of a blast, a wave and a barricade, the seven GI emissives' values, and the force geometries' pass rows, fades and uniforms. They reuse thaum's ripple step, wave rules and Barrier pose helpers. The unwired `force_draw` path, which drew at centimetre scale with invented tints, is removed. Not in: anything that starts these effects (the powers, the weather), drawing them (the effectbarrier, effectbubble and effectfbrcd programs, the rain's frame-buffer passes), GI injection of the emissives, their sounds, and registering the force entities with the AI's sight.

Done (change `join-the-mesh-streams`; meshunits2 lane): the mesh unit's second pass: `Mesh::merge` joins parts as the original does (flags default 1, element-0 texture coordinates, GI untouched); the render word's 0x800 bit is the shadow depth pass's cull selector (`Mesh::shadow_cull_front`); stored RGBA8 vertex colours are parsed but not uploaded, since only dirt modes 1–3 read them (`exa-tool corpus colours` counts 2708 coloured meshes, 859 of them in a dirt mode, none in mode 4); a faceless mesh warns once. RF_Mesh about 40%, TMesh about 71%. Not done: calling merge from a loader, the shadow-pass cull and dirt modes 1–3.

**M5 "looks and sounds like Exanima":** reference shots taken from matching
camera positions in the original and in Exarust are hard to tell apart.

Done (change `correct-the-light-and-effect-units`; lightfx lane): the light and effect units were checked against the executable: the voxel light node (drives the game), camera node data, the material defaults and manager model (the shared `_Default` fallback drives the loader), the shadow-map plumbing, the 2049-quad spline strip, the 128-entry particle UV table, the voxeliser constants, the spray node names and the shock charge object are tested data. Next: camera planes, shadow sizes, the loader through the material manager and a splines caller.

Done (change `compose-skin-and-merge-parts`; skinning2 lane, reviewed): the character skin composite (`chargen_d`/`_s`/`_n` and the beard's three overlays drawn only over the map's quarter-by-quarter face corner, at most five normal layers stopping at the first non-positive amount, the callback's colour→normal→surface order) is a tested CPU function. A garment is painted from one stem, the first `&` material of a depth-first walk, and every `&` part takes it (wired; the two shipped two-stem garments now show their first stem on both parts). The `_SKIN` merge (material runs back to back, index-range groups, cull masks), the sixteen-frame group stack and the apparel matching's parent context are tested but not wired. Not in: feeding the composite to the skin material, the merged mesh as the draw path, layer strength and jitter, the item preview job. RF_Skinning about 56% → 74%, FLX_Character_Textures 50% → 70%.

Done (change `plan-the-dressed-character`; chargen3 lane, reviewed): the dressed character's append list follows the generator: each garment, in layer-level order, appends its chosen nodes in slot order 1–18 gated by its visible mask and carrying the slot's cull bits, then the body's visible slots in slot order, then the overlays (a race without look bit 0x4 merges the body ungated); each body slot takes the impact material of the garment with the largest uncovered word. Also in as tested rules: the feral underwear overlay (damage and dirt (2−f)·f·255, round-to-even), the placed, player and corpse spawn plans, creator roles, the attach pass (main-hand weapon, then shield, else torch, else second weapon; one flipped grip per character) and the background rebuild's apply step. None is wired yet: the game still draws a mesh per node. Not yet: drawing the merged mesh, the skin compositor, hair cloth, off-hand items in play, the armour pass's special kinds, the original's 20-word layer-level scan. SG_CharacterGen about 50% → 62%.

Done (change `review-the-mesh-unit-third-pass`; meshunits3 lane, reviewed): mesh normals sum each smoothing key's corners in the original's order (reverse incidence order, split into key runs by its unstable in-place swap partition), with face normals cached per face so load time does not grow; this is wired for every model. The mesh unit's face record and its queries (closest point, strict and inclusive ray, sphere push-out, reach, swept sphere, with the original's NaN-passing compares) and the editable-form assembly with its material regroup and deep copy (the rigid `dst⁻¹·src` in the original's float order, the normal stream skipped, gaps filled with 0/255, flags 1 and edges 7) are reproduced and tested. Not wired: the assembly in the item, grid and character builders, the queries (collision stays rapier), the cloth flat normals, the swept tests' push-out callbacks. RF_Mesh about 40% → 75%, TMesh 71% → 82%.

Done (change `lay-out-gi-blocks-and-inject-the-sun`; vxgi2 lane, reviewed): the voxel GI lays its blocks out once per level over the grid's built cells (the original's row scan, eight-cell blocks slid inside the grid, one block at cells (3, 3) without a grid); the camera draws its own block by the floored 48 m offset, or the block nearest the focus 20 m ahead, and the last three built occupancies are reused. The injection samples at the slice boundary with height over width, shadows point lights through the cached cube pool, and injects the sun first in every drawn group at 3 × 0.3 of its colour, shadowed by Bevy's fitted sun map (the review fixed lights placed 4× too far along the depth axis and an indoor sun that was always dark). Tested but unwired: presets, the shape list and draw box, the margin copy, thin and centred splats, the dynamic update region, sub-upload bytes. Not in: nine resident blocks, the margin blit pass, shape injection programs, the separate GI sun map and second direct light, the cube compare's bias factor. TVXGIManager about 60% → 70%.

Done (change `wire-the-merged-character-mesh`; wire-merged-mesh lane, reviewed): each dressed character is drawn as one merged skinned mesh built through `exa_assets::merge` from the generator's dressed-mesh plan, with a sub-mesh per material run and the covered body slots hidden; cloth, hair and held items stay per node. The skin run takes the CPU skin composite (`chargen_d/_s/_n`, the beard, five normal layers), generated off the main thread and cached by look. The review fixed the frame convention (the lane's transposed rows left the arms raised in the bind pose and tore the shoulders), restored the runs' tangents and removed the seed UV offset, which belongs to the decomposition programs only; load and frame time are unchanged or slightly better. Next: the per-triangle cull bits, hair as cloth, the decomposition and apparel layers in the composite, the feral darkening. SG_CharacterGen about 64% → 70%, RF_Skinning 74% → 82%, FLX_Character_Textures 70% → 77%.

Done (change `model-the-shader-program-cache`; shaders2 lane, reviewed): the GPU program unit's CPU rules are tested models in exa_render: the program cache (buckets keyed on the material and shader word pair, entries on the object and environment pair, failed builds kept, the warm-up pass shown by `exa-tool shader --warmup`), the per-program bindings and cached uniform locations with the SdwSmp window counted in vec3 taps, the six-slot light fill (the view's cap, unshadowed-first ordering that keeps slots, the per-kind position, a separate depth-scale term, the stale flag and reset), the textures.flb programs with the MPrj matrix taken from the executable, and the material writer's dirt and displacement branches. Nothing is wired into the WESL renderer (Bevy owns the pipelines and lights). Still to do: the GL halves, the geometry stage, the blood tint from the diffuse texture's record. FLX_GPUPrograms about 55% → 65%.

Done (change `wire-item-icons`; wire-item-icons lane, reviewed): the inventory pack and the loot panels draw item icons: each reserved icon is rendered by a private off-screen camera (two a frame), read back asynchronously into the manager's atlas, with a CPU paint for headless runs or lost readbacks. The review replaced the lane's guessed framing with the original's 40° icon field and the engine's scene fit (small items draw small), projected the extents in centimetres, and isolated the icon camera, sun and meshes from the world's systems (an `Offscreen` marker and a dedicated layer below the light layers); before that the icon camera rendered the level and the icon meshes showed at the world origin. Remaining: subtype subdivision, streamed icons, re-rendering on a GUI-scale change, releasing icons when a panel closes. SG_ItemGUI about 61% → 65%, TGUIItemContainer 77% → 78%.

Done (change `model-the-draw-state-and-uniform-values`; shaders3 lane, reviewed): the GPU program unit's per-draw plan (`exa_render::draw_state`) models light gather and clear, the object word, the fade and per-draw emissive rewrites, super-sampling sub-frames, animated clocks and motion-blur shutter times, the single-pass variant and the effect parent matrix, and the six built-in texture vertex programs are byte-exact. The review kept light colour and filter radii in double, made the fog position's w the scene height limit that caps y, made the refraction background follow the scene switch with a double 0.85 compare, read the factory's emissive and specular choices from the masked word, halved the last pass index for half passes and added the motion-blur shutter time. Only the height-map switch-off is live. Remaining: the GL-side program, buffer, bind and draw calls, the shadow-sample tap table and the refraction pass. FLX_GPUPrograms about 65% → 74%, TShaderBuilder 88% → 93%.

Done (change `sample-gi-at-slice-centres`; vxgi3 lane, reviewed): the voxel GI reads each voxel's own slice centre as the shipped injection shaders do, point lights carry the original's three-vec4 light block, the cube compare is d·((bias + 0.94)/far)·5120 in double with the bias from the shadow detail plus the light's quality offset (capped at 6, × 0.005), surfaces sample half a voxel up and terrain and grass at the voxel's bottom, and a node's centred-splat flag is honoured. The review added a naga-vs-CPU uniform layout test, took the per-object offset in double and labelled the unranged-light lookup a guess. Remaining: the nine resident light blocks, the margin pass, the shape fragment bodies and their wiring, the sample exception, the thin-mesh byte and the top-slice flag. TVXGIManager about 70% → 76%.

Done (change `live-item-card`; itemgui3 lane, reviewed): the item description card is live: a right click on a pack or loot entry builds it from the catalogue's card facts, with names through the original's string resolver and the shown page's own tint, rank and socket bytes, its own icon key, and a fade when the pointer leaves; closing a panel releases its icons and freed slots are reused; equipment changes re-sum encumbrance into the physical and fighter stats. The review fixed the string resolution (the inherit id had become 0 and an invented "Item N" title showed), the card's icon key, a hit test that answered true anywhere in a panel, a doubled value column, and an encumbrance regression that dropped every spawned body's armour encumbrance on its first frame. Remaining: extract the description phrase tables from the executable for the sentence builders, the character view, the container popup and the card's socket controls. TGUIItemContainer about 78% → 79%.

Done (change `review-the-scene-environment-day-track`; sceneenv2 lane, reviewed): the engine's built-in six-record day track (keys 0.38 to 0.70, checked value by value against the image) drives the scene environment, kept in sRGB as the shipped game keeps it (the start-up linearise step depends on a flag nothing sets, so the frame linearises the horizon instead); on the outdoor arenas the sun light takes the record's sun colour as stored and the fog the record's fog, replacing the haze stand-in; the frame publishes the rain wind, the tint and the sun-height value; the grid gains its set tables by index, node-to-sector lists and the character's cell bytes. The review fixed the track being linearised twice (sky colours through the sRGB curve twice and a near-black sun), restored the lightning flicker's NaN branch and removed a duplicate tint strength. Remaining: a frame system for the weather tick and time of day, the sky cube's masked tint. TSceneEnvironment about 55% → 65%, RFE_GridEnvs 57% → 61%.

Done (change `rank-gi-blocks-and-mark-thin-meshes`; vxgi4 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the GI manager's block residency ranks blocks by box distance with the original's stable, NaN-safe sort and builds the next block in the background while the camera's block is in place, every built occupancy is kept (the cache is now keyed by layout index, fixing rebuilds when the layout skipped an empty block), thin meshes take the quarter-weight splat from their first surface's material, and outdoor levels' top-slice flag lets the lid slice carry light. Remaining: nine GPU light volumes with per-object block selection, the margin blits, the shapes' fragment bodies, the unranged cube far plane. TVXGIManager about 76% → 79%.

Done (change `review-item-card-bytes-and-redress-apply`; itemgui4 lane, DeepSeek, stalled after three commits, reviewed): the card's weapon sentence edits names as bytes (a short bracketed name empties as the original's length cut does, and multi-byte names cannot panic), the apparel material splice follows the original's in-place loops at the string's end, and the doll apply keeps the plan's stowed ids, refreshes the body when both are empty and clears the weapon part once. The review undid the lane's reversal of the card's sentence choice (the weapon page still wins over the apparel body), removed its invented merged model marks and an unused sprite-sheet loader (the decode and crop stay tested). Remaining: the apply's cancel path, the per-kind model marks, drawing the sheets.

Done (change `finish-the-shader-builder-entries`; shaders4 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the shader builder's last entries are in: the separate shadow-declaration callback, the exact 8×3-float shadow-sample table (also in the surface shader at full precision), the material loader's texture-upload wait, the signed shift and divide wrap in the shadow-tap count, and the cache free that keeps the stale flag. The review corrected the Global block's time lanes: the view position's w and the time delta's x are the super-sampling sub-frame offset times the frame scale, its y is minus the scale over the factor, with no exposure in any of them (the lane had swapped one wrong reading for another). Remaining: the sub-frame jitter matrices, the GL glue, wiring the Global block and light slots into Bevy's pipeline. FLX_GPUPrograms about 74% → 75%, TShaderBuilder 93% → 94%.

Done (change `run-levers-and-plates`; levers lane, Claude): exanima01's 40 lever arms and 12 push surfaces are live bodies with the original's set-up (levers mobility 1, plates 0.3/0.03, no gravity, 0.001 of velocity kept a second) and their steps after the solver; a turned lever drives its arm 0.02 rad a tick and signals its targets, a timed return counts down asleep or not; plates are pushed by the double-click, a class 5 signal or a weight on their rails; MechanismSignal is routed to the door bodies or push surface under the target. A root slider link now keeps its own travel as slack (it had the child's 289 m). The 37 sliding leaves stay static unless `--sliding-doors` (they creep up to 2 cm in 2 s). GPU run on exanima01: 59-60 FPS, the same 46-52 bodies awake as before. Remaining: sliding-door lift and rest, relays, fire/light jobs, kind 6 light fade, mechanism sounds.

Done (change `reproduce-the-scene-removal-frame-steps`; scenenodes3 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): the scene frame drains its 255-usable removal ring at frame start and processes hide, delete and free entries at frame end as the original (owned subtrees wait, owners handed back, a finished entry replaced by the last), light activation runs for every sector, and sector bounds turn each room's boxes by its rotation with the assembly's sum order. Model only: the running game does not draw through the Scene yet. RF_SceneNodes about 80% → 82%.

Done (change `stream-terrain-textures-and-placed-objects`; streaming5 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): terrain textures stream through the stream-first path and a tile spawns only once both maps arrived; a level loaded in one call finishes waiting records at once; the level load builds its placed objects through the content stream with the original's level wait and missing-objects message; the texture manager's named image and file inserts and the always-off fast-load path are in. GPU: arenao3 terrain draws at 60 FPS (2.0 s load), exanima01 loads in 19.4 s as before. RF_Textures 77% → 81%, FLX_Content_Stream 60% → 64%.

Done (change `blend-the-link-mobilities-and-break-the-grabs`; tetra7 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): props blend their link mobilities as the original's group masks do (characters reset theirs afterwards, settling an earlier evidence conflict), body links recompute their shares from the current mobilities, the breakable grab follows the assembly (four errors of the old port fixed) and releases every grab to a struck part; the node clock and body draw fractions are tested functions. GPU runs on exanima01 and arenao3 hold 58-60 FPS. TTetraRBD 64% → 67%.

Done (change `submit-the-force-effect-passes`; thaumgpu lane, Claude): the force shell aura and the blast and torrent ribbons now reach the GPU through their own passes, scripted presses take the digit row, and bar casts and the aura's copies are logged. A GPU run on the arena cast the Shell (22 skinned copies, a faint violet rim on the duellist) and a blast (cast result 1; the ribbon not caught on screen). TForceShellEffect 75% → 82%, TForceBlastGeometry and TForceTorrentGeometry 62% → 70%.

Done (change `clamp-gi-terrain-at-the-lid`; vxgi5 lane, DeepSeek, stalled after two commits, reviewed): the GI terrain fill follows the original (heights truncate, a column below the block is skipped, one at or above the top slice clamps to it with no surface byte, so the lid's 5 becomes 20), the corner offsets are formed in double, and the three GI programs' binding table and the low-quality base ambient are in. The review reverted the lane's 29-column sector floor scan and reordered shaping sum (both misread loop bounds or sum order) and fixed its shadow units and emission samplers. TVXGIManager about 79% → 80%.

Done (change `drop-blood-jets-at-teardown`; blood lane, Claude): the blood code already in Exarust was checked against the assembly: the jet frame now uses the approximate normalise and the part-relative frame the turn's transpose in the original's sum order, the spray reset (dropping only the jets) is back and runs at a level teardown, and the weapon blood clamp lives in one place. GPU duel on arena1: a bleeding blow starts a jet of 11 droplets and stains the blade, at 59 FPS. TBloodSprays and RFE_Effects_Blood were unrated (0%) although drawn: now 85% and 78%; TWeaponBlood 53% → 60%.

Done (charforms lane, Claude): the character-creation and settings screens' ports were checked against the assembly and fixed (the key-capture screen's Escape, mouse and focus, the warm-up screen's row answer and fade, both screens' text alpha, the 64-bit skin and hair conversions, the edit page caption); rebinding now goes through the key-capture screen with the saved table's scan codes, Apply opens the warm-up screen after a shading change, and the creation form's preview, close and change callback are tested functions. None of these screens is reachable yet: the menu's settings and character screens are not wired. Nine components that were 0-14% are now 35-65%.

Done (guipalette lane, Claude): the game's windows paint with the line skin's shown palette as the original's fade setter scales it (form group 170, others 125 when shown): the inventory, journal, loot and trade windows at fade 1, the match board and Manage Company with their 256-tick fade-in, the item card with its squared fade; buttons use the line skin's atlas frame and cream inks through one shared helper (also used by the Settings form). Remaining: fade-outs, the board tabs' record words, the non-active task's 0.75/0.5 scales; the inventory of a naked arena player shows only its encumbrance label and Primary button (to check against the original).

Done (change `keep-the-foot-and-hand-words`; motilepart9 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): death's loop walks the lower legs, not the held items, so the earlier "dead holder stiffens the soft weld" rule was invented and is removed (with its per-tick system); the foot's thin-plate bit is a stored word the leg routine sets and collapse and death clear, the hands' contact words follow the original, a gobbler's femurs get 10 cm of slack on collapse and a held shield gets its class bits; the routine earlier read as go-limp is the revive of a corpse, now a tested function. GPU duel on arena1 and exanima01: 59-60 FPS, fighters stand and strike. TMotilePart 55% → 56%.

Done (playerctl9 lane, Claude): placed containers open from the double click in their popup, as the original's dispatcher orders it; the level's container children that name random tables are filled at world load (exanima01: 292 in 71 containers), level-local item records resolve their models for icons, and GPU icon renders run one at a time (overlapping renders on the shared icon layer had drawn magnified pieces of other items). A GPU run opens exanima01's chest of drawers with its boots, shirt, belt and trousers. Remaining: icons untextured, walk-to-interact, the door sound. SG_PlayerControls 61% → 64%, TPopUpContainer 0% → 80%.

Done (popups lane, Claude): the pop-up and loot ports were checked against the assembly: the end-session dispatch no longer swaps practice and arena, the item card's leave starts a fresh fade and a right press over it no longer reaches the panel (live), the container popup's close saves its contents, the parchment's hit factor is divided in double precision, the loot filter is the golem core-crystal test, the inventory refuses a drag out of the pack during a weapon-set swap, and role values are written to the entry the original's commit picks. Not live yet: world containers, the parchment opener, the arsenal window, the inventory doll, golem activation.

Done (change `save-unit-seventh-pass`; savegames7 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the arena load's item classification follows the original's routine exactly (body section first, armour type 3-6 to the armour shelf, then weapon and shield sections by presence), replacing an invented classifier; the review removed the lane's second copy of the player-record reset, which the original calls from the save's load before the import, never from the transition flow. XA_SaveGames 66% → 67%.

Done (change `open-the-settings-form`; settingsform lane, Claude): the Settings form opens from the menu and draws as the original (records re-read from the exe, the real layout and rescale, the skin's shown palette and the line skin's control draws), with its pages, controls, key capture and Apply/Cancel working; the original leaves the menu drawn under the form, as Exarust now does. A GPU run at 2400x1620 shows both pages. Remaining: other windows (inventory, journal, hub) still paint with the unscaled palette and look faint. RF_HW_Config 78% → 85%, TReadKeyScreen 80%, TCacheShaderScreen 70%.

Done (change `read-the-hand-flags-as-the-equip-pass`; chargen6 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): the equip pass's hand flags were read the wrong way round (a flip flag taken as a lit light, the half-sword flag as a character-wide flip) and are now read as the original does; both spawn paths run the same equip and attach, so a level's placed characters hold their shields and second weapons. SG_CharacterGen 77% → 78%.

Done (itemgui5 lane, Claude): apparel icons are painted from the item's appearance as the original's streamed icon does, GPU icons wait for their textures and retry an empty first readback (which had left the first icon of a session untextured), and the inventory, loot window and journal are built at unit 4 and rescaled to the live GUI unit as the original shows its forms, so the inventory now draws its frame, view panel and pack. A GPU run shows exanima01's chest with painted boots and belt and a textured dresser icon. Remaining: the character view's figure, the encumbrance bar, world apparel painting.

Done (motiledyn9 lane, Claude): blows now play the original's damage sounds: a wounding blow plays the victim's wound set by race (flesh, skeleton, golem), and a wound with yellow left plays the victim's own pain voice (short groan, heavy groan or grunt, with their waits), from a voice built per fighter as the original's init does; the random grunt every blow played is gone; the balance footwork's steps now rustle when they start and play the race's footstep when they land. The muscles-off sampler's gait overlay, height target and queued-clip switch and the target capture are ported from the assembly as tested functions (the sampler is not scheduled). TMotileDynamics about 78% → 79% reproduced, 66% → 67% live.

Done (thaumtree7 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): the power tree's text panels are the skin's shaded box in its text ink and its label mode draws boxes before names as the original's two loops do; the power bar marks running powers' slots by their class's mark method (persistent powers fade over 200 ms when they end; bubble, shell, kinesis, strikes and others stay lit), loaded from the caster's running powers and updated when a power ends. TThaumTree 72% → 78%.

Done (change `draw-the-inventory-character-view`; invdoll lane, Claude): the inventory's character view now shows the player's own dressed figure, turning toward the pointer, with the view's camera and light; the encumbrance record is the item GUI's rating-dots control (encumbrance x 10 as dots), and dropping apparel, a weapon or a shield on the figure equips it. The original's inventory has no preview bars (they belong to character creation and the arena). A GPU run shows the figure at 59-60 FPS.

Done (motiledyn10 lane, Claude): a body off its feet now probes its ground under the pelvis each tick as the original's body tick does, and the feet run the hover mode (wraiths) and flat-floor mode (wired, not yet set) with the original's probes and skipped steps. TMotileDynamics about 79% reproduced, 67% → 68% live.

Done (motilepart10 lane, Claude): held shields and weapons are struck as bodies, so a blade meeting a shield is blocked as in the original (classified from the swing's bits and the contact's side, then the damage pass spends the shield's stamina, leaks into yellow and dazes) and weapon meeting weapon resolves as a clash; `--shield` and `--opponent-shield` hang a loading shield on the player or the duellists. A GPU run blocks the duellist's blows on the player's shield. TMotilePart 56% → 57%.

Done (playerbeh lane, Claude): the player behaviour's tick now rebuilds its contact list, runs the late-swing engaged check (feeding the practice lesson from the strongest engaged enemy's fighting estimate) and the practice tick as the original does each tick; a wind-up port that had misread its inputs was removed. TPlayerBehaviour 67% → 69%.

Done (playerctl10 lane, Claude): the world-object click runs as the original's controls do (devices used first, then a card or the narrated description), a chest picked as level geometry is found by the original's bounding search and opens, a used consumable leaves the scene with its sound, opening doors plays their lock sound, and level-local records get card facts; walk-to-interact was not built because the original has none (an out-of-reach double click does nothing). SG_PlayerControls 64% → 67%.

Done (motilepart11 lane, Claude): held weapons keep their weapon bit as in the original, so clashes and weapon parries now happen live (they had been misread as blocks); a torch blocks with the bare arm; a block rings the shield's or torch's own material sound. TMotilePart 57% → 58%.

Done (itemfactory3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): factory weapons paint each part at its atlas region's size with the original's width crop and repaint on a condition change; the procedural material's programs, pass uniforms, job ring and surface cache are modelled; the review fixed the UVScale fold, the crop, a repaint misplacement bug and the sampler names.

Done (motiledyn11 lane, Claude): a struck player's camera now jolts and the pad rumbles as the original's damage routine does, the striking weapon rings once per swing on its first clash, and getting up gives the legs the clip's reach; a GPU duel logs the jolts, rings and clashes. TMotileDynamics about 80% reproduced, 68% live.

Done (motilepart12 lane, Claude): a block rings the striking weapon at the original's own rule, fists carry the weapon bit while unarmed, and death silences the voice. TMotilePart 58% → 59%.

Done (particles3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the particle emitters run their per-frame pass with the original's light, emissive and sound rules, fire emitters carry their detail values and loop sound, and the arc texture scrolls as the original's. TParticleSystem 82% → 86%.

Done (change `wire-the-skin-parent-frame`; skinning3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the skin build passes group centres from parent to child parts as the original does, and apparel paint uses the original's double-precision condition and mask names. RF_Skinning 82% → 85%, FLX_Character_Textures 77% → 79%.

Done (change `hold-bodies-and-arm-strikes`; thaumforce lane, Claude): kinesis and throw now hold their bodies in the force's own environment under a shell over the body's meshes, and Strikes puts an aura on each held weapon with the original's strikes shader; GPU runs show both. The posture switch now leaves a power's looping clip alone, as the original does, so a kinesis no longer ends at once. SG_Thaum_Force about 78% → 80%.

Round 38 audioenv3: the full voice pool's stand-in voice, story-level ambience and the outdoor arena weather ambience rule.

Round 39 lightning: a strike no longer clears the frame to black. The flash-clear branch is dead in the original (one read, no writer); a strike only tints the record fog colour with lightning blue along the original's flicker, the clear, sun, ambient and fog reach unchanged (EXA_LIGHTNING_FRAME forces strikes). The tint is not visible yet because the world material draws no fog.

Round 39 worldfog: the world material applies the original's fog (scratch colour over the stored fog span, mixed by the GI or ambient term, alpha fade on unlit opacity surfaces); the lightning-tinted record fog colour now lights the voxel GI from the sky, as in the original.

Round 40 meshunits4: the mesh unit's swept-sphere test and the material texture matrix; review made the sweep shorten to each accepted hit.

Round 40 vxgi7: GI shapes drawn on the GPU, resident blocks and neighbour margins; review fixed the margin fractions, the sky map matrix and the telebubble rounding.

Round 41 particles4: particle quad frames, the fire host emissive driver and the arc rules; review fixed a transposed axes product, a key off-by-one, the first-frame light bit and invented arc loops.

Round 41 shaders5: the super-sampling sub-frame fill as a tested model; review fixed the view matrix translation row, the clock order and the emissive word, and folded duplicate helpers.

Round 42 giseam: the outdoor shadow seam at the camera GI block's edge is gone (step 23.7 to 2.3 grey levels) by stepping every resident block and picking each object's block as the original does.

Round 42 lightpool: the exanima01 start room's white pool is the level's own shaped light, which Exarust draws; the held torch drowns it (retail's story start leaves the torch on the floor). At matched framing with the torch on the floor, frame means match retail within 1-2 levels. A light now ranges only with the flag and a radius above zero; --debug-lights, --no-torch and --floor-torch added for retail comparisons.

Round 42 skinning4 (re-landed with the bone-0 bind fix after a GPU regression): garment cull, depth-first joint capture and skin decomposition live; review gave the cull bits back to the garment slots, fixed the joint walk and rewired the decomposition inputs.

Round 43 braies: the practice opponent was bare because its template's local-bit item ids were looked up in the game's object database (its trousers, shirt and sandals became gloves, a helm and boots); actors now keep their own items and local ids route to them as the original's character builder does, so the duellist wears its own trousers, shirt and sandals and holds its club. The garment cull and visible masks were checked against the original and match.

Round 43 rendertail2: the scene-node render tail (light activation and alias copy, geometry node activation and hand-off, append-only attach, terrain block activation, kd builder flags, smoke per sub-view) as tested models, and a material's own environment cube live; review removed invented rules and duplicated luma, registries and scene-content wrappers.

Round 45 gameevents (G1, G4 in part): the trace corrected the port's reading of the GameEvent handler (voice request, not motion; delay, not duration; a power grant, not "has power") and named the record's class word and potential.

Round 45 storystart (U4, G2): the story start's steps reach the live game; tips show on knock-out and death; the manual opens from the menu. The start's "light" is the torch item laid at the start pose, which Exarust applies only without a carried torch, pending the owner's decision.

Round 46 dialogue (U1): the dialogue is finished; the player is named from the creator's saved preset when the creator is skipped.

### Phase 6 — AI (started)

Done (change `build-the-navigation-grid`): every level gets the original's
navigation grid when its load ends, a 30 cm grid of voxel columns built
from its terrain, its meshes' GI sample points and its tile-grid floors,
reduced to two layers with the cost bytes the planner reads; `--debug-nav`
draws it and `exa-tool nav` dumps it ([NAVIGATION.md](NAVIGATION.md)).
Done (change `build-nav-terrain-only-where-blocks-are`): the grid's
terrain comes only from the 6 m blocks the level builds, the renderer's
(in a tile-grid level the flagged ones), so the rock between exanima01's
rooms is no longer floor. Done (change `layer-every-terrain-material`): a
terrain tile stacks every material it uses (up to 16) instead of its four
heaviest, so exanima01's dirt rooms and the outdoor levels no longer show
the black fill through dropped materials. Next: the grid queries and the original's
look-ahead, then A\* and NPC paths.

Done (change `sense-a-contact`): a contact is seen out to 12 m (18 m once
tracked, 6 m asleep). Hostility with neutral traits settles near
0.0375 times the size of a negative attitude, and that heat chooses
notice, watch, engage or flee. A duellist that engages takes the guard.
Done (change `choose-the-fight-blow`): once the fight has started, an unarmed fighter inside eight tenths of reach can swing overhead and outside it can take the other side. An armed fighter stepping forward thrusts when its preference wins, and takes the overhead at age one half. A facing score above 4 takes the other side. Energy under eight tenths of the maximum refuses the swing. The duellist starts that blow.
Done (change `score-a-fighting-place`): reach 50 cm, size 1 and a step of 80 cm score a strike of 0.25 at 25 cm and 1 from 120 cm through 130 cm. At 600 cm the strike term is 0 and the fighter keeps closing. A spot 1 cm past reach plus 150 cm has a close term of 0. Outside a fight the close term doubles and the strike term is 0. A large opponent's ideal is 50 cm longer, so 100 cm scores 1. Wanting melee doubles a strike of 0.25 to 0.5. The duellist stands at 1.20 m and walks at 2 m. The attack-arc danger is not in.
Done (change `score-an-attack-danger`): a side swing on its arc end at phase 0.5 scores danger bits 0x40000000 at skill 0 and bits 0x40800000 at skill 1. Phase 0.35 scores bits 0x3ff28f5c. A thrust inside its arc at 100 cm scores bits 0x3f000000, and one outside it scores bits 0x40800000. At 200 cm, at phase 0, and when the side is not sweeping, danger is 0. A held weapon keeps the side-swing danger past that limit. Outside a fight the strike term is 0 and that danger stays. Feral 1 halves the close term and scores no danger. A dull feral fighter keeps the close term. The approach term and the recovering close tweak are not in.
Done (change `approach-a-fighting-place`): at 25 cm and at 200 cm, reach 50 cm and skill 0, the approach is bits 0x3ec00000. A wide fighter at 25 cm scores bits 0x3f88b000. At 400 cm, and outside a fight, it is 0. A recovering swing at 25 cm scores close bits 0x403c0000 and approach bits 0x3fc00000. Action 5 at phase 0 publishes kind 1, start bits 0x3fdf66f3, end bits 0xc01c61aa and look bits 0xbeb2b8c4. At phase 0.5 a hand on the side sets the end to bits 0x3fc90fdb. A recovering swing of that action publishes kind 4. The facing scale and the idle-action clock are not in.
Done (change `scale-a-facing`): a fighter who is not attacking, with the candidate beside it, scores approach bits 0x3f400000 at 25 cm. Straight ahead keeps bits 0x3ec00000. A press with the opponent facing aside scores strike bits 0x40300000 and approach bits 0x40840000. An armed action outside a swing publishes kind 4 when the idle stamp is 5 and the clock is 4, with both edges on the heading. Stamp 4 against clock 4, and stamp 0 against the maximum clock, publish kind 0. A dead body is knocked out at clock 50 with no delay and is down. A walking body stays walking. A blow does not call that entry. The part rewrites that precede it are not in.
Done (change `rewrite-a-dead-part`): a dead entry sets a held part's mark to 0x2001 from 1, its gate to 6 from 15, its span bits to 0x40000000, and its identity turn to 0, 0, 0, 1. An unheld part keeps mark 3 and span 9 and still takes gate 6. A negative basis takes turn 0, 0, 1, 0. A link of count 1 becomes kind 4 and bits 0x30. Count 0 stays. The marker clears. Loose 2 times 0.5 is bits 0x3f800000. Posed and the behaviour mark become 1. A step base of 4 becomes 2. The body is then down at clock 50. A walking body keeps its parts and stays walking.
