The race setup and the three calls after the part loops are not in. The characters section stays 27%.
Done (change `finish-a-dead-entry`): when both pair words are 0, class 0x800 and body kind 0x100 set the hands to firm and open, leave the bare mark on, clear the class word, and set the primary to -1. Class 0x500 marks the hands and clears the bare mark. A pair word of 1 leaves the hands. Feral 0 and feral -1 with a base of 1 score reach bits 0x42a00000. Feral 1 scores bits 0x42600000. Empty slots fill. An offset of 3, 4, 0 faces along x. A walking body skips this. The virtual call and the weapon pass inside the step are not in. The characters section stays 27%.
Done (change `hear-a-disturbance`): a disturbance above 0.3 is walked to. An alert of 0.2 or more faces a target more than 45° off, and otherwise looks around after 0.7 to 3 seconds. A quiet cell stays on the idle.
Done (change `idle-roam-patrol-sentry`): a roam waits, walks to a wander point, waits 500 ms plus a draw of 2000 ms, walks toward home, then waits 1000 ms plus a draw of 4000 ms. From a generator at 0, an arrival at 2000 ms waits until 2500 ms and a home arrival at 3000 ms waits until 4125 ms. A patrol only repeats the wander. A sentry holds the guard pose. A disturbance during a wait is walked to and the phase stays.
Done (change `pass-a-door-on-the-path`): a walker with no path, a cursor at the end, or no door keeps walking. A slider at travel 0.29 is more than 0.7 open and is left alone. A shut hinge holds the walker and is toggled open for 100 ticks. A slider at travel 0.3 holds the walker and opens for 60 ticks. A hinge already toggling is held without another toggle. The rest of the path is not in.
Done (change `give-up-a-door-wait`): a walker that is not waiting at a door stays as it is. At exactly 10 seconds a shut hinge is toggled and the wait still holds. One millisecond later the walker seeks again, the avoided node is cleared, and cursor 3 skips bit 3, while the search stays held and the hinge is not toggled again. A slider at travel 0.29 moves the walker past the door and lets the search run. One millisecond after 10 seconds that slider seeks, the search is already allowed, and cursor 32 skips bit 0. A start near the top of the clock does not give up at a small now. The heading sway is not in.
Done (change `arrive-at-a-span`): a walker that is not approaching a span stays as it is. A busy approach at its stamp stays there. Exactly 250 cm from the midpoint starts the door wait. A missing span seeks and sets only the high skipped bit. A later miss 251 cm out also skips cursor 3. The midpoint one millisecond later starts the door wait at that clock. The aim snap is not in.
Done (change `sway-a-wander-look`): a look that is not wandering stays as it is. At the clock, from extracted angle 1, the look eases to 0.91 and the offset stays. One millisecond later a unit draw of 1 sets the offset to 1.8, steps the roll to 134775814, and sets the timer to 5126. A heading of 4 at posture 1 eases to about −0.0685. The flag call is not in.
Done (change `snap-an-aim`): an aim snap copies the body's place. A heading whose height is 9 and whose forward part is 1 has yaw 0. Roll 1 steps to 134775814 and the timer becomes 3094 at clock 2000. The pace word and the body's marker are cleared. A defence clip starts Idle over 30 frames. A walk clip stays. Idle already playing does not restart. A wander look at that clock keeps its offset.
Done (change `pick-a-wander-point`): a walker with no room does not pick. The only exit from the origin toward a room point 100 cm ahead aims 25 cm forward, at height 0, with a stop of 20 cm and a radius of 150 cm. An exit 100 cm deep toward the origin aims 75 cm deep. An exit that leads back is drawn again, and that second exit 200 cm ahead aims 150 cm forward. The only exit that leads back is kept. Follow and sleep are not in.
Done (change `follow-a-leader`): no leader stands when there is no activity or panic is at least 1, and otherwise runs the activity. A leader facing the follower closes inside 150 cm and does not at 150 cm. The band is 400 cm, 280 while following, and 1200 while busy. Urgency is 0 at 500 cm, 1 at 801 cm, and 2 at 1601 cm or when the leader sprints. A planned spot 100 cm aside does not replan. 101 cm does, and the plan stops at 50 cm. A hidden follower who is not walking stands. Sleep is not in.
Done (change `lie-dormant`): a walking body whose flags are the shock stagger and whose tone is 1 sleeps into the dormant condition. The stagger flag stays and the dormant flag is set. Tone and the tone target become 0. It is a statue with gait 2. The tick after that changes nothing, and a second sleep keeps it. Waking when a target is seen is not in.
Done (change `wake-a-sleeper`): a sleeper whose flags are only the dormant flag stays asleep until it sees a contact. At clock 2500 that contact drops role 44 and knocks it out: down, the dormant and waking flags, no statue, gait 0, tone 0, pin gain 0.08, and the sleep countdown raised to 256. A sleeper that also has the shock stagger stays dormant. The tone rise after the knock-out is not in.
Done (change `rise-after-a-knock-out`): through 1500 ms the tone stays 0, the clip base is 0.4, and the condition is down with both flags. At 1501 ms the tone is 0.001 and the dormant flag clears. At 1502 ms it is 0.002. Getting up at phase 0 adds 0.002, and at phase 1 adds 0.001. A held body keeps the dormant flag. At 8000 ms the clip base stays. At 8001 ms a clip base of 0.995 finishes at tone 1, the target becomes the stance factor, and the flags clear unless held. A body that is not waking is unchanged.
Done (change `awaken-and-refresh`): the tick that drops the dormant flag tells the one person who can see, and not the person who cannot or the non-person who can. The next tick tells nobody. When the clip base reaches 1, a 40 cm weapon and an 80 cm weapon reach 80 cm and prefer thrust 6. With no hand bit, a weapon whose slash, crush and pierce are 0 uses the race reach of 50 cm and prefers 10. The witness memory update is not in.
Done (change `remember-an-awakening`): at 1501 ms and second 100, a seeing person who needs two hits counts 1. One already counting, with a 2 second delay, stamps 102 and sets the high bit. A second witness bit stamps 100. A last use of second 1000 stays, as do a person who cannot see and a non-person. The next tick leaves the count. A ten-second cooldown stays at second 1009 and counts 1 at second 1010. A never-again nibble stays. An activated stamp inside a ten-second window refreshes to 109. At the window, with a 2 second delay, it re-arms at 112. A future stamp stays and reports updated. Held-off leaves an activated word. A never-again window at the last second refreshes to that second. The grip pass, the arm-mass scale, and steering along an accepted path are not in.
Done (change `hold-a-refreshed-weapon`): at 8001 ms a ghoul holding fists is held again. Its balance is 1, its arm stiffness is 1, its hands weigh twice, and its reach is 50 cm. A Fael whose unarmed reach is 150 cm weighs four times, reaches 150 cm, and sets arm stiffness to one half. Steering along an accepted path is not in.
Done (change `step-a-carrot`): a ground point 50 cm behind a 200 cm segment keeps fraction -0.25 and aims 50 cm along. A point on a corner keeps the first segment, and the goal is 20 cm up and 100 cm along the next. A vertical segment is skipped and the goal is the last point. Exactly 100 cm of segment left has no goal, and the cursor stays at the start. The out-of-scene slide is not in.
Done (change `slide-out-of-scene`): with the low scene bits clear, the start of a 100 cm segment becomes fraction 0.02, the ground moves 2 cm, and a pelvis base of 90 raises only the standing height. Fraction 0.75 on an 8 cm segment with 8 cm after it becomes -0.28125, and the ground is at 5.75 cm. A vertical segment counts as length 0.1 and crosses behind the next start. One centimetre from the end of a last segment stops at the end. A set low bit does not slide. The run bit and the soft stop are not in.
Done (change `clear-a-run`): urgency 0 sets the run bit off the last segment, and on it while the carrot fraction, read as a double, is not past 0.99. The f32 value 0.99 is past that double. Ten centimetres from the carrot stops, sets the stopping bit, and keeps the carrot. Exactly 30 cm clears the run bit and does not stop. Forty centimetres up does not stop. A standing point 100 cm above the carrot pulls the goal 20 cm along the facing. Urgency 1 stops at 99 cm from the last point and does not at exactly 100 cm. Urgency -1 sets the run bit. The line walk is not in.
Done (change `walk-a-line`): a line across an open floor from cell 1 to cell 6 keeps its end. A floor of 40 at cell 4 blocks before that cell and keeps the asked height. Starting on the cell before that floor keeps the start. In the scene that blocked line clears the run bit, asks for a re-plan, and leaves the carrot. A clear line keeps the run bit. Out of the scene, and a higher scene bit alone, the line is not walked. The straight walk and the nearest walkable point are not in.
Done (change `query-the-grid`): a floor five above the current height is open to a straight walk and blocked to the line walk. Six above blocks the straight walk. A drop of four makes a later rise of six block. A high floor one cell ahead is not an obstacle, and two cells ahead is. A penalty of 61 on an early cell is an obstacle. A penalty of 60 is not, nor is 61 on the last cell. A point off the centre of a cheap cell keeps its place and takes the floor height. A penalty of 80 steps to the neighbour toward the origin. Footprints are not in.
Done (change `stamp-a-footprint`): a footprint at height 1 adds 63 to the centre, 32 to a neighbour, and 16 to a cell beyond. The obstacle test then fires on a line through the centre, and removing the footprint clears both the penalties and the test. A centre in the outer five cells is not stamped. At height 5 the upper floor takes the 63 and the lower stays 0. The mover refresh is not in.
Done (change `start-a-long-path`): an empty path and a single point are not followed, even when forced. Two points exactly 150 cm apart are not followed. Two points 151 cm apart are followed. Two points on top of each other are followed when forced. Steering along the points is not in.
Done (change `refresh-a-mover`): a plan through a footprint costs less than the same search with the footprint still down, and the centre is 63 again, with 32 on a neighbour. A zero-extent box covers its cell and a neighbour, inside the margin of three. A rectangle on cell 12 joins the one on cell 10, and one on cell 17 stays apart. Seventeen separated rectangles keep the newest sixteen. A sample at voxel 20 raises a lower floor of 10 to 20 and the cost pass runs. Voxel 30 raises the upper floor to 30 and leaves the lower floor at 10. A sample of voxel 12 on a floor of 20 whose underside is 15 raises the upper floor to 12. Voxel 255 and a sample outside the grown rectangle do not. The scene-wide countdown is not in.

Done (changes `add-roles-and-search`, `derive-the-npc-personality`, `fix-search-gather-and-finder`, `read-the-roles-once`): roles, relations, the attitude, encounters and the entry finder follow the original (`exa_ai::roles`, reading `exa_formats::roles`, the one parser of `charroles.rdb`; the corpse script sits after the sections of bits 0x1 to 0x8, and the level's locale comes from `locales.rdb`). Applying roles runs the init assignments in role-list order, forces the follow under mind control, maps activities to idle states and creates idle activity 6, the companion's search (`exa_ai::search`, gathering to tidying, item effects returned as deeds). The AI manager rules (rising undead, zones, alert, approach clamp) and the constructor's personality and attach are in as functions. Placed living characters carry roles, relations and a brain that rates the player every frame; hostile human-frame NPCs duel and sleepers wake. Not in: running the roles' scripts from the brain, power overrides and the group blend, roam/patrol walking, the manager frame and zones in the game, a search scene.

Still open: follow, sleep, and where the wander point is picked.

Done (change `perceive-as-the-combatant`): `exa_ai::combatant::perceive` is the original's perception pass for one observer: the contact record with effective attitude and trust, the group blend, the threat estimate, the crowd penalty carried across passes, the third-party concern target, the quick-swing event and the familiarity drift; with it the script's concern call and its two event bits, the queued combat step's clears, the move-point clamp, the nav footprint (round to even per axis), the hit rule with the attacker's mark, forget, replace and reset. `exa_ai::place`'s combat position score is corrected against the instruction listing (danger in and out of a fight, the approach peaking at the strike ideal, side-swing and thrust danger, the observer's heading, feral and dull scales; the measured point is approximated by the body position). The duellist perceives its target through the pass and scores its spacing with it. A `play exanima01 --opponent --press W@40-120 --fixed-frames` run of the merged batch logged the duellist in CombatIdle at 2.47 m and four weapon hits on the player between ticks 1056 and 2595 (`target/verify/gpu-opponent3.{log,png}`). Not in: the role database feeding the pass at run time, encounter scripts and the familiarity write, the mind-power calls, the removal broadcast, the pathfinder's footprint insert, the candidate search, target selection and morale.

Done (changes `run-the-npc-combat-state`, `flee-and-classify-contacts`, `check-the-leader-in-combat`, `keep-the-combat-edges-as-the-original`; npc_ai lane, wave 3): the 18 KB NPC combat state (`exa_ai::combat`) runs the duellist once per body tick: its exits, practice, the estimate (the direction kept within 1 cm), the weapon draw and lunge, the chase count and eagerness, the three drives with commitment, overshoot and lesson, the dead first count and jitter draws, the 0/8/10/16 plan, the 17-place search, the attack decision with its friend test, steps, the long step, the guard and fierce branches, the swing sweep and heading. A k = 0 duellist hesitates while its eagerness climbs (about 0.012 a tick toward 6), presses within 3 s, steps in at 5 m and strikes from inside 3 m; a fresh human guards in Cmbt-Idle, a tired one in Defence. The contact classifier is exact and runs in `sense::decide`; the target pass, flight (courage halved, stress +2, the way out of a room, the cornered fall-back) and the follow check are functions, not wired. The keep-off clamp's unordered compares are the original's, and the AI manager's approach clamp now calls it. Not in: the cast chooser, the chase route and path planner in the fight, navigation queries (defaults through `combat::Field`), the stab aim, releasing the swing in the game (flagged only), the run-stop helper, the body's own step physics (a stand-in step walks), a record personality (skill 0, blend 1, one contact at level 3). On the RTX 3090 a `play exanima01 --opponent --press W@40-120 --fixed-frames --frames 1800` run of the merged wave logged the duellist entering combat (Defence, then CombatIdle) and four weapon hits on the player (ticks 973, 1096, 1227, 1357, wounds 0.31–0.42) until it died 250 ticks after its collapse (`target/verify/gpu-opponent-98.{log,png}`).

Done (change `follow-the-planner-and-search-instructions`; ai2 lane): the pathfinder's A\* follows the original's instructions: ties-to-even cells and heights, the goal-height heuristic, the node's own-layer floor, the blocked window border, unsigned compares, the 100 cm end rule with the rough cut, and string pulling through the budget line walk that holds the far corner's floor (`exa_world::nav::path`). A long NPC-sized plan on the shipped levels takes about 1 ms or less on the worker. The grid keeps its floors as built, so the mover refresh starts from them, raises them by the underside rule and the half-voxel sample, and lowers a floor again once its mover has gone; the ring keeps filling through the scene-wide countdown. `exa_ai`'s unused second planner and the nine spec requirements describing it are gone. Corpse scripts take each role's first Player entry, and the search's take, wear and drop deeds edit the scene as the original's executors do. `--debug-path x,y,z` plans from the player and draws the result. At integration (batch 12, with the power classes, the force effect nodes and the fixups) the merged tree passed the workspace's tests and clippy on the worker; on the RTX 3090 `play exanima01`'s frame-90 screenshot matches the last batch's (`target/verify/batch12-exanima01-90.png`); `--debug-path 3,0,0 --press W@60-200 play arena` logs a reached two-point plan and replans after each metre walked; `--opponent --press W@40-120 --fixed-frames --frames 1800 play arena` still fights, with the duellist's blows on the player as light in the tree before the batch (impact sums 0.03 and 0.06 over seven hits for 5c09b655, 0.04 over seven and 0.41 over fourteen for the merged tree), so the lighter hits are run-to-run variance in the fight's course, not this batch; settled `--perf-log 5 --frames 1800 play exanima01` runs alternated with the tree before the batch averaged 46.7 and 31.0 ms a frame (medians 47.5 and 28.9) for the merged tree against 57.7 and 35.6 ms (57.5 and 32.3) for 5c09b655, on a host at load 9–12 whose noise is larger than any difference, so no slowdown is measurable. Next: NPCs planning and following paths in the game (with the combat state's chase and flee), props marking and refreshing the grid, and running the role scripts.

Done (change `chase-cast-and-integrate-contacts`; motileai lane): an NPC's chase plans its route through the nav grid's reviewed A\* with one plan turn a frame (`exa_ai::chase`, `exa_ai::route`); the chase ends the combat tick only while the route is followed, marks the foe unreachable for 3–6 s when the route falls short, and the duellist walks it. The run stop runs before the drives and the can-act gate is the original's; a duellist releases a swing once. The cast chooser and its tear-down are in (`exa_ai::cast`), with no power system to answer them. The contact integration, the full target pass, the nearby list and the morale and alert drift are ported and tested but not scheduled, because perception does not produce the contact words yet. The per-contact place scorer's notice and band terms are overwritten in the original and are not kept. TMotileAI about 59%. In the arena opponent runs at integration the duellist kept closing and striking through the 30 s run (6 and 6 hits against 14 and 6 on the previous main, within the run-to-run spread). Next: feeding the integration from perception and scheduling the brain pass, a power system for the cast, and the scene-side half of the AI tick (the activation holder, the room list, the head look, the gait and the locomotion). The bark sound, the footprint lift, the object flag for the round-robin kind, the chase goal's motion word and the cast busy bit are labelled guesses.

- Perception (sight, hearing), navigation on tile grids and terrain
  (the original's 30 cm two-layer grid and its A\*, no navigation mesh),
  activities and idles.
- Combat AI that drives the **same control inputs** as the player
  (movement, facing, attack direction, stance), so NPCs obey the same
  physics. Skill-dependent timing and aggression.
- Companions and hirelings, arena opponents, monsters (golems, demons,
  wraiths, ogres, skeletons).

**M6 "fair fight":** NPC-vs-NPC and player-vs-NPC duels that behave like
the original's.

Done (change `wire-ai-brain`; reviewed): the NPC brain runs in the game. Every frame each brain clears its contacts as the original's target pass does, perceives every character within reach with the role database's attitude, the observed character's contacts and the relations' trust (a knocked-out character's concern drops by 0.99; dead, asleep or removed characters are cleared; a lost target raises the alert), then drifts its morale, integrates its contacts and runs the target pass over every character it saw. The pass decides the duellist's engagement, replacing the mind's rating; a neutral NPC watches a nearby fight. On the RTX 3090 an arena duel still engages about 4 s after the spawn and `exanima01` keeps its frame rate. Not yet: the original's one pass a tick (the longest waiter), a line-of-sight test, the nearby list, retargeting and flight once the room graph exists. TMotileAI 60% → 61%, TCombatant 85% → 86%.

Done (change `fade-and-describe-the-thaum-tree`; thaumtree3 lane, reviewed): the power tree screen fades in over 1000 ms (window `min(2(1 − r²), 1)`, bar child `1 − r²`) and out over 256 ms, a domain switch fades out and back in, and the view steps in 17 ms frames with the pan blended between steps. The bar opens and closes over 900 ms with the GUI smooth step `(3 − 2x)x²`, both animations running together when both are flagged; the tree draw's learn and flash fades use the smooth step too. The shown power's name caption appears at once and its wrapped description box after 601 ms, measured with the GUI font; label mode names every node while a power is dragged, and the stretched link runs from the placed node. All of this is wired in `power_screen`. The review removed a duplicate saved-power reader (the grant routine read twice). Not in: the GUI form and domain wheel, the skin's text draw, UI sounds, the draw head's cursor zoom blend, the spline texture. TThaumTree about 42% → 48%.

Done (change `layer-the-force-and-displacement-passes`; thaumforce lane, reviewed): the five effect materials (`thaum::effect_material`) and their four layered passes (`thaum::effect_pass`) are reproduced as CPU draw plans: no draw without a pass count, layer 0's colour set first, the effect material's and tendril's per-layer depth term in double precision (the shader's and shell's in single), brightness (display bit 0x40) and the shader's coordinate divided by the divisor, the aura and shell materials writing 0x9000 to the node's state word, and the displacement shell pass separating its depth-only pass (0x200) from its fade pass (0x400) and restoring the fades. In the running game Shell, Strikes, vanish, Long Door, summon and constant-displacement casts spawn their effect nodes, the shell aura outlives its power, and a Long Door places the body at the destination in metres (the review fixed a move 100× too far). Not yet: GPU submission and GLSL for the passes, the effect geometry meshes, the vanish's show/hide on the drawn body, sounds and the demon. SG_Thaum_Force about 47% → 60%, SG_Thaum_DIsp 45% → 57%.

Done (change `reproduce-the-motile-ai-routines`; motileai2 lane, reviewed): the NPC brain now links into the 600 cm room grid and gathers its cell's characters (each cell stands for a room until a room table is loaded), decays its group alert every 10 s, re-applies its roles when they change, and turns an idle model toward the original's eased, swaying yaw. Tested but not running: the ally-class sense (rewritten from the listing), the head look, gait, walk and alert look, the idle driver and go-to-object state, the room walk, the lifecycle hooks, and the reset the original applies when a level is written out (the lane's wiring of it to scene entry was removed because it healed NPCs and made duellists leave combat). Next: a level cell-to-room table and room exits, an NPC locomotor for the head and body look, the ally-class branch in the perception pass, a level write on leaving to call the reset. TMotileAI about 61% → 70%.

Done (change `draw-and-wheel-the-thaum-tree`; thaumtree4 lane, reviewed): the power tree screen gains the draw head's zoom blend about the cursor and the eased opening scale about the first node, spline-ribbon links with their start and end handles, join and width terms, the screen's six UI sounds and the arrow-key bar cycle, the form's creation fields and the original sheet bindings, and a domain wheel child built on `effects::wheel` whose confirm (a known, different domain) switches domains in place. The review restored the first zoom level (−8) and the slot click's stored node, the glow accent's alpha and the single-precision spline width, removed a third copy of the grant routine and a guessed wheel, and brought back the bitmap-font fallback for the tree's texts (`Rayform08R` is not in the installed packs, so the lane's glyph quads drew nothing). Remaining: the wheel's own ring and icon draw, the GUI form class and skin panels, a source for the GUI font atlas, GPU submission of the tree's draw. TThaumTree about 48% → 64%.

Done (change `review-the-force-ribbons-and-hits`; blastfx lane, reviewed): the force blast and torrent build their ribbons and draw them with the glow stand-in (`effects::ribbon`, `draw_blasts`, `draw_torrents`) following the original's quad emitters (the previous point is the previous call's point, the head's face pass uses p₀ − p₁, each step keeps the instructions' precision). The release alerts the caster's known characters within 18 m; a struck character takes the shared knock (length summed z² + (y² + x²)), its instability counter +1 and concern 1 at 0.01, once a tick for the torrent. Thrown chunks and running points meet the level as swept balls; a bolt takes the sampled static walk, limbs the face walk at a reach of ten. The review removed the bolt's invented sampled limb walk, restored the torrent's limb reach of ten (keeping round 19's bit-for-bit strike pin), and corrected the knock order, the NaN pin gain, the ribbon width and the face sign. Not in: the GL passes, sounds, GI injection, the support-pin gain and recoil consumers, the engine's exact queries, simulated objects' points, the turn spring. TForceBlastEffect about 57% → 72%, TForceTorrentEffect 68% → 76%.

Done (change `run-the-brain-through-its-rooms`; motileai3 lane, reviewed): the NPC brain runs its perception, AI tick and flight once per body tick. Perception sees through the original's sight callback: in grid levels the room reach test (the same room, or an open door whose point lies within 2 m: `exa_ai::rooms::in_sight` over `Rooms::light_reach`); without a grid, everyone (the review replaced the lane's invented nav-grid eye ray). The AI tick reads the level room and previous room (`Npc::enter_room`, keeping the last real room) and the room's exits and answers each contact's way out from them, so scared NPCs run to an exit (`brain::flee_walk`, straight steering once per tick, no nav route). The out-of-combat look chain runs, its gait helper rewriting the body's heading vector, and the attach's sense mapping is fixed (class 0x12xx gets the standard sense, other races the ally sense, which no running race selects yet). Still missing: activation moves and body re-place, per-room occupant lists, a nav route for the flight, the roam/patrol walker and idle driver. TMotileAI about 70% → 73%.

Done (change `review-force-and-displacement-units`; thaumforce2 lane, reviewed): every Force and Displacement focus, facing, distance, pace and clip check follows the original's unordered-compare branches in the live casts, creator pitches take the double step, each active creator keeps its power record (ids, costs, the long door's drain, destination and forward axis), the summon adds no node, and the material routines' node transitions and multipoint GI table are in. The review replaced the lane's per-material release with one release callback per unit (12 program handles plus the GI table for Force, 2 for Displacement), moved the end routines to the tendril image, and removed invented arguments and raw offsets. Remaining: GPU submission of the effect passes, the effect geometries, sound playback, wiring the constant displacement into a body, the summon's demon, the AI long door's destination. SG_Thaum_Force about 60% → 70%, SG_Thaum_DIsp 57% → 66%.

Done (change `fix-the-npc-motion-arithmetic`; motileai4 lane, reviewed): the NPC walk, alert look, room walk, idle driver, investigation, body-in-the-way test, look writes and re-placement match the listing, with double-precision gates, NaN branches, the use timeout after a completed use and the collapsed body keeping its placement; the personality's eagerness keeps its original sum order with its shares in double, and the ally sense weighs an in-combat observed body at 0.25 (both live). The review reverted the lane's eagerness reorder and corrected the ally factor, the use timeout, the investigation gate, the walk direction, the body-in-the-way shift and two look writes. Remaining: a roaming NPC walker to run the idle driver, walk and alert look, the scene holder move, the physics re-placement, the standard sense's per-contact threat. TMotileAI about 73% → 74%.

Done (change `edit-and-label-the-thaum-tree`; thaumtree5 lane, stalled after two commits, reviewed): the tree's node and link-turn drags follow the move handler (a tenth in double, the link taken from the form's focus id), a flash and its refusal end when the flash's time is up, the spline count converts through 64 bits, a focused prompt caption is the skin's glowing text, and tree text uses the GUI level's main font with the bitmap font as the fallback; missing domain icon sheets draw no icons and the wheel's confirm plays its sound. The review fixed the node drag's precision, a link-turn focus field nothing set, a refusal cleared after one frame, an invented label centring and caption outline, and removed dead fields. Remaining: the press that enters the edit drags, the wheel texture's resample, the link ribbon texture. TThaumTree about 64% → 66%.

Done (change `walk-roaming-npcs`; motileai5 pass by a Claude agent): roaming and patrolling NPCs walk their room graph live: they plan on the one-route-a-frame planner through the navigation grid to a quarter of the way through a drawn door, walk the "Walk" clip, settle and pause, and a roam then walks a leg into the room it aimed at; every waiting brain runs the original's idle head (the alert look while panic is at least 0.2 with the in-combat mark, then attention with the clamped look window), replacing the look-at-target guess; a body re-entering the scene mid-leg faces its route goal. Remaining: the idle-activity driver's use of the walk, turning by body locomotion, the scene holder move and physics re-placement, the standard sense's per-contact threat. TMotileAI about 74% → 76%.

Done (change `paint-the-domain-wheel`; thaumtree6 pass by a Claude agent): the power tree's domain wheel paints itself as the original does: a flamelets backdrop, six sector bands scaled by the domains' picked shares, the chosen domain's glow, six icons on the 76 px ring (the hovered one with four glow copies), the six-segment ring frame and the hovered domain's rounded label, from `domainwheel` resampled to 32 units. No retail press enters the tree's node, link-turn or link drags and the link ribbon has no texture; both are documented rather than invented. Remaining: the GUI form class and the base form's key handlers. TThaumTree about 66% → 69%, TDomainWheel 37% → 68%.

Done (changes `free-the-tree-key`, `key-the-shown-thaum-tree`; powertree pass by a Claude agent): the power tree never appeared because the debug pause key was P, the tree's own key: the press opened the tree and paused the game clock its fades run on, so it stayed at zero alpha. The pause moved to F8 and simulation keys never take a bound game key; a scripted-press test with the installed packs checks that the tree opens, fades in and draws. The shown tree now takes the keys as the original's does (arrows step the bar, any other key closes it after the 50 ms opening guard) and `exa_ui::form::form_key` models the base form's key handler. Remaining: the canvas z-order against the HUD, GPU submission of the tree draw. TThaumTree about 69% → 72%, TGUIForm 85% → 86%.

Done (change `keep-the-power-tree-canvas`; treedraw pass by a Claude agent): the running power tree keeps its software canvas between its 33 ms redraws and redraws only the boxes of the batches that changed (on the open tree the domain wheel and the link glow, about 11% of the window), in parallel row bands with per-row spans, byte-identical to a whole redraw; the canvas goes to Bevy with straight alpha above the HUD. On the RTX 3090 the open tree now runs at about 59 FPS instead of 19.

Done (change `refresh-the-grid-where-items-are-let-go`; navrefresh pass by a Claude agent): the navigation grid's refresh runs in play once per fixed tick after the bodies step, every queued rectangle at once, from the bodies in the 6 m rooms it spans. As in the original, only letting go of an item lifted onto the cursor queues a rectangle (the floor it was lifted from); a door swung open or a crate knocked aside queues nothing, so NPCs still cannot plan through a door closed at load. `--nav-refresh-moved` (an Exarust debug flag) also queues doors and props that come to rest somewhere new. Remaining: the registration queue for flagged nodes and the scene-wide countdown's writer. TPathFinder about 88% → 90%, SG_PlayerControls 56% → 57%.

Done (change `ask-the-nav-grid-in-the-npc-combat-state`; aicombat pass by a Claude agent): the duellist's combat state asks the level's navigation grid the questions the original asks its pathfinder: each place costs the grid's cost sample through the combatant's formula (1 on open ground, more near walls and other characters' footprints), places and the long step stop where the line walk meets a wall, a foe behind an obstacle counts the fighter as stuck toward its chase, and the duellist lifts its own footprint around its combat ticks as the AI tick does; before, every grid question had a neutral answer and the duellist stepped through walls. Remaining: the stab's head tracking with the held-weapon pushes, the executor's striker guard, the search activity over the loot. TMotileAI about 76% → 78%.

Done (change `follow-and-search-the-dead`; charai pass by a Claude agent): the AI action executor makes the original's held-count test first, counting the fists (the empty hands hold both), so unarmed fights still start and only a body with no strikers is refused; the companion's search runs in the game for the first time: a level brain whose roles choose the follow follows the player through the navigation grid and, inside its band, searches the level's dead bodies, reading item values from the object database at the weighing's offsets and carrying out its takes on both sides' possessions (`--searcher <id>` makes a level character an Explorer for checking). Remaining: level containers and loose items as searchable instances, the kneel clip, re-dressing after a take. SG_CharacterAI about 38% → 41%, TAISearchActivity 73% → 80%.

Done (change `route-the-flight-on-its-turn`; motileai6 lane, DeepSeek, stalled after two commits, reviewed): on levels with a navigation grid a fleeing NPC picks and routes its way out on its own planning turn, replans while no route is followed or near the goal, follows only routes whose ends lie more than 150 cm apart and marks the exit's room as fled when the route ends near the threat, so it runs around walls to its door instead of beelining. The review replaced the lane's copy of the route planner with the real one (it had dropped the planner turn and the follow test), routed from the feet's height, and removed a stand reset that duplicated the existing look settle and a restart that changed nothing. The idle return is a tested function its chase caller does not call yet. TMotileAI about 78% → 79% with this round's combat-state work.

Done (change `review-the-motile-ai-tick-rules`; motileai7 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the duellist's entry takes its target through the original's set-target rule (flat bearing, aim from its yaw), the AI leaves a fight through the leave-combat routine, familiarity drifts only existing relations, and removed characters are forgotten; the review fixed the lane's footprint gate (it read the combat state, not the route-following flag) and removed its duplicate footprint stamps, invented first-sighting relations, a combat-idle stance on entry and the estimate tick's rules in set-target. TMotileAI 79% → 81%.

Done (change `measure-the-combatant-origin`; combatant3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the duellist scores its combat places from the opponent's measured origin and publishes the original's fighting estimate; the review corrected five lane rules against the assembly. TCombatant 86% → 92%.

Done (change `raise-the-skin-boxes-and-style-frames`; guiskin3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the GUI draw skin's boxes, troughs, thumbs and style frames follow the original's virtual table; the review reverted three lane errors against the assembly. TGUIDrawSkin 90% → 91%.

Done (motileai8 lane, Claude): NPC footprints follow the original's gate in play, the duellist leads a moving foe when it sets its target, leaving a fight runs the original's leave routine with its 3 s look timer, contacts are admitted by weight as the original does, and the duellist holds its stance while parrying. TMotileAI 81% → 83%.

Round 39 aihit: blows now reach the struck character's behaviour in the original's order (clash and block told first in their branch, a wound after the damage, pain voices and DamageTaken); exa_ai::struck runs the combatant base's courage drop, swing mark and practice lesson (live on the GPU) and the motile AI's concern rise with contact word 3 (tested; duellists without a brain have no contacts).

Round 39 motileai8: the NPC brain's hit reaction, grabbed-flag timeout and investigation goal as tested functions; review reverted a wrong move of the alert look's 45 degree gate and made a NaN concern skip the reaction.

Round 39 thaumtree8: the power tree's bar and prompt hit tests follow the live interface scale, and the bar no longer fades with the wheel.

Round 40 aiengage: brain-bearing characters now engage the player (the brain and duel tick name the foe by the player's fighter, not its root, so the target pass's pick matches); the player's arena roles follow the original (Unaligned in practice, the company's Faction 1 in the arena); combat-mode W moves a muscled body again (the capsule takes the footwork step's velocity). GPU: a brain duellist engages at tick 270 and lands 8 hits in 50 s.

Round 41 combatant4: the duellist reads its foe's swing word, fighter blades and reach, and scores places by engagement level; review moved the foe lookups onto the fighter after round 40's foe fix.

Round 42 golemwake: the golem alarm zones run live: the AI manager's zone check for the player (3 cm step, radius and box tests, the key-token pass search) wakes every statue golem within 800 cm of an entered exanima06 alarm zone, which then targets the player at concern 10 (GPU-checked); golems cannot fight yet because their bodies are not physical in Exarust.

Round 42 motileai9: the standard AI sense ported and the race word picking each brain's sense live; review added the kind gate and fixed the selected character read.

Round 43 ai3: encounter scripts run in the game and the script scene setup is shared; review reversed four lane inventions (line helpers stopping short, an off-floor key, a corpse-section override, a not-busy approach).

Round 43 alarmreturn: the golem alarm return runs live from the story revive (dead player with red at least 0.01, five seconds later): every awake, non-dying guardian within 3000 cm goes home and sleeps as a statue, and the player is reset 200 cm past the Alarm_ box facing back with motion 0x1d; carrying the zone's key token lets the player cross without waking anything (GPU-checked). New debug flags --story-session, --give and --fell-player.

Round 43 golembody: golems get physical bodies built as the original builds every race's (ragdoll, the character init's muscles with 0.995 link smoothing, a mind and a duellist, Stand 2 start); statue golems rest under the sentry tick (dormant body, blows turned, sleep scales on contact mobility) until the alarm wake ends it. On exanima06 two woken golems close on the player and strike while 45 statues stay at rest; their blows land with the halberd haft for no damage yet.

Round 44 polereach: spear stabs now land at the point (9 of 11, was 0 of 3); golems barely improve because their factory halberd has no body. Plus tooling: test runs tile four games on the primary monitor (--window-at / EXA_WINDOW_AT).

Round 45 aisense (C3): the backlog premise was wrong; humans already used the original's base perception, and only the Golem Suit runs the standard sense.

Round 45 bitesocket (C2, P2): there is no separate feral bite in 0.9.5; any held weapon sticks in a wound and being feral only shortens the hold. A port bug that wrote the stick bits into a word nothing reads is fixed.

Round 45 clashstain (C1, C7): the struck weapon rings on a clash and wounds ring each armour layer from the routine's own table; blade stains clear at a world reset only; an existing bug that left a cleared stain pass drawn is fixed.

Round 45 matclass: the armour class off-by-one was in the audio crate only and is fixed.

Round 45 weaponbody: factory weapons are bodies and wound where they touch; golem stabs still mostly land on the haft (they stand inside their halberd's span), so a retail capture of a golem stab is the next check.

Round 46 activities (C4): the backlog premise was wrong again; no role installs the idle driver and the investigation's disturbance is a constant 0, so NPCs never walk to objects in the original.

Round 46 koknock (C8): the premise was wrong; a knocked-out body never turns its muscles off in the original. A revived story player never got up before; now input wakes them.

Round 46 tickchain: AI timings no longer depend on the frame rate; cost 0.17 ms a tick in exanima06. Added --schedule-ambiguities and the profiler's brains line.

The player's head tracker runs while it stabs (the behaviour tick calls it only then): the eye is the character frame times (−20, 10, 0) cm and the tracked flat yaw is the look the sampler reads. Other actions leave the look alone. The far variant still has no caller.

A mechanism relay now forwards in the running game: a broadcast reaches every tagged door linked to it, a pair sends to both targets, and a combiner keeps its state on the record. A sequence relay still has no stored counter, and a light job still has no electric object to power.

### Phase 7 — Game modes and content

Done (change `run-an-arena-career`): a new company starts with 343 copper
(2 gold, 4 silver, 7 copper). It hires seven combatants and one of each
specialist. A standard match takes its fee; a win pays the prize;
experience is the template amount times the damage share, plus half with
a trainer.
Done (change `advance-the-story`): a story loads `exanima01` and advances
through `exanima08`. A topic can be chosen once. The journal opens on
"New Entry" and stores the written text.
Done (change `cast-the-four-domains`): long door places the body, shock
takes yellow health, blast knocks 60 along the aim, and calm lowers
concerns. Each spends its focus and does nothing without it.
Done (change `price-the-power-table`): the table holds the 33 recorded
rows. Barrier at 0.07 for a 500 ms tick costs 0.035, and 0.0175 with the
sustain technique. Repeating blast with the repeat technique costs 0.05.
Blast spends 1.2 times its cost, barrier twice, calm 0.3 times, the long
door once, and vanish 80 times.
Done (change `read-a-power-tree`): a tree whose first word is wrong is refused. The node count is the word at byte 52. A node at 10, 20 stays there. A node at 15, 25 becomes 5, 5. One 16-byte link is kept. A link count below 1 stores none. A file that ends after the magic is an error. Drawing the tree and the text library are not in.
Done (change `read-the-tree-text`): a text chunk stores Blast and an empty string. A tree with no chunk has no strings. A chunk that ends after its first word is an error. The second node is still at 5, 5. Drawing the tree is not in.
Done (change `draw-a-tree-line`): a node at 2, −1 with zoom 1 and no pan snaps to 20, −10. A node at 0.25 snaps to 2, 0 and stays at 2.5 while dragged. A factor of one half pulls 20, −10 to 10, −5. A link with both marks at zero draws nothing. A large node at the origin and a small node 400 to the right, both turns zero and zoom 1, starts at the large reach above the origin and ends at the small reach above 400, in 19 points, and is dim when the source mark is zero. Icons, frames, glows and the thick-line quads are not in.
Done (change `draw-a-node-picture`): an icon with no columns draws nothing. Power 6 on a sheet of 4 by 2, size 10 at 100, 50, sits at 95, 45 in cell 0.25, 0. A second byte of 1 uses bias 1. A view of 10.5 is 10 for a wide node and 5.25 for a narrow one. Frame slot 5 at size 10 is an 8-unit square at −4, −4 in cell 0, 0. A glow of width 10 and outer 4 has inner 8.4, 33 spokes, and the first sits straight up. Learning a node and the thick-line quads are not in.
Done (change `learn-and-thicken`): a lone node is marked 0x10000. A known id with bits 20 to 23 set still matches, and the later pair wins. A link to a closed node stays closed. A link to an opened node gains 0x10000. A top nibble of 7 does not. A mark of 0x5000 becomes 0x15000. A width of 2 through 0, 10 and 20 sits at 10, −2 and 10, 2. A zero-length chord stays on the point. The other effect meshes and charge-up are not in.
Done (change `charge-a-blast`): a 12 ms tick rates the charge at 0.01, and 1000 ms at five sixths. An amount of 0.5 plus 0.5 stays at 1 and keeps charging. An amount of 0.6 plus 0.5 clears to 0, sets the state to 1 and releases. Fizzling 0.5 by 0.1 leaves 0.3. Fizzling 0.3 by 0.2 becomes 0 with state −2. After release, 0.2 rises by 0.3. A still chunk grows by 0.0018 in one tick. Chunks at 0 and 1 with radius 1 move to −0.5 and 1.5. A pair 2 apart stays, and a pair on the same point stays. The other effect meshes are not in.
Done (change `fade-a-barrier-ripple`): no ripples leave the dome extent at 30, and four ripples make it 35. A ripple at shown 1 and strength 1 becomes about 0.96 and 0.95, and a hit 10 ahead eases the place to 0.5. Shown and strength of 0.06, and of 0.07, are gone. Wave, torrent and shell meshes are not in.
Done (change `step-a-wave`): a 12 ms wave rate is about 0.02, and 1000 ms is five thirds. Charging 0.99 by that 12 ms rate releases. The blast rate for the same tick leaves the amount at 1. A charging radius grows by 0.0015. A released front of speed 0 has reach 0.6, radius 0 and trail 1. Speed 10 grows the radius by 0.006 and sets the trail to 1.7. Torrent and shell meshes are not in.
Done (change `fade-a-shell-ripple`): shown 1 and strength 1 at scale 1 become 0.96. The same pair at scale 2 becomes 0.92. Shown 0.09 and strength 0.09 become 0.05 and stay. Shown 0.05 and strength 0.05 are gone. The torrent mesh and the shell mesh are not in.
Done (change `step-a-torrent`): a charge of 1 below phase 1 sets the level to 0.4, and a charge of one half sets it to 0.3. Phase 1 with channels at 0 eases them to 0.03 and 0.036 while a level of 0.4 falls to 0.39. Phase 2 multiplies channels at 1 by 0.96. A level of 0.03 falls under 0.02 and stops. The stream mesh and the shell mesh are not in.
Done (change `place-a-torrent-stream`): spans of 1, 2 and 3 count as 1, 2 and 2 streams. A stream along height 1 is placed at height -6. Two streams ask for 10 records, 130 groups, 1560 bytes and 1040 indices. A radius of 2 at scale one half and margin 1 reaches 12. Age 1 of 2 samples raises channels at 0 to 0.0025 and 0.005. A second channel of 1.8 still rises, and the next float does not. A point at the origin inside a reach of 10 moves to 0.01. A point on the reach stays, and a point past it is missed. The index winding and the shell mesh are not in.
Done (change `place-a-shell-vertex`): a position at 1, 2, 3 offset by a sum of 0, 3, 0 is raised on its height. A zero sum stays at 1, 2, 3. A hit of 0.1 stores about 3.333. A hit of 0.3 stores 6. An eighth hit is refused. The copied index list is not in.
Done (change `copy-a-shell-index`): two visible ranges and a hidden range copy to 14, 15, 16, 11, 12, 10, 17, 18, 19. A reversed triangle stays reversed. A range that ends early is empty. A short list is an error. A missing remap is an error. The skinned section filter is not in.
Done (change `keep-a-skinned-section`): a section with bit 2 and a hidden section that also has bit 2 copy to 14, 15, 16, 11, 12, 10. A section without bit 2 is absent. The same hidden section copies nothing on the object path. A reversed triangle stays reversed. A range that ends early is empty. A short list is an error. A missing remap is an error. The compacted vertex map is not in.
Done (change `compact-a-shell-vertex`): marks for a skinned range, a plain range, and a later skinned range are 0, 255, 2. Three vertices number as 0, absent, and 1. Indices 2, 0, 2 become 1, 0, 1. An absent index is an error. A short mark list is an error. The GPU buffers are not in.
Done (change `record-a-shell-slot`): three vertices store section 0 local 1, nothing, and section 2 local 2. A later link is ignored. A base of -1 subtracted from 1 stores 2. A short corner list is an error. A missing base is an error. The GPU buffers are not in.
Done (change `count-a-shell-index`): two skinned triangles, a hidden skinned triangle and a range that ends one early count as 3 triangles and 32 bytes for two kept vertices. A plain range adds nothing. A range that ends two early counts as -1 triangles and 16 bytes. The kind is 3 and the base is 0. The GPU upload is not in.
Done (change `fade-a-shell-by-its-size`): the stored size is 800. Opening for 0 stays at 0, opening for 1200 stays at 1, and opening for 1201 finishes at 1. A held open enters the hold state. A hold at 0 stamps 200, and a half rounds to a stamp of 10. A close of 400 is one half, and a close of 800 finishes at 0. A held close shows 1 with a gain of 1.5. A resting fade stays. The GPU upload is not in.
Done (change `light-a-shell`): an opening light at 0 is 0, and at one half it is a tenth of 0.75. The float just under 0.666 stays on the bend, and the next float is a twentieth. A resting light at 1 is a twentieth and keeps its gain. A closing light at one half stores a gain of 1.4. The GPU upload is not in.
Done (change `pair-a-shell-light`): a resting pair is 0.1 and 0.75. An open at 0 is 0 and 1. The float just under 0.25 is just under 0.1 and 0.75. An open at 0.25 is 0.1 and 0.75. A hold keeps its pair. A close at 0 is 0.1 and 0, and a close at 1 is 0 and 0.75. Three triangles ask for 28 header bytes and 12 payload bytes. A name of 0 stays, and a name of 1 unmaps. The play renderer does not issue the buffer calls.
Done (change `submit-a-shell-index`): two skinned spans submitted with the flag set and a name of 1 put 3 indices on the mesh, a header of 28 and a payload of 12, and unmap. The same spans with the flag clear keep a room of 32 bytes and do not unmap. The Bevy render graph does not issue the original's GL calls.
Done (change `save-the-session`): a session save restores that session. Since `reproduce-the-save-slots` the save is a package in the original's shape, its `#playerdata` section carrying one Exarust chunk (tagged `EXRS`) with what the original's sections do not hold yet; a retail save opens as a package and keeps its sections when saved over, but restores no session, having no Exarust chunk.

Done (change `confirm-a-shop-trade`): the shop refuses two empty baskets, and refuses a buy the company's copper cannot cover. A larger sell is received.
Done (change `scale-match-experience`): a shared finish multiplies the template by 1 + tier/4 and splits it by contribution. Tier 0 with shares 1 and 3 credits 250 and 750; tier 4 with a trainer and equal shares adds 1500 to each. The special board lists Challenger through The Beast. How a tier scales a fee was not read.

Done (change `close-the-arena-day`): a merchant's wage is 2, a physician's 12, a trainer's the skill total, and a combatant's half of that, at most 5, plus 1. A new career is on day 0. Ending the day pays the bill. A day mod 7 of 1 gives the week to three or more of the six events with no tie. Day 7 drops an entry under health 3276. Four won weeks grant four rewards; from day 0 that lands on day 22.
Done (change `rest-overnight`): a night adds 6553 to health, or 9830 when a physician is hired, and stops at 65535. A pool of 58981 becomes 65534. A pool of 58982 becomes 65535. Health 3275 across seven nights is 49146 and stays entered. Health 1000 with a physician becomes 10830. The trainer's lessons are not in.
Done (change `teach-a-lesson`): with no trainer a technique of 7 stays at 0. A trainer who knows 7 adds 1000. A trainer who knows 3 adds nothing. A merchant and a physician stay at 0. A word of 4500 snaps to 5000. Five other skills at level 1 make the grant 166. A first skill at 25000 is left there and the next skill becomes 166. Item prices are not in.
Done (change `price-a-hire`): 11 copper hires nobody and stays at 11. 12 copper hires a combatant and leaves 0, with 12 spent. A second physician is refused and the spent total stays 24. Item prices are not in.
Done (change `price-a-shop-line`): a line of 7 buys for 7 and sells for 1. A lower mark of 2 buys for 24, a higher mark of 2 for 288, and both marks of 2 for 2880. Two lines of 3 sell for 1. A line with every bit set buys for 23591520. Buying 40 then buying 10 against a sale of 100 leaves 318 copper and 15 earned. Emblems and the reward item identities are not in.
Done (change `scale-a-match-stake`): a stake dword is copper, silver times 12, and gold times 144. Challenger asks 12 for a prize of 36. The Beast asks 24 for a prize of 432. A duel prize is 12. Tier 1 doubles both. Hiring one combatant and winning Challenger at tier 1 leaves 379 copper, with 72 earned. Emblems and the reward item identities are not in.
Done (change `grant-season-rewards`): a swept season grants four item ids. Season 0 is 1175, 1174, 1177, 1176. Season 1 is 1167, 1165, 1168, 1169. Season 2 is 1171, 1170, 1173, 1172. Season 3 is 1178, 1179, 1180, 1181. Day 134 grants season 0 again. A lost season grants none. Emblems are not in.
Done (change `keep-the-season`): a save on day 8 with the first two weeks won loads those weeks and no reward ids. Sweeping the loaded career through day 22, then saving again, restores day 22, one season, four rewards, and the ids 1175, 1174, 1177, 1176. The original's packages are not read.
Done (change `select-the-klean-emblem`): the name klean selects background 52 and design 81, with layer bytes 7, 0, 0, 6. The pictures are emblembkg052 and emblemdes081. Any other name selects nothing. A candidate's listed price was not read.
Done (change `price-a-candidate`): no skills and no gear cost 12. A skill total of 2 costs 48. Skill totals of 10 and 12 cost 432. A gear line of 7 costs 18. A two-silver line costs 31. 47 copper refuses the skill total of 2. A full roster with no copper is refused before the cap, and with 100 copper the cap refuses without spending. A second physician leaves the spent total at 60. The other emblem controls are not in.
Done (change `draw-an-unwatched-match`): teams of strength 4, then 2 and 2, then 10, drawn from rolls 1, 4 and 12, name teams 1, 2 and 3. The rolls become 0x08088406, 0x20221015 and 0x6066303d. Mode 16 for team 1 names team 2. A strength of 100 set aside still names team 1. An empty list names nobody and still steps the roll to 0x08088406. The bracket that clears losing records is not in. `XA_Arena` is 24%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `clear-a-losing-bracket`): width 1 with force 5 leaves ids 5, 0, 3 and 0 and steps the roll to 0x08088406, and reports another group. One group from roll 1 leaves id 1. Roll 100 leaves id 9 at roll 0x235391f5. A zero id of strength 100 stays 0, id 1 is cleared, and both ids of 3 stay, at roll 0x20221015. A forced group does not step roll 4. An empty list keeps roll 7 and reports no other group. `XA_Arena` is 26%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `draw-a-rival-field`): roll 0 draws 1, 2, 17, 6, 8, 14, 9, 4, 11, 12, 3, 13, 5, 19, 7, 15 and ends at 0x4b157bf0. Roll 1 draws 1, 17, 5, 7, 14, 8, 4, 10, 11, 2, 13, 3, 18, 6, 12, 19 and ends at 0xead32bb1. Three companies are left out. Spawning them is not in. `XA_Arena` is 28%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `choose-a-daily-match`): rolls 0 and 1 both choose templates 0, 1, 4, 1, 2, 3 and end at 0x1210d4e5. Roll 4 chooses 0, 1, 6, 4, 4, 1 and ends at 0xb761a41e. A limit of 2 stops a third pick, a slot before the earliest is refused, and mask 3 refuses a top-three bit it shares. The clock that replaces the roll, the template copy, and the fee scale are not in. `XA_Arena` is 30%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `scale-a-daily-match`): counter 0x04000000 replaces roll 99 and chooses 0, 1, 6, 4, 4, 1, ending at 0xb761a41e. Counter 0 chooses 0, 1, 4, 1, 2, 3 and ends at 0x1210d4e5. A high bit on the same low word does not change the row. A copied fee of 3 and prize of 12 at slot 3 with bit 2 become 12 and 48, and the tier is 3. Kind 8 shows 0 and 5. A tier of -4 becomes -1. Unit 0 at slot 4 leaves tier 4, and unit 1 leaves tier 0. Bit 4 at slot 0 from roll 0x10000000 stores extra 3 and ends at 0x50000001. The typed field walker and the unit generator are not in. `XA_Arena` is 32%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `draw-a-unit-and-copy-fields`): seed 5489 draws 0xd091bb5c then 0x22ae9ef6. The first unit's bits are 0x3fea12376b800000. The index is 2. A mark of 5489 against stamp 0 draws 0xd091bb5c and reverses the mark. That unit from seed 1 at slot 4 sets the tier to 4. Bytes 0x11 through 0x88 with fields at 1 and 5 copy in full. A three-byte buffer and a backwards field are errors. Managed strings are not in. `XA_Arena` stays 32%. The runtime section stays 4%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `leave-before-a-match`): a hired physician at 699 is removed and the death count is 1. A hired merchant at 700 is removed and the count stays 0. A hired trainer at 65000 stays. A hired combatant at 0 stays while the physician behind him is removed. A hired physician at 100 is removed and the merchant at 100 stays. An empty roster removes nobody. Releasing a kit and unlinking a world character are not in. `XA_Arena` is 34%. The runtime section stays 4%. The game-modes section stays 0%.
Done (change `begin-an-arena-match`): in-game command 4 in arena removes a hired physician at 699, asks for a save that does not abort on low health, marks the hub busy, clears the click hook, and sets the phase to transitioning. An empty roster still does that. Story, practice, command 1, and another page change nothing. Closing windows, writing a save package, and the transition are not in. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The game-modes section stays 0%. The runtime section stays 4%.
Done (change `dismiss-marked-windows`): the same command dismisses a child flagged 0xce100000, then a fully flagged child, and leaves an unmarked child, a later marked child past count 2, and a child flagged 0xce000000. The physician at 699 is still removed. Story dismisses nobody. Count 0 and count -1 dismiss nobody. The method on the child and the call after the two lists are not in. `TGUIForm` stays 0%. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The game-modes section stays 0%. The runtime section stays 4%.
Done (change `close-a-dismissed-child`): a pinned window closes child A, skips an already closed child, then runs its own callback. The notice is raised. Argument 0 is stored. An already closed window is still dismissed, runs no callback, and leaves its child open. The physician at 699 is still removed. The capture release, the task's other children, detaching, redraw, and the call after the two lists are not in. `TGUIForm` is 1%. The UI section stays 0%. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The game-modes section stays 0%. The runtime section stays 4%.
Done (change `finish-a-form-close`): a captured shown window that is the hover drops the capture, clears bit 0x20, clears the hover, closes the sibling that names it, hides, and the redraw word is 3 after four asks. An already closed captured window drops the capture and does not ask. The active form clears that pointer and does not ask. A miss leaves the bit. Cursor restore and the call after the two lists are not in. `TGUIForm` is 2%. The UI section stays 0%. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The game-modes section stays 0%. The runtime section stays 4%.
Done (change `restore-a-cursor-and-follow-lists`): releasing the top capture publishes the lower code 0x15, shape 0, and the default. Code 0x8007 publishes shape 7 and does not mark the default. The only capture publishes code 0 and no holder. Mask 1 with clock 99 and mode word 6 copies the ten hand words, leaves mode word 3, and stamps 99. Mask 0 with no focus leaves the hands unset. A focus with mask 0 latches and clears the pending byte. The shape table and the virtual call are not in. `TGUIForm` is 3%. `SG_PlayerControls` stays 8%. A point must lie strictly inside the band and a card; it then names that card's ordinal plus one. At screen 1000, clock 2048 and scale 1 the cards occupy 212-468 and 500-756 and the band 652-1164. `SG_GameMain` is 15%. The UI section stays 0%. `TGUIDrawSkin` stays 55%. `TGSLines` stays 87%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.

The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `start-the-cloth`): the cloth start adds 0x10000 to its flags, copies the seed, installs a 4x4 identity, sets its step to the square root of the count times 1e-6 and links a body carrying 0x10000000. Done (change `activate-the-cloth`): activation adds 0x20000000 to 0x300, 0x10000 to 8, 0x204030 to 0xc and 0x6000 to 0x188, sets phase 2 and scale 0.4, writes the handle, copies the root origin and adds 0x8000 when slow. Done (change `sync-the-cloth`): the sync skips its transform and pose when the object's meta flags carry 0x10; otherwise it transforms once and poses with scale 1.0 twice. `TVCCloth` is 74%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `press-the-cloth`): the pressure pass skips on gate 0x100, normalizes every normal, stores the offset as the normal dot the edge plus its length, projects the edge's normal component, scales it by the negative root of 0.1 over its squared length above 0.2 or subtracts it, and moves the normal by the corrected edge times the negative length. Done (change `destroy-the-cloth`): the destructor frees four stored pointers, the origin only for a root, destroys three managed fields, frees each list entry and the list, runs the base always and makes the release and final calls on a positive free flag. `TVCCloth` is 78%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `scale-the-first-hit`): a blocked or helpless combatant returns unchanged; otherwise the first hit scales by (1 + stat) x 0.025 + 0.95, a target without bit 0x100000 takes the threshold when lower and gains the bit, and a body with the bit makes one special call. The combat-rule figure moves from 27.3% to about 29%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `wake-the-combat-targets`): the wake clears bits 0x6 from the body's 0xdf8 word, wakes the body once and calls the 0x148 virtual for each listed target; a clear argument does nothing. The combat-rule figure stays about 29%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `forget-the-combat-target`): the forget removes the first entry equal to the target by moving the last entry over it, clears the two stored slots and the body's hit and contact pointers when they match, and leaves the list alone when the target is absent. The combat-rule figure stays about 29%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `reset-the-combatant`): the reset zeroes the count, three qwords, two words, a byte and the body's hit and contact pointers, sets 0x7a0 to -1, clears a marker's two words and decrements a reference's count. Done (change `replace-the-combat-target`): the replace sets the first matching entry to the new target, substitutes the two stored slots and the body's hit and contact pointers when they match, and leaves the list alone when the entry is absent. Done (change `charge-the-thaum-tree`): the charge adds the tick over 120 in double precision, clamps to -9 through 5 and derives the gain as (5 + charge) x 0.2 or (4 - sqrt(-charge)) x 0.25; the started byte and clock stamp are set on the first call only. `TThaumTree` is 19%.
