The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `hit-the-skill-point`): the 857-byte TSkillUI point hit returns 0 for state bit 1 below page 2, the base for a foreign or refused point, 0x10000 for the page-2 bottom band, the base at state byte 4, 1 or 2 for a high node offset between 13 and 4 units, a rounded 1-through-8 slot for a low node over 10 units at 4.5, or the fallback. `TSkillUI` is 25%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `update-the-nearest-morph`): the morph controller stores its target count and copies its two arrays, and keeps the nearest target only when a new distance is strictly closer, with NaN refused. `RF_Morphing` opens at 1%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `find-the-nearest-morph`): the controller scans its transformed points and returns the index and smallest squared distance from a maximum-float start, refusing NaN distances; an empty list gives −1 and the maximum. `RF_Morphing` is 2%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `switch-the-morph-target`): only the tag 0xEDC0C589 switches; on a match the point and points are copied, the point is stored at the target and the target refreshes. `RF_Morphing` is 3%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `blend-the-morph-points`): each source point times its weight is added to the matching destination point, once per point count and once per target entry, with the shorter count winning. `RF_Morphing` is 5%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `apply-the-morph-parts`): the walk counts and applies to each node whose kind masked with 0xFFF is 0x89 and whose 0xC00000 bits are 0x400000, then recurses into every child, returning the count. `RF_Morphing` is 6%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `merge-the-morph-weights`): a scale at or above the epsilon merges up to four source ids, adding weight × scale into an existing slot or appending a new id while the last used slot leaves room, and stops at the first id below one. `RF_Morphing` is 7%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `append-the-arena-records`): the arena log appends twenty-byte records with an id and one value, or an id and four words, raising the count and returning the slot. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `blend-the-morph-point`): the query starts at the block base and adds each block point times its weight when the weight magnitude reaches the epsilon. `RF_Morphing` is 8%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `parse-the-morph-block`): the tagged block is read as the tag 0xEDC0C589, an unknown word, the first count and points, the owner's skipped points, the second count and, per entry, the first points and the owner's points; truncated or foreign blocks return none. `RF_Morphing` is 11%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `pick-the-arena-value`): the pick counts the positive of three values, advances the seed by seed × 0x08088405 + 1, indexes by the high half of seed × count and returns the value, seed and index. `XA_Arena` stays 34%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `search-the-skill-links`): the 1363-byte TSkillUI link search tests the two slots beside the hit node, ranking by the largest dot above 0.1 with the y offset scaled by 4 for a mostly horizontal direction, then scans five satellites around every node and keeps the smallest length over the dot squared above a 0.3 dot, returning the code and point. `TSkillUI` is 32%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `fade-the-skill-screen`): the draw opens from alpha 0 in state 4, returns 1 with bit 0 clear or a passed deadline (clearing the bit), and otherwise uses t = (deadline − clock) × 0.002, x = (1 − t)(1 + t), alpha min(x, 1) and sound x(2 − x). No coverage change; the rest of the 2955-byte draw remains open. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `fade-in-the-skill-screen`): bit 1 clear changes nothing; a passed deadline sets state 4 and either returns alpha 0 on page 2 or closes; otherwise the sound becomes (deadline − clock) × sound / delay. No coverage change; the rest of the draw remains open. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `track-weapon-blood`): the original's TWeaponBlood list is modelled: a hidden source adds nothing; otherwise a source takes the first free spot or grows the list with its scale over 80 and inverse root, an amount and tag of 0; clearing the count empties it, clearing a spot frees it, and an amount add clamps 0 through 1. TWeaponBlood 53%, combat section 14%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `skin-morph-vertices`): the 448-byte morph apply is modelled: each vertex takes the three points its joint names through the remap as a 3 × 3 basis, multiplies its offset by them, subtracts the controller's base, applies the owner's rows and adds its own base. RF_Morphing 14% (2,346 of 17,099 bytes). The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `forward-the-main-menu`): page 1 runs action 0 and page 8 action 1, each entering the session and sounding only when input is ready; any other page does nothing. No coverage change; TMainMenu is not separately rated.
Done (change `walk-the-character-view`): the chain walk stops at the first node whose record is missing or whose flags carry both 0x12000000 with a negative word, and follows the next node otherwise. No coverage change; TCharacterView is not separately rated.
Done (change `open-the-settings-menu`): the settings form is created and attached when none exists or re-shown when it does, laid out, entry 1 selected behind the resume flag and finished with −1; closing and flag clearing act only on an existing form. No coverage change; TEndSessionMenu is not separately rated.
Done (change `solve-the-morph-basis`): the 767-byte solver projects the query onto the control triangle's plane (the normal at the record start, then the three points) and returns barycentric weights, choosing the branch by |x0+x1| against |y0+y1| and taking the third as 1 − u − v. RF_Morphing 18% (3,113 of 17,099 bytes). The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `start-the-morph`): the 893-byte start creates the owner's helper when missing (ensuring its controls only when both are missing), refreshes the owner output, binds each vertex by the smallest squared distance from the maximum float, stores the nearest candidate's barycentric weights as the offset and the vertex as the base, and records vertices with no candidate as unmatched with joint 0 and a zero offset. RF_Morphing 23% (4,006 of 17,099 bytes). The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `adopt-the-morph-point-order`): the adoption seeds a target triangle whose keys match a reference triangle under a rotation (XY key distance at most 1e-8), floods through shared points mapping each unvisited neighbour to an unvisited reference triangle, leaves unmatched points on their identity and applies the map; adding a part refuses a count mismatch and otherwise stores zero weights and target-minus-reference deltas with no children. RF_Morphing 76% (13,311 of 17,099 bytes). The node identity pairing and tag stream selection remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `charge-the-spirit-form`): the Mind power's spirit form charges or fades by one value each 30 s, holds at 1, ends below 0.002, and publishes 1000·value while fading or a smooth 1 − (1 − value)² blend with the (1 − smooth)·rate + 1000·smooth strength and the 1 − value vector scale. SG_Thaum_Mind 2%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `toggle-the-mind-effects`): the mind mask starts removal on event 2, clearing body bit 0x10000 while removing and setting it otherwise; the mind sense starts removal on event 2 and triggers while removing only when its stored value is above zero. SG_Thaum_Mind 3%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `fade-the-mind-sight`): the overlay forces alpha 1 while shown, starts fading out past its end, moves alpha by the elapsed milliseconds over 2 s in both fades and ends below 0.001; the sight picks the nearest candidate inside the n·scale·0.5·40/width radius. SG_Thaum_Mind 6%. The billboard basis and shader upload remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `blend-the-mind-weights`): the sight weights A = 0.2·v78 and B = 0.2·(v974 + v978 − v7b4), force all three to 1 on a marked body or A above 1, otherwise floor A, clamp B, take the maximum, the ratio A/(A+B) and the threshold max(M+0.2, 0.6·v7c, 0.015·v74) capped at 1, and blend the entry by 2^(−dt/1000) (one half at a second or more). SG_Thaum_Mind 7%. The dwell windows and visibility tests remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `billboard-the-mind-sight`): the quad eye anchors at the origin plus (anchor_a+15)·m1 + anchor_b·m2, the direction runs normalized to the camera, the axes blend m2×dir/a×dir against m0×dir/c×dir by 1 − |view·dir.x|, U and V scale by 0.025 and the eye output is the negated view direction. SG_Thaum_Mind 9%. The shader upload remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `colour-the-mind-sight`): entries take colour0 {0.1,0.4,0.6}, colour1 {0.8,0.6,0}, a third lerped from {0.4,0,0} to {0.31,0.78,0} by the third value, alphas 0.1 + 0.6·(1 − first/second) in double precision and a quad alpha less a positive fade; kind 1 uses the pale palette. SG_Thaum_Mind 10%. The shader upload remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `dwell-on-the-mind-sight`): a sight tick runs at most every 4 ms; the window is 3000 ms above dwell 1, 5000 ms on a marked body and none otherwise; the fade is the elapsed time over the window and the node is lost past seen + window. SG_Thaum_Mind 11%. The visibility test remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `apply-the-loyalty`): event 2 on an applying loyalty returns 2, otherwise it moves to removal; an applying loyalty refuses a missing or marked target into state 2 or applies marker 0x10010a to a valid one and returns to state 0; a removing loyalty removes the target and returns 2. SG_Thaum_Mind 12%. The controlled-list allocation remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `command-the-dead`): event 2 on an applying command-dead returns 2, otherwise it moves to removal; an applying command-dead moves a missing target to state 2 or applies marker 0x10010d, raises the target flag and returns to state 0; a removing one removes the marked effect, clears the flag and returns 2. SG_Thaum_Mind 13%. The serialization and visual remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `rebuild-the-morph-remap`): the 680-byte rebuild composes the map as input[map[i]], gathers each part whose flags are clear by input over its stride (component count times the kind's 8, 1 or 4-byte element) and count, leaves flagged parts alone, always rebuilds the arrays, indices and vertices, and on bit 0 purges, marks the owner dirty, stamps 0xe4 and re-binds the 0x2e0 object. RF_Morphing 27% (4,686 of 17,099 bytes). The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `cook-the-morph-data`): the core's blob and delta are modelled: the magic 0xEDC0C589, the size 12(P+1)(V+1)+12, the base, the rest points and each part's pivot and per-vertex deltas, with a delta equal to the barycentric triangle point minus the pivot rotated by the owner's rows. RF_Morphing 32% (5,472 of 17,099 bytes). The target diffusion and skin stream remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `fill-the-skin-weights`): the 32-byte skin records merge a scaled source through the four-slot merge, normalize by stopping at the first zero joint (negatives zeroed) and dividing through the last slot by the sum, fill a target at 1/sqrt(distance squared) over the distance sum, and run three relaxation passes of half self plus half inverse-distance neighbours. RF_Morphing 37% (6,327 of 17,099 bytes). The target diffusion and registration remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `diffuse-the-morph-targets`): each unknown vertex with known neighbours takes their weighted mean at 1/sqrt(distance squared), is promoted together with the pass's other fills, and the passes repeat until one changes nothing. RF_Morphing 40% (6,843 of 17,099 bytes). The stream registration and owner re-bind remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `register-the-morph-stream`): the stream registers only with weights and owner bit 0x200000, reusing the low-0x1000 entry or growing the list with flags 0x04000001 and type 0x02a41000, and zeroing V·32 bytes; the target list takes the flagged triangles' remapped corners in the order 1, 2, 0 without duplicates. RF_Morphing 43% (7,353 of 17,099 bytes). The blob byte layout and owner re-bind remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `serialize-the-morph-blob`): the blob round-trips as little-endian magic, size, base, rest points, part count and each part's pivot and deltas; parsing rejects a wrong magic or a size not matching the vertex count and requires the stored part count to agree. RF_Morphing 46% (7,866 of 17,099 bytes). The three evaluator routines and the frame/apply remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `evaluate-the-morph-points`): the three evaluators are modelled: restoring returns the blob base and rest points, the weighted base adds each part's pivot times its weight, and the full evaluation adds each part's pivot and per-vertex delta times its weight to the rest points, skipping weights under 1.19209e-7. RF_Morphing 51% (8,755 of 17,099 bytes). The 1,128-byte frame/apply remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `bake-the-morph-parts`): the gather indexes children by the two name digits and zero-fills gaps; the bake selection-sorts ascending by ordinal from a best of 100, sets each delta to its position minus the model's, and with more than nine parts subtracts part 5's vertices and deltas from parts 6 through 8. RF_Morphing 62% (10,563 of 17,099 bytes). The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `price-the-path-grid`): the grid maps world to cell as trunc(world × inverse unit + 0.5) and back as (cell − 0.5) × unit, orders the neighbours NW,N,NE,W,E,SW,S,SE with costs 7,5,7,5,5,7,5,7, accepts a step only with a set floor, a −20 through +4 rise, A at most 180/(quality+1) and shifted B at most 600, adds 10·dz², the direction, twice A and the shifted B, and heuristically measures ten times the 3D distance truncated. TPathFinder 32%. The open list, two-layer selection and reconstruction remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `search-the-path-grid`): the A* uses a LIFO open list popped by the smallest f (newest wins ties), a 4095-node cap and the acceptance distance, re-parents an open neighbour to a smaller f, rebuilds the path from the best node's parents and collapses collinear runs; the goal is reached only when the best node is the goal cell. TPathFinder 44%. The two-layer rule, line-walk shortcut and endpoint fix-up remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `snap-to-walkable-cells`): a cell is walkable with a set floor and A+B under 80; a walkable start returns at once with base + floor×10, otherwise a best-first 32×32 window sweep in distance order (newest wins ties) returns the nearest walkable cell or nothing within 220 nodes. TPathFinder 58%. The two entry variants and layer pick remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `shortcut-the-path`): the first and last points are kept, and each point is dropped when the direct line from the last kept point to the following one is clear, kept otherwise. TPathFinder 59%. The original's backward indexing and endpoint fix-up remain. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `trim-the-path-end`): from the path end, up to eight points whose penalty byte exceeds 64/(quality+1) are dropped, the first point is always kept and the remaining count is returned. TPathFinder 60%. The greedy line-walk budget remains. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `budget-the-path-shortcut`): the shortcut passes its line walk the budget of the later cost minus the kept cost plus ten, keeping the first and last points. TPathFinder 61%. The two-layer floor rule remains.
Done (change `select-the-path-layer`): layer 0 is taken when its floor is at most four above and refused past ten; a floor five through ten above marks no layer and layer 1 can still win when higher and within four; the chosen rise is held to −20 through +4, including the layer-1 ceiling rejection in the narrow band. TPathFinder 63%. The scene-wide countdown remains.
Done (change `clear-the-path-stamps`): the 256×256 stamp table is cleared to the blocked value on its whole border: the first row, the tail from 0xfeff and both edge entries of rows 1 through 254, with the interior left to the caller. TPathFinder 64%. The constructor globals remain.
Audit: the coverage ledger is generated from context/re/component-map/map/data.json by context/tools/component_map_doc.py. With the entries of that audit the headline was **17.0% reimplemented** and **5.7% provided** (the current figure is in [`COMPONENT-MAP.md`](COMPONENT-MAP.md)); the sections are runtime 4%, core 41%, data 21%, rendering 35%, world 29%, physics 30%, characters 33%, combat 31%, items 17%, AI 13%, thaumaturgy 4%, audio 28%, script 0%, game modes 0%, UI 0%, other 0%. The 14.4% quoted in older Done lines is that earlier data snapshot, not the current tree.
Coverage update (2026-10-20): the UI, mode-screen and line-skin components were re-rated from the actual crate helpers, replacing stale fixes that claimed the UI held only font.rs and fps.rs. UI is now 13% (TGUIDrawSkin 45%, TGSLines 60%, TSkillUI 25%, TMainMenu 20%, SG_GameGUI 8%, TGUIForm 5%), modes 1% (TClassSelect 40%, TEndSessionMenu 5%) and the audited headline 18.7% reimplemented with 5.7% engine-provided. The modes section remains conservative because XA_Arena, XA_SaveGames, SG_Character_Create and SG_GameMain have detailed fixes at 1-3%.
Coverage update (2026-10-20, second pass): the mode components were re-rated from their crate helpers, replacing fixes that still said no save system or creator flow: SG_Character_Create 15% (create.rs page helpers), XA_SaveGames 5% (save_chunk.rs), XA_Arena 5% (arena_log.rs and the arena map), SG_GameMain 5% (loading, starts, presets, music). Game modes is now 5%, UI 13%, and the audited headline **19.0%** reimplemented with 5.7% engine-provided (16.0% by function count).
Coverage update (2026-10-20, third pass): TMotileAI 2% -> 18% (idle/roam/patrol/sentry, path walking and door waits, duellist choice, contact rating, hearing, orders, placement, sleep and memory) and SG_PlayerControls 3% -> 8% (movement request, click classification, drag and box limits, encumbrance blend, focus/watch hooks). The AI section rises and the audited headline is **19.6%** reimplemented with 5.7% engine-provided (16.4% by function count). Missing: the 18.4 KB combat-state core and the Cursor Look/key-table/drop/interact half of the player controls.
Coverage update (2026-10-20, fourth pass): the tree and Force effects were re-rated from thaum.rs: TThaumTree 15%, TForceBlastEffect 20%, TForceDomeEffect 20%, TForceWaveEffect 20%, TForceShellEffect 60%, TForceTorrentEffect 20%. The powers section rises and the audited headline is **20.4%** reimplemented with 5.7% engine-provided (16.8% by function count). Missing: the tree draw and text-chunk loader, the effect meshes and the GL uploads.
Done (change `build-kaiser-mips`): the Kaiser table (8·2^(level-1) taps, beta 4, c = 2^-level, I0 and sinc, normalised) and the separable axis pass (integer ratio, centered start, wrap or clamp per tap) are modelled in exa_render::mipmap. RFP_Mipmaps_Kaiser 20%. The format converters and upload path remain.
Done (change `drive-the-kaiser-chain`): build_mip_chain resamples each level directly from the base, horizontally then vertically, with the per-level Kaiser table, stopping when a destination dimension is zero, their sum is below 3, or a dimension does not divide exactly. RFP_Mipmaps_Kaiser 30%. The NPOT fallback and upload remain.
Done (change `upload-the-kaiser-mips`): rfi_to_image now appends the Kaiser chain for decoded RGBA8 textures with one stored level and power-of-two sides of at least 4, setting the mip count to the base plus the generated levels; other textures keep their stored levels. RFP_Mipmaps_Kaiser 40%. The NPOT fallback and GL upload remain.
Done (change `parse-the-tree-text`): the power tree's 0xABCD0000 chunk is parsed as a 16-byte header with the count, count+1 little-endian offsets, the blob size and length-prefixed UTF-8 strings, with every out-of-range read refused. TThaumTree 18%. The 11 KB draw remains.
Done (change `create-the-tree-text`): a fresh chunk carries language 0x6e65, flags 0x4e4, kind 1, marker 0 and one empty string, and the parser reads the same four header words. TThaumTree 20%. The 11 KB draw remains.
Done (change `focus-the-force-kinesis`): the kinesis scale is ((1/speed) + 0.015) * 0.5 with the 2.8 reach and 0.5 damping, and the activation handler moves to removal on bit 1 or 3, releases a held target and returns 2, or hooks the owner, raises the target flag and registers the callback. TPwrForceKinesis_S 10%. The grab update and release body remain.
Done (change `advance-the-force-barrier`): the barrier orientation swaps each point's y and z, and the pose advance makes the current pairs previous, the next pairs current and stores the frame. TForceBarrierEffect 6%. The setup, update and draw remain.
Done (change `update-the-force-barrier`): the barrier update computes its fraction as (target frame - base frame) + probe and lerps the current pair toward the previous one, with the 0.2 padding. TForceBarrierEffect 9%. The setup and draw remain.
Done (change `model-the-effect-ticks`): the item-effect glow is 0.5*(0.01*level)^0.7 with 0.004 charge accumulation and attach-once entries; the golem dormancy scales are 0.05/0.02 with the distance+pi+roll-0.5 homeward delay; the arc helpers cover the (0,53,0) colour, the part filters, the 0.8+0.2*roll brightness and the 0x21-node release. Equipment effects 20%, golem guardian 20%, RFE_Effects_Electricity 10%.
Done (change `weigh-the-dynamics-volume`): the dynamics volume's summary walks the padded count grid, returns (sum/count/dim - 0.5)*scale and count*product(2*scale/dim), falls back to mass 0.1 when empty, and sorts entries with the original's middle-pivot quicksort. RF_Dynamics_Volumes 5%. The grid construction, solid test, corner masks and sphere extraction remain.
Done (change `lay-out-the-item-panel`): the item panel's grid shape and backwards fill, the five-pass relax and seeded scatter with the 0x8088405 generator, the fixed-cell topmost alpha hit test, drop/raise and shift-left removal, the accept rules, the list/DB position codecs and the popup inner size. TGUIItemContainer 20%. The drag payload, mouse handlers, item cards, icon cache, doll load and popup placement remain.
Done (change `match-the-room-manager`): the world-to-cell conversion now multiplies by the original's f32 1/600, the room rectangle converts to centimetre bounds with the 0-280 cm band, the packed edge word rotates by quarter turns, and a direction vector gives the original's opposite one-cell step. TGridEnvManager is re-rated to 45% on the flood, portals, door reach, tracker, fades and shells already in rooms.rs and room_view.rs. The render objects/buffers, partner mask, migration payload, descriptor copies, segment tests and object attachment remain.
Done (change `drag-the-item-panel`): a mouse-down maps to no action, a drag start with the cell-centre grab, use, open or card (right passes through, drag needs permission and a hit), and drag end maps to no change, cancel, snap back, linked transfer or removal. TGUIItemContainer 25%. The callbacks, popup placement, item cards and icon cache remain.
Done (change `drive-the-motile-body`): the motile body's two time warps, 0.75 tired clock, dual charge/capacity reservoir, segment-table inverse-mass redistribution with 0.175 mobility, friction by mode and contact smoothing, the action mask/threshold and 40-tick/0.9/0.99 ready gates, the cycle reset and the local impact band. TMotileDynamics 25%. The 20.3 KB tick kernel, solver, SetState, torque and animation kernels remain.
Done (change `fill-the-arena-table`): the six standard, seven ranked and five tournament match records; the id-11 'beast' lookup; base-12 packed cash with the slot multiplier; the 1+0.25*slot XP pot, health scaling, manager bonus and damage split; rank points, loadout tiers and XP gains; wages, recovery and the unfit threshold; the 7-day/4-week calendar; the weekly and season prize pools; the 19 company profiles and the point limits. XA_Arena 15%. The match flow, opponent generation, live board, shop, tournament resolution and UI remain.
Done (change `schedule-the-arena`): the slot-biased rank roll and Challenger's 0-5 window, the secondary value's slot scaling, the cap/min-slot/reject generation filter with the engine generator and retry, the template and prize draws, the win/practice/other settlement tags with the Beast counter, and the last-week/all-wins season award. XA_Arena 18%. The live board, opponent generation, shop, tournament resolution and UI remain.
Done (change `step-the-motile-tick`): the tick's posture scalars, follow gain and point step, posture ramp and support decay, tone-weight paths, cadence target, pitch impulse, angle wrap and impact threshold. TMotileDynamics 27%. The main dispatcher, limb pull, support/friction stage, helper A/B and state machine remain.
Done (change `measure-the-form`): the form's skin measures and DPI border, its 302x402 defaults, the 4/36/centre/far anchors, the styled client rectangle, the scroll step/thumb/x and setter, and the label brackets and unit/3 nudge. TGUIForm 12%. The main-menu row spacing (the binary formula does not fit its own list), text advances, key traversal and the settings/manual rows remain.
Done (change `page-the-main-menu`): the six main-menu pages with their exact rows, order and enable flags (title, conditional VERSUS start, in-game CONTINUE/NEW GAME, arena and story pause, gated NOVICE class) and their row-origin fractions. TMainMenu 35%. A retail 1600x1080 screenshot measured ~70 px row spacing, so the raw 70*0.5*round(H/135) reading remains unresolved and no spacing formula was guessed. The draw, constructor, global back router and settings/manual rows remain.
Done (change `arm-the-arena`): the six loadout tiers' 8/11/10/14/4/4 weapon rows with names and variant ids read byte-for-byte, the non-zero variant draw and the 5/10/15/20/30/40 point limits. XA_Arena 20%. The loadout cost, opponent spawn, live board and shop remain.
Done (change `measure-the-controls`): the three control text spans with the 3U|1 quirk, the trackbar cursor/value/thumb/caption geometry, the 6U list row and 3U separator, the clamped 1-based row hit, non-wrapping navigation, the 16-row extent, the content width/shortcut rule and radio/check activation. RF_GUI_Controls_Ex 12%. The text field, drop-down events, spinner, colour picker, progress bar, image/texture views and menu draw remain.
Done (change `space-the-main-menu`): the main menu's row advance trunc(70*row_h), its top trunc(height*origin)+index*advance and the top<=y<top+band hit. The live row_h is 1.0 at 1600x1080 and the retail screenshot confirms ~70 px rows; the rule producing row_h remains open and no resolution formula was guessed. TMainMenu 38%. The draw, constructor, global back router and settings/manual rows remain.
Done (change `model-the-settings-page`): the Settings and Controls pages' exact rows, values, bindings, tabs, row tops, columns and footer buttons from the retail 1600x1080 screenshots, with a linearly scaled 630x800 layout. No coverage change (screenshot-derived); the embedded control-template records remain for an exact pass.
Done (change `route-the-motile-dispatch`): the unsigned 0x40 shock window, the posture/clip split and lying-still gate, the attack arm curve with the size/clock/stab/second-arm rules, the arm floor, parry ramp, gait clamp, auto-parry deadline, switch phase and get-up scale. TMotileDynamics 30%. The animation calls, main draw, limb pull and remaining branches remain.
Done (change `steer-the-main-menu`): every row kind's action, a per-page selection clamped to the row range and activation that returns None for a disabled row. TMainMenu 40%. The draw, the row_h scale rule and the settings/manual rows remain.
Done (change `correct-the-arena-rolls`): the arena rank roll now rounds to nearest-even as the original's rank roll converts it (`cvtsd2si`) (Elimination at slot 3, r 0.5 is rank 3, not 2), and the 19 company profiles are stored in memory order (the three profile bytes; Fitten Gang is 42 8C 01). No coverage change; a correction to existing credit.
Done (change `complete-the-settings-rows`): the Settings page's Music toggle and the Controls page's full 22 bindings (the eight lower rows scroll below the screenshot's first page), with the template defaults for the exposed rows. No coverage change; the page remains screenshot-derived, with the exact record/anchor pass still queued.
Done (change `lay-out-the-settings-records`): the 0x68-byte control records (class, id, x/y, w/h, text, style, state), the class skin sizes, the y-anchor deltas and x gaps, the previous-row place resolver, the 9 tab/button, 27 settings and 22 binding records, and the 434x536 unit-4 form with its 408/416/676 lists. TGUIForm 15%. The settings/controls layout no longer depends on the screenshot. The scroll-wheel step, tab page bodies and apply/reset logic remain.
Done (change `roll-the-arena-board`): MatchInstance and roll_instance with the MT unit rank, nearest-even rounding, Challenger window and LCG weapon row; the six-row daily board with caps/min-slots/rejects; the tournament board with the eight-index weekly prize history; and the 19-company/16-slot shuffle on the same LCG. XA_Arena 24%. The MT state/seeding, roster/entrant flow, opponent spawn and live board UI remain.
Done (change `fill-the-arena-roster`): the 0x30 roster rows and 0x14 entrants, the 8-byte opponent spawn profile, the 0xCCC unfit filter, the 2..6 round-id assignment, and the tournament resolution (3+ no-tie champion, company id+2, week flags, (day%28)%4 season set, championships/days/seasons). XA_Arena 28%. The MT19937 seeding, live board UI, actor spawn/body calls and shop remain.
Done (change `switch-the-settings-pages`): page 0 shows Settings at (0,64,426,408), page 1 shows Controls at y 56 (widescreen) or 96 with height 472−y, and the 412/676 controls scrollbar has a 232-px thumb at 17+150·f, all scaled by unit/4. TGUIForm 16%. The wheel handler, sub-tab bodies and apply/reset remain.
Done (change `route-the-motile-states`): the dispatcher's exact route order — 0x800 skip, 0x40 shock, posture split and its get-up/lying/upright/stagger/fall leaves, then the attack/weak/kick/parry/parry-end/power/gait/switch/standing clip branches. TMotileDynamics 32%. The branch bodies, animation calls and limb pull remain.
Done (change `seed-the-arena-ranks`): the standard MT19937 generator (state, twist, tempering), the 2^-32 unit draw and roll_instance_mt, which consumes the stream only when a template needs a rank roll. XA_Arena 29%. The arena MT seeding and the shared-stream consumers remain.
Done (change `scale-the-main-menu`): the live 632x453 client reads row_h 0.375 and 1080 reads 1.0, closing the rule as 0.5*round(H/135)/4; the row advance stays trunc(70*row_h). TMainMenu 42%. The draw, constructor, global back router and settings/manual rows remain.
Done (change `arm-the-opponents`): the tier weapon row, the 0-based variant pick and the company/generic spawn levels (60000/50000 + rand(9999)). XA_Arena 30%. The actor/body creation, condition and equipment costs remain.
Done (change `paint-the-main-menu`): the paint pass starts at trunc(H*origin)+line_height, advances by trunc(70*row_h), centres rows, greys disabled rows, brackets styles and reserves the selected row for a last draw with the unit/3 nudge. TMainMenu 44%. The sprite submission, constructor and global back router remain.
Done (change `align-the-control-labels`): the control-label horizontal switches (left/centre/right/after/default) and vertical switches (lift/centre/base/below/default) of the original's control-label alignment. RF_GUI_Controls_Ex 13%. Text-run drawing and control handlers remain.
Done (change `package-the-saves`): the retail save container (story/arena magics, sentinel and 24-byte directory, 0x2000-aligned 16-byte-named sections), the id/len/payload chunk framing and both 48-byte player-data headers. XA_SaveGames 12%. The scene exporters, DB blocks, checkpoints and file naming remain.
Done (change `track-the-combatant-head`): the head aims at the look-at minus the eye; under 50 cm it keeps the previous direction, in the 50–100 cm band it replaces the vertical with (10000 − d²)·1e-4, then yaw chases the engine's cheap xz atan2 and pitch the floored negative asin by at most π/100 a tick, the yaw held within ±π/4 of the body, and it publishes the direction, a 250 cm point and the flat unit dir. TCombatant 30%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (change `track-the-far-head`): the second head-tracking entry shares the bands, slew and yaw clamp but publishes a point 150 cm ahead and has no −0.6 pitch floor. TCombatant 33%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%.
Done (audit): the coverage ledger was reconciled with the tree: SG_Character_Create 32%, XA_SaveGames 7%, TThaumTree 23%, the TPathFinder row 58%, and the section rows for combat (14%), AI (24%), game modes (18%) and UI (16%) instead of 0 or 1%; every table row now agrees with its paragraph. No code change.
Done (change `cache-the-cube-pipelines`): the cube-shadow prepare caches its specialized pipeline id by the produced vertex-buffer layout and key, matching Bevy's own dedup, so a queued pipeline is not specialized twice. The dev-profile launch that previously panicked at bevy_render pipeline_cache.rs:323 now exits 0 with the NVIDIA adapter and a saved screenshot; the log and image are in target/verify/gpu-launch-dev.{log,png}. No coverage change.
Done (change `ease-and-tag-the-save-chunk`): the save settings chunk is the header 0xECADCA01, a small or large 12-byte tag and two dwords, read back with an old flag when one million is above the tag float; the save curve is (2x)² × 0.5 below 0.5 and 1 − 2(x − 1)² above. `XA_SaveGames` is 5%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `convert-the-save-records`): conversion needs mode 4, no pending work and either the legacy entry or a record read before tag 0xDBAC0D06; the record table writes the count and each record's kind and two qwords. `XA_SaveGames` is 6%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `format-the-save-number`): a save number is zero-padded to three characters, with "000" for zero and negatives and longer values left whole. `XA_SaveGames` is 7%. The published headline stays 14.4% and 5.7%. The fresh sum stays about 15.5%.
Done (change `fix-the-torch-shadow`): point lights now use the original's 3 cm shadow-map near plane instead of Bevy's 10 cm default, so the held torch's character shadow follows the head, torso and legs instead of smearing into a floor-wide blob; light ids ride above Bevy's default 0.08 depth bias in 1e-5 steps and decode with round((bias - 0.08) / 1e-5). No coverage change. Verified at `target/verify/shadow-near.png` against the old `shadow-fixed.png`, and by two final-tree launches (`gpu-launch-62/63`).
Done (change `warm-the-gi-block`): a rebuilt GI block now warms in a pending texture set while the block on screen stays bound, and swaps in only when its full warm-up has run; a level change drops both. The old path swapped a black block immediately, which showed hard black shadow patches and a soft circular GI halo for about 40 frames while the player moved. No coverage change. Tests: `a_rebuilt_block_waits_for_its_warm_up` and `the_first_block_is_used_while_it_warms`. `make check` and `openspec validate --all --strict` pass; two final-tree NVIDIA RTX 3090 launches loaded exanima01 in 5.1 s and 4.9 s and exited 0 (`gpu-launch-64/65`).
Verified: the whole workspace test suite also passes on the fedora-gpu worker (30 cores, RTX 4000) after two float assertions were made tolerant: the hardware reciprocal square root is implementation-defined, so the stagger target and the shell vertex normal are compared with a relative tolerance instead of bit for bit. That worker run covers every crate; the local suite is unchanged. Verified: an interactive `play` run with `--opponent --press W@40-120 --fixed-frames` logged the duellist entering combat (Defence, CombatIdle at 2.47 m) and a weapon hit on the player's bone 5 at tick 802, then saved its screenshot and exited 0; the log and image are in `target/verify/gpu-opponent.{log,png}`. Two earlier runs of the `play` profile on level exanima01 logged the NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 adapter (Vulkan, driver 615.78.08), a finished `level exanima01 ... loaded in 4.0s` and a saved screenshot, both exiting 0. Logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{1,2}.{log,png}`. Two further runs on the tree after the skill fades, the weapon blood list, the morph apply and the menu forward logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 3.7 s and 3.6 s, each exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{3,4}.{log,png}`. Two more runs on the tree after the morph basis solver and start logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 3.6 s each, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{5,6}.{log,png}`. Two further runs on the tree after the morph cook, skin stream, target diffusion and stream registration logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 3.6 s each, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{7,8}.{log,png}`. Two more runs on the tree after the path grid, A* and snap work logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 3.8 s and 3.6 s, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{9,10}.{log,png}`. A run of `play exanima02` loaded 6792 props, 2353 objects, 181 lights, a 677-cell grid and 21 characters in 3.7 s and exited 0; its 13 unresolved terrain layers are the documented shipped-data layers that name nothing in the palette and fall to node 0. A `character` view run on the NVIDIA adapter also exited 0. Two more runs on the tree after the path, coverage and UI re-rating work logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 4.2 s and 3.8 s, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{11,12}.{log,png}`. After the Kaiser mip chain was wired into texture loading, two runs logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 6.7 s and 6.2 s, exiting 0 (mip generation adds about 2.5 s to the load); logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{15,16}.{log,png}`. After filtering the base as bytes and parallelising both mip passes, two more runs loaded the level in 5.2 s and 4.9 s (the whole loading screen 20.4 s and 21.7 s) and exited 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{18,19}.{log,png}`. Two runs on the tree after every terrain material is layered logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 6.4 s and 6.8 s, with 153 shelled rooms and no truncated terrain tile, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{85,86}.{log,png}`; the dirt rooms before and after are in `target/verify/black/{zoom,zoom-fixed}.png`. Two runs on the tree after the cube faces follow a moved light and the grid look-ahead logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 6.0 s and 6.6 s, with 153 shelled rooms, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{83,84}.{log,png}`. Two runs on the tree after the frame profiler logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 6.7 s and 5.6 s with `--profiler`, with 153 shelled rooms, exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{75,76}.{log,png}`. Two runs on the tree after the map panel logged the same adapter and a finished `level exanima01` in 6.1 s and 6.4 s, with 153 shelled rooms and the panel open on 29 levels (scripted F9), exiting 0; logs and PNGs are in `target/verify/gpu-launch-{73,74}.{log,png}`. Done (changes `write-a-chunk-tree`, `keep-character-subtrees-out-of-the-export`): `exa_formats::rfc_write` writes a scene as the original's level export does, every chunk in the original's order; an empty scene is CF AF 23 3D. After two scene nodes the first prop takes number 3; a character's subtree is not walked; materials are collected lowercased against the shared list (`Brick01.dds` becomes `Brick01_rfi`, kind 0x40); a mesh switches to u32 indices at 65535 vertices; a keyless track stores last index 0xFFFFFFFF. RFE_Export_RFC2 75%. Not in: building an export from the live world and a save package that carries it.
Done (change `earn-discovery-and-stock-the-shop`): `exa_game::experience` has XA_ExperienceSys's rules (item-kind discovery, kill points by rank, explored cells, the global, player and companion conversions, the fighting estimate, story music choice), checked against disassembly, but no hook runs in a game yet; `exa_game::trade` and `exa_formats::trade_record` have SG_TradeSystem's stock update, weights, pick and coin settle, but nothing calls `Company::restock` or `Company::settle` and the shop window, its GUI and the inventory side are missing. XA_ExperienceSys 3% → 70%, SG_TradeSystem 0% → 60%. Review correction: the engine's small round helper used by exploration sets MXCSR toward −∞, so it is a floor; TSimSector, TGridEnvManager, TMotileAI, RF_SceneNodes, TStaticTerrain and TVXGIManager call it too, and their ports should be checked for round-to-nearest.

Done (change `run-the-role-scripts`): role records and dialogue graphs (role entries and `narrator.rcd`) are read in full, and scripts run as the original walks them, quirks included (the CRLF else trick, the byte after `)` consumed), with the Actor and Role classes and all nine globals (`exa_script`). Topic memory follows the cooldown table, activation hits, delays and windows with the shifted memory load. The conversation engine searches dialogues role by role both ways, starts entry nodes through the two calm filters, bands moods at 70/30/−30, speaks a random line per speaker at half-bubble pacing, follows links, runs event nodes, lists replies and topics with [Other], and runs the narrator; bubbles last 1000 ms + 50 ms a byte. `ScriptPlugin` loads the data and steps the engine on the virtual clock. Review fix: AddConcern and the mood's distance test compare as the original (NaN cases). Not in: drawing bubbles and the reply panel, any live trigger (nothing sends Narrate/Talk/PickReply or uses objects), the AI model behind the cast (it keeps a stand-in role list beside `exa_ai::roles`), saving topic memory.

Done (changes `run-the-thaum-effects`, `time-the-arc-shocks`): the Force, Energy and Displacement casts follow the original (state gates, persistent slots, focus prices with the 1.2 and 0.8 factors in double, the AI advice words, the barricade and dome places, the throw pick, the shock charge, the phasing streak and its 500 ms restore), and three effects run in the game through `ThaumEffectsPlugin`: the electric arcs (grow, branch, trade, jitter, shock a struck character once the clock is within 150 ms of its next shock time, then 500 ms plus a draw under 200), the force barricade (1.6 s rise, ripples, shoves through `Ragdoll::push_sphere`, knockback, the energy lowered per part struck, 750 ms fall) and the displacement wisps, drawn with an additive stand-in (`GlowMaterial`) and shown by `--debug-arcs`, `--debug-barricade`, `--debug-wisps`. Not in: a player or AI cast path, the effect shaders and noise texture, lights and sounds, the rest of the shock reaction, the barricade's navigation stamp, the displacement render passes.

Done (change `run-the-power-classes`; powers lane): the active power classes behind the thaumaturgy casts (`exa_game::thaum::power`), reviewed against the disassembly: the base power's reserve-held focus, two-tick end and HUD word; Mind (Sense Minds and Mask Mind in both forms, Confusion, Enrage, Trust, Suspicion, Loyalty, Command Dead, Control Mind and the possession marker, Spirit Form with its spirit body); Force (Shell, the bubble and barricade powers, Strikes, Kinesis steering and pick, Throw's lift, steering and coast); Energy (Shock and Shock Weapons); Displacement (the shared vanish phases, Vanish, the Long Door, Constant Displacement's hum and the Summon); and the unit routines (known-power search, AI dispatch, saved-power rebuild and Power Glean). The classes average about 58% of their bytes. At integration the vanish phases hide and show through the vanish effect node (`effects::vanish`, `VanishStep::apply`) instead of a second copy of it. They are plain tested functions with nothing calling them yet: next are a power bar or cast dispatcher that creates them from `ForceStart`/`EnergyStart`/`DisplacementStart`, `exa_combat` reading the shell damping and strike factor, and then the kinesis aim sweep, the player/body swaps, the spirit and demon spawns and the effect creators.

Done (change `port-the-mind-casts-and-force-streams`; thaummind lane): the Mind casts were re-ported from the instructions onto the shared cast body and power classes (`exa_game::mind::casts`): the front end, the Control Mind lock, the 0.3 gate in double, kinds 1–2 living and 4–5 undead, Calm, the timed effects, Trust, Suspicion, Loyalty, Control Mind, the dead powers with the fixed 0.2-decay revival, Sense, Mask and Spirit Form; the power rebuild restores saved Mind powers; Mind sight was rewritten (`exa_game::mind::sight`). The force blast and torrent simulations are ported (`exa_game::effects::{blast, torrent}`): the icosahedral directions, the aim within 45°, the charge, fizzle and thrown ticks, the torrent's spawn, pull, split and removal quirk, the alert, the contact arithmetic, the frame values, the geometry classes and the multi-point lights. The earlier port's errors (thrown chunks that never moved, wrong directions, caster-bound casts draining the pool, invented draw records) are gone. SG_Thaum_Mind about 80%, TForceBlastEffect about 54%, TForceTorrentEffect about 63%, the two force geometries about 56% and the two force emissives about 90%. Not in: the ribbon vertex builds, the engine contact tests, the GL passes and the noise, the player turn spring, and any caller (no power bar or AI cast starts them).

Done (change `correct-the-tendril-golem-and-shell-rules`; tendril lane): the tendril emitter, force barricade, force-shell aura, golem guardians and per-character item effects checked against the instructions: world entries/teardowns, shell rigid+skinned copies with fade plane and uniforms, golem wake/sleep/alarm/return rules, item-effect list with charge, hand dispatch, garment steps, focus slot are tested rules. The barricade's line is stamped on the nav grid penalty with the original's weights while it stands (exa_world::nav::stamp) and removed when it falls. Next: cast dispatcher spawning the force shell, AI host installing golem hooks and arming Alarm_ zones, character generator dressing bodies with item effects, the character collision query for arcs, drawing the electricity/force/shell programs in play.

Done (change `match-the-power-tree-and-force-looks`; thaumtree lane): the power tree reader (name/description indices, last-index text count), tree screen zoom/pan/hover/focus/press/slot/bar load-store-cast-toggle, grant routine + learning gates, default pairs, key handler, running-power limit, veil, Force creators' constants/sounds/noise/scroll passes match the disassembly; tree canvas drawn by software rasteriser running six fragment programs, wired to P/C keys, GUI pointer and use key (stand-in power body). TThaumTree ~42%, Force ~47%, DIsp ~45%. Not wired: grant/key callers, veil, Force geometry/GL, bar flags/animations, tooltip layout, KnownPower duplication; next: cast dispatcher creating power classes from the bar.

Done (changes `build-the-gui-widgets`, `rescale-the-forms`): the original's GUI widgets in `exa_ui`: the six GUI fonts with their recorded advances, the GUI levels (unit 3/4/5/6/8/12, font pairs, icon cells) followed by `GuiLevelState`, which rebuilds both icon atlases and the twelve glyphs on a scale change; text wrapping with the tcol/scrp/algn/rimg/{% %}/inpt markup; the text box editing as the original (\r\n as one, Right stopping between them, Ctrl+C/X/V, line-snapped eased scroll); the icon atlas packing, growing and drawing, never finding a negative id; the table view, the item view (grid, list, columns, rename, 600 ms type-ahead) and the seven basic controls (the trackbar's stick read as a float, summed in double); form rescale by the original's anchor and 16-bit rules, the message box layout and the GlphCommon order. Not in: forms that use the widgets, the GL draw bodies, GUISymbl/GlphCommon loading, the image resample, the single-line edit field.

Done (change `reproduce-the-save-slots`): the save package is written as the original's directory writer places it (the gap scan over every entry pair with the sentinel, moving a short section, the written entry's offset from the one before), opened as the original opens it, with the 52-byte story and arena headers, the character placement (reference and database bit, position, heading, condition, sub-chunks), masked load dispatch, the journal and the world database sections. `exa_game::slots` has the slot rules: names and numbers, the scan's keep/skip/delete per mode, the most-recently-used order in `Exanima.set`, new slots, deletion, loads with the checkpoint copied back, checked and unchecked saves, checkpoints, permadeath, legacy removal and the transition save; the clock runs on a signed tick difference and death is health widened to double against 0.01. Exarust's session save is now a package (no more `EXRS`), and saving over a retail save keeps its placement, sections and the chunks Exarust does not write; two retail saves rebuild byte for byte. Not in: the scene export, the world databases' contents, level sections and their import, followers, spawn and portal placement and the menu scene.

Done (change `review-the-save-package-and-load-flow`; savegames2 lane): `exa_formats::save_package` holds the package as the original does (directory, put, delete, open, untouched bytes); the story and arena headers and the chunk readers follow the signature rules, the load mask and the old-arena conversion; the writers emit chunks in the original's order (the arena's: journal, arsenal, stash, shop stock, company, extra, employees, merchant, hireables, tournament); `exa_game::slots` follows the scan, load, death, checkpoint and transition flows; and `exa-tool saves <file>` checks read-only that a real save rebuilds byte for byte. XA_SaveGames about 42%. At integration (batch 13) `exa-tool saves` read the user's own story save (exanima01, clock 8684 s) and arena save (arenahub, data version 9) without writing them: both parse, and the package and the player data rebuild byte for byte. Not in: wiring the slots into the running game, the level and world-database imports, the scene writer, the old-format merchant and tournament reads, the arena id pass, item classification, hireable regeneration and loading a retail save into a session. `read_package` needs Exarust's own marker chunk (a documented guess).

Done (change `complete-the-character-creator`): `exa_game::create` and `exa_ui::creation` hold the character screen shared by story, arena recruiting and versus: the mode's gear filter, the story background presets and their settings keys, the template a named background wears, the entry plan per screen mode, starting skills, the experience budget with its training-skill leftover and worth, the starting kit's seven tables, the voice prefix, variant draw, pitch and preview throttle, both pages' captions and panels, the power-tree commit, the commits per mode and the continue screen's save or checkpoint chooser. The creator writes its hand and worn items with their flags into the merged character record. Not in: drawing the form, the model preview, voice playback, the name box, the dialogs and wiring the screen into the game.

Done (change `run-the-arena-match-board`): `exa_ui::match_board` copies one match record per tab and builds the lineup from it (floor rank, side size, the Captain event's two-rank leeway, flag 1 lifting the one-leader and marked-fighter rules, flag 4's tournament weapon and its check); the six standard matches come from the original's table and the standard tab repacks the fee and prize scaled by the rank plus one; double clicks and drops move fighters with the original's room, bench, swap and marked-swap rules; the daily events, tournament winners and status line, season rows, "Entered n/m" label, ENTER rules, layout, paint order and hit test follow the original's tab switch. `exa_game::arena_board` fills a board from a career and the generated daily board. Not in: the form template's own rectangles, the item and coin icons behind the cells, the bracket and match start, and a Bevy plugin that draws the paint list.

Done (change `reproduce-the-draw-skin`): `exa_ui::skin` is the original's whole base draw skin (`TGUIDrawSkin`, 73 routines read at the instruction level): the scheme's four colours expanded into twelve shades each with the accent, its mixes, the hot group and the two alpha ramps; the layout metrics; an immediate-mode batch (program selection that binds once, blend word, ink, texture offset, quads, lines and triangles in one shared vertex list); the outlines, gradients, striped bars, selection bands, rounded fills and frames, bevel and shadow rings and scroll arrows; the control parts, form marks and icons, form chrome, default background, the page's clip, scroll and draw order and the label placements, inks, glow and fade; the skin's programs in the original's order. Not in: submitting the batch to a render pass (menus and the HUD keep their present painters, so nothing on screen changes), the textured subclasses (`guiskin.rfi`, the HUD's stone skin), rasterising text and icons.

Done (change `finish-the-line-skin`; gslines lane): the line skin (TGSLines) has one model per method. A first pass had duplicated the dispatcher and five helpers that `exa_ui::skin` already models and had broken the parent's centred icon and the fifth unit length; the copies are gone and those two are restored. `exa_ui::line_skin` holds only the missing methods, checked against the assembly: the unit lengths, the line skin's own centred icon (shades 4, 8, 7 and 6, opaque or with the alpha raised by 0x40), the scroll refresh (no bit 2; a NaN slide is 0), the progress strip, the compact skill screen's integer span bar and the maximised window frame. `dispatch_form` now draws the frame and the strip; its close-edge tints follow the tab quads and `hit_line` falls through to the strip test. At unit 5 a 100-wide window with a 40-wide title splits its strip at 17, and a maximised 100 by 80 window draws eight quads. TGSLines about 85% (was 60%). Not re-verified: the slider and scroll bar, and `dispatch_form` records the close square twice. Nothing is drawn in the running game yet.

Done (change `reproduce-the-arena-and-teleporter-forms`): the rules of six forms that had no counterpart (77 routines, 30 743 bytes), read from the original's instructions. The technique selector (`exa_ui::technique_selector`, bridged by `exa_game::techniques`) picks and forgets techniques on the record's six skills, replaces the technique in training, refunds into it and marks the tree's slots by the original's ranges; the compact skill screen of character creation (`compact_skill`) has its metrics, hit word, keys, wheel, pips and nodes; the arena loadout editor (`loadout`, with `career::Member::loadouts` holding a rank's kit as the record's own loadout) its layout, cost and equip rules and close; the divination reading (`div_card`) deals, eases, turns and shadows its three cards; the emblem editor (`emblem_form`) starts on the original's palette and its randomiser keeps the colours' bytes. The teleporter's destination screen (`selection`, `exa_game::destination`) places and reveals its rows with the engine's sine and cosine, and a click runs the journey: the bubble above the teleporter, the teleporter's glow, the small crystal's charge, `TravelToLevel` when the resting bubble shrinks and, for the adapter, the single-row mark and the save request. Not in: drawing any of the forms, the controller action layer, the loadout's drop handler against the arena stock, the level change and save the journey asks for, the bubble's GI light, the emblem shader's pictures.

Done (change `rule-the-skill-screen-and-extended-controls`): `exa_ui::skill` decides which techniques may be learnt (the learning row's requirement mask from the screen's node table and the record's prerequisite table, both prerequisites funded), a ring node's press state, the screen's own unlearning with its committed points, the learning commit, the experience pass (through the experience system's two conversions) and the ready flag; the ring, satellites and link search put the engine's sine across and the cosine down, and the group icons, orbit bars, spokes, tooltips, learning row, forget dialog, popup fade and screen fades are modelled. `exa_ui::controls` has each extended control's message rules (text field, combo box, colour swatch, spinner, progress bar, separator, image and texture views, caption, trackbar, scroll bar), with the unordered clamps that let a NaN through and `CVTSS2SI`'s indefinite result. Not in: the screen's draw list, shaders, reveal sound and close, the drop-down list form, the spinner's drag capture, the colour picker, and a running form that mounts the screen or the controls.

Done (change `correct-the-gui-core`): the GUI core's window manager (`exa_ui::gui_core`) follows the original's task: the focus and its tooltip request after 400 clock units, the modal form, the pointer's drag threshold and capture, double clicks within 450 units and one pixel, the press and release dispatch, the drop hand-over, the level switch's lengths, the clamp and the toggle group. The text routines (`gui_text`: ellipsis fit, truncated advance, width fit, wrapped glyphs hanging from the line top, the menu font record), the single-line editor (`text_edit`: caret walk, refused byte, copy and cut of an unselected text, the draw's three paths), the 3D viewport control (`viewport`: jitter scales, still and progressive samples, the frame-rate readout, cursor actions), the control records' append and two-pass layout (`gui_controls`) and the main menu's hit band, paint, commands by game mode and session state and practice page (`exa_game::menu_pages`) are corrected against the instructions. The running F1 menu screen (`exa_game::menu_screen`) now routes its pointer through the window manager: it selects under the pointer and runs a row on the release over it. At integration F1 panicked on a duplicate component in the canvas's spawn (fixed in 6d78e9a1); on the RTX 3090 `--press F1@200` in `play exanima01` then opens the title page over the level (`target/verify/gpu-launch-97-menu.{log,png}`; the rows are placed by the menu font's widths but drawn in the base font, so they do not line up yet). Not in: drawing the glyph quads, edit field and menu rows (the running menu still uses the base font), the forms unit's own classes, the tooltip form, the gamepad focus test and the system clipboard.

Done (change `correct-the-form-base-and-game-gui`; gamegui lane): the form base class and the in-game GUI unit were re-read against the instruction listings and the first pass corrected. `exa_ui::form`: sibling layers by attribute word, the active test, unset places, maximise and restore, the runtime resize, scrollbar, wheel and drag rounding, the hit test with the client first, the fade, snap slots and routes. `exa_ui::game_gui`: the inventory and loot tags unswapped, owner-dependent placement, the journal, the counter control, the power slot, the status marker, the ready alert, the curtain, the compass, the heartbeat fade, the menu cursor, the HUD order and the save and close scopes. In the game I and J open the inventory and journal tasks (`exa_game::gui_screens`) and Escape closes the focused one; the screens are not drawn yet, and K no longer does anything. TGUIForm about 76%, SG_GameGUI about 60%. At integration the launcher's press test was corrected to accept `Esc`, which the branch had made a valid press name.

Done (arena lane, wave 3: lane 6's eight arena changes, `manage-the-company-windows` and `settle-the-tournament-on-the-return`): an arena match runs from its draw to the return (draw, tracks, beds, rounds and lives, fee; the tick, the falls at −0.15/0.01 in double, round end, continue, forfeit, the return's counters and 13 records); the company is staffed (hire, recruit, remove, dismiss, the hire list's draw and renewal) and writes its daily report; matches are laid out as their templates do and a tournament's teams placed on the bracket ring; the arena's save chunks (company, day, tournament, hire) are read and written; the hub plays its music and routes its clicks, and the board's Begin and the end of the day are gated. The company windows' rules are in: the manage refresh (the 8 and 10 gates, labels, name colours), the report click, the roster drag with its id remap, the arsenal form (rows, steps, commit, pages, the rank slider and points line, the event lineup), the sale from the arsenal with the holders' list-length quirk, the lesson order, the tournament start (bracket, the ×6 unentered pass, forfeit, rematch lineup) and, on a day divisible by 7, the return's second settle at ×5 (rivals 2..6). Not in: a hub screen that draws and wires the windows, spawning actors for matches, the template spawners beyond their layouts, the record viewer, loadout cost and outfit copy, the employees and merchant save chunks, `career::Company` folded onto `staff::Staff` (so `end_day` still teaches only combatants); only the play-mode match tick and the arena music run live.

Done (change `share-the-roles-and-script-events`; dialogue2 lane, M8): the character role list is shared (`exa_formats::roles::HeldRole`, wrapped by `exa_ai::roles` and `exa_script::cast`; the role weight's share in double precision), and role init scripts run through `exa_script`'s interpreter; a corpus test confirms every shipped init script gives the activities it did before. The review checked the lane's rule changes against the assembly and reverted most of them: mood NaN handling (ordered compares give the neutral band), the memory load shift (node 0 gets the stored count), near and witness event filing (the contact holds the target; the witness files entry = target, key = role), the role-to-role search key, StartDialogue's gate (any active conversation's actor slots, whatever its state) and the floor rounding (Trust, Concern and narrator durations round to nearest even). It kept or added the StartDialogue actor requests, the event node's actor count and its role-set reports (held roles checked against the database), the InZone marker test (3D squared distances) with 64-bit id compares, the WitnessRole range filter, the plain narrator message, the object script's one actor slot and the drain's charge-flag rule. Not in: the AI filling the cast and contacts, the game consuming `ScriptEffect` (the GameEvent narrations, inventory, checkpoints), the level walks and line of sight for WitnessRole and InZone, the relation half of the role-set check, and drawing the bubbles and the reply panel.

Done (change `run-the-game-flow-and-splash`; gamemain lane): `exa_game::flow` models the game-main flow: the mode switch, the story and practice starts with the engine's angle rows and lights, continues, the near and far camera shots and cursor warps, the story, practice and knock-out hooks, the death and end menus, the level-exit save with the checkpoint rule, the respawn at exanima05's portal, the quit autosave, the exit-trigger nudge, the practice scene filter, the music choices, the unlock words and the save managers; `exa_game::game_event` holds the story game events; `exa_game::splash` runs the splash screen's 11-state machine on the frame time and the stamp clock, behind `exarust --splash`. SG_GameMain about 45%, TSplashScreen about 72%. Not in: applying the flow's steps (FlowPlugin only logs them), the transition screen, the splash's sound and menu theme playback, scripted modes and the versus lobby. The per-mode flag words and the story spawn record's float are guesses.

Done (change `reproduce-the-arena-staff-and-outfits`, new spec `arena-staff-outfits`; arena lane): the arena's staff and outfit rules. `exa_game::arena_ids` prepares and remaps the saved item ids (the id preparation order and the two post-load remaps) and holds the one fighter-rank rule; `exa_formats::arena_save` frames the employee, hireable and merchant chunks. `exa_game::arena_outfit` and `arena_outfit_data` hold the profession grids (four rows), the weighted picks, the row fillers, the level grants and the random-NPC and outfit generators (by profile and by level) in the original's draw order with double-precision sums; `exa_game::arena_spawn` holds the beast, hire and floor-probe plans as data. `manage` and `loadout` hold the roster text and ink, the health fill, the upkeep, the loadout form's bind, open, reload and refresh rules and the shared `career::hire_fee`; `manage_icons` holds the icon atlas (2 wide, 4 tall). `arena` and `tournament` gain the saved-special restore, the weapon-row check, the prize copy and the first-undecided settle. XA_Arena about 50% → 65%, the save and rank helpers 0% → 85%. Not in: the body spawner and the item-fact pass, the form windows, the hub's stop-ambience flag, the icon handle per control, the final armour mode in the max-rank path, the world-state family test and the worn-float carry-over. Nothing new runs in the game yet (only the hub music pick); the spawn plans have no caller.

Done (change `reproduce-the-gui-tail`; guitail lane): the original's remaining GUI classes now have reviewed, tested Rust models: task layer (exa_ui::gui_task), colour picker (colour_picker, engine RGB<->HSV helpers), combo popup (drop_down), palette swatch (col_swatch, lifted palette), creator's tone and hair pickers (pickers, start-up tone bytes, human_skin::tone_linear), skill atlases (skill_gui, SkillScreen page/attach rules), manual and one-time tips (help_system), item view drop/rename slots, icon manager tail, trade window (exa_game::shop_window), chase camera wall probe. None of the new forms are drawn or wired yet; the wall probe runs without an answerer (needs the level-mesh flag filter); the hair picker's shading goes through `hair::pick`; the trade window needs its panels and Trade button connected to the arena hub.

Done (change `port-the-character-extras`; charextra lane): the AI action executor is the existing exa_combat::attack::execute with its main-side start sound (pitch then gain, double). Electric shock and force release follow the original (150 ms window, burn leaves a kind-2 stagger step, pain reaction). Race voice names use the first template's flags; a missing race takes the executable's default record. Local/world character databases (lazy shared records, tag routing, add/release/payload/flush/save/load) serve the save-slot readers. Creation form preview frame/layout/drop routing, morph registration helpers (target ordinal, rotated control match, nearest records), motion fallback/child sort, CPU vertex skin modelled. Not wired: shock, start sound, voices, DB writes, creation form, morph helpers.

Done (change `check-the-core-helpers-against-the-assembly`; coretail lane): the core helpers were checked against the assembly. exa_physics::engine_math is the single engine-maths implementation (UI forwards; body twist uses RSQRTSS look basis). exa_formats::text has the original's formatting/parsing; FPS readout keeps zeros. Save packages open without writing. Prelude tips, actor template reader, practice opponent (traits, team 0x10, 3 s) in; --opponent uses opponent01's template. Terrain build stops at eleven brushes. Object manager and randomiser rules exist as functions, unwired except the modelless-operative placeholder (need live object DB + loader per-record material rules). Next: tips form, uncached actor reader, practice spot, terrain material record + mask packing, fold remaining sin_cos/fast_ln copies into engine_math.

Done (change `match-the-material-factory-rules`; materialfix lane): the running material path follows the original's loader and factory: the late opaque rewrite reads the loaded class and flags, premultiply, emissive doubling and map binding follow the plug-in-masked feature word, the shadow-cast rules match, and the compares are in double. Visible: transparent records with opacity above 0.99 and refraction records draw opaque, a lit one-source diffuse is darkened by its opacity, unlit records get no diffuse map, emissive doubling applies only with an emissive map, refraction and alpha-tested records cast shadows and additive ones do not. Open: the shared diffuse/opacity colour slot, the texture-load-dependent opacity flag, the unwired factory tables.

Done (change `reproduce-the-render-tail`; rendertail lane): the render tail's light flicker and fade, the light-body flicker words, `cube_image` through the codec, node parts and the terrain block slab bounds are wired; the shadow-bias scales, the factory tables, the image copy/create/convert routines, the smoke mesh, the 2-D batcher and the UV matrix exist as reviewed functions without callers.

Done (change `trace-texture-gui-and-render-rules`; texgui lane, Phase 5): the blue-first and ZYX channel orders, DXT1a, ALPHATC, LATC and DXT5n decode as the original's converters do; the per-unit texture bind cache, the render list choice, the draw queue, the still capture, the planar vertex layouts, the post fades, the GUI blend cache, the skin globals, the textured skin and the table view corrections are modelled. Only the decoders are wired into the game.

Done (change `reproduce-the-data-tail`; datatail lane, Phase 1, registry under Phase 8): the data-tail helpers were reviewed and reproduced: the sorted id index, the locale table, the memory archive, the project paths, opening the Resource pack and the debug command registry (the shader reload and the hotkeys are wired; the model-subdivision command only logs). Library-only: the UID index, the memory archive, the stream thread, the weighted planes, the model release, the subdivision and the placeholder swap.

Done (change `run-the-parry-in-the-body-tick`; Phase 4 Combat): the parry rule family now runs in the body tick as the original's callers run it, and the blow stagger is stored on the body, so wiring completeness rose. Still unwired: the armed grip, the explicit target, the Fend entry, the request-an-attack clearing, the capable-flag forbids, the learned-skill deadline word, the continuation record, the part-check counter side effects, the Force-wall interrupt is unexercised, and the `exa_physics::body::stagger` helpers have no caller.

Done (change `wire-casting`; Phase 7 thaumaturgy): casts run in the game through `thaum::cast::CastPlugin`: bar keys reach the bar cast entry, the Force, Energy and Displacement front ends and the creators (shell, kinesis, throw, strikes, bubble, barricade, shock, shock weapons, vanish, long door, constant displacement, summon) run, and the body tick walks the active list. The invented known-power gate, the release-flag event and the stand-in power body are gone. Since then (PR #2 and its review) the blast, bolt, wave, torrent, barrier and bubble entities, the kinesis and throw targets, the throw's sounds, the HUD focus bar, the shell's combat damping and the AI chooser are wired. Not wired: the shell's aura entity, the shock burst, vanish and demon effect entities, the Mind casts, the saved record and grant, the queued-slot retry, and the other powers' sounds and draws. A duellist whose record knows powers now casts: its slots are answered by their domains' AI evaluators before its combat state runs, and the chooser's pick starts through the domain's front end as an AI caster (`exa_ai::DuelPowers`). A blast or bolt cast now makes its blast entity: it plays cast motion 0x22, takes the caster's cast hook (answering −1, so a later cast's start or the release flag fizzles it while it charges), charges in the right hand toward the AI's target's chest or the cursor, pays its drain each charging tick, ends the cast through the shared cast end when short of focus with a hook set (fizzling only while the hook is its own), clears the hook and power id itself when thrown (the cast motion playing on), and pushes the limbs its chunks reach (Exarust's own sphere test). A barrier cast now makes its dome entity: cast motion 0x23 with id 0x10205 and the hook that ends it, the cost paid a tick (half once standing) with a shortfall calling its hook and ending the cast through the shared cast end, and its shell pushing other bodies' limb spheres out and opening ripples. A bubble cast now makes its dome where it was cast: cast motion 0x24 with id 0x20207 and its hook, the hook and power id cleared when it stands (the cast motion playing on), a break and 700 ms shrink, once, when its power ends or its caster is gone, its caster held in and other bodies pushed out. A wave cast now runs its wave round the caster's body root as an entity (`thaum::wave_live`): cast motion 0x24 with id 0x204 on the blast's hook, the drain paid each charging tick (a shortfall ends the cast through the shared cast end and fizzles the wave while it holds the hook), the release clearing the hook and power id itself, and its thrown chunks pushing other bodies' limbs. A torrent cast now runs its torrent as an entity (`thaum::torrent_live`): cast motion 0x26 with id 0x10203 and a hook that fizzles or ends it, the drain paid while charging and half while running (a shortfall calls the hook and ends the cast through the shared cast end), its running points pushing other bodies' limbs, and its removal on the first frame that would build no triangles. Cast hooks now answer by the event's bits: the shell's raise installs its hook (0 to bits 0x29) and the bubble's answers 0 to bits 0x28, so neither lets another cast start (event 0x20) until the Force Raise clip ends; a motion replacing the cast motion, or a one-shot cast clip ending into the stance, asks the hook with event 4 and drops it on a negative answer, and looping cast clips loop. The shared cast end clears the hook and power id and, from exactly casting, returns the body to its stance over 30 frames in the same tick. A duellist's or placed character's caster now takes its body's technique word from the record's learnt techniques and its persistent slots from Concentration's Continuity tiers, as the original rebuilds them; the player's caster keeps technique 0 and one slot until its saved record is read. Each body tick now settles focus as the original does around its power walk (the reserve's change moves into the capacity, spent focus leaves the pool at a climbing share, and with nothing spent the pool regenerates by √capacity × 0.000301205 × 0.1), so the pool a duellist weighs is its settled focus, and the HUD's focus bar shows the player's pool and capacity. The shell's damping and Force Strikes' factor now reach the hit formula: each caster's fighter carries them (`exa_combat::PowerFactors`), so a shelled fighter takes half a blow's impact and a striking one deals 1.5 times it.

Done (review of PR #2, change `correct-pr2-rules`; Phases 3, 4, 6 and 7): PR #2 (a Cursor agent's 94 commits) and the review's fixes reproduce, checked against the original's instructions: the body tick's arm state machine in the original's order (a shock leaves the arms, a daze wins over a parry, the attack drive zeroed every tick, the parry read from last tick's flag), the clip clock's double add and its stance rate reset, the feral thorax hunch, the gait probe swayed by the body's speed, the standing lift leads, the moving pelvis spread and falling chest blend, the support pins applied in sequence, the sole turn, the stand-still stamps, the lying leg and foot release, the dormant countdown and keep, the in-combat retention, death clearing only the feet's strikes, the pair impact's hit-event gate and engine cosine, the limb impact sounds and gates, the shock stagger, readiness through a parry, the presence reset (a hidden duellist still decides but neither moves nor casts), the footwork's long-step latch from the chest and the fast-eased walk end, the elixir on the world clock (1800 s), the burn's crackle levels, and in thaumaturgy the live blast, wave, torrent, barrier and bubble, kinesis and throw targets, the shared cast end, the vanish hook, the player's hook asks and the duellists' casts. The invented bleeding damage over time (a copy of the burning routine fed by wounds) was removed. Open: the shock reaction's caller, the particle body-contact pass that feeds the burn, a shield body for the block's stamp, the world clock's saved base at load, the original's placed flag and group root for the pair gates, the player's game-view and status gates around the hook asks, the footwork landing's slow-turn extension, and the shell's aura, vanish and demon entities.

Done (change `draw-the-game-screens`; Phase 7 UI): the GUI draw path is wired (`exa_ui::gui_draw`, a CPU rasteriser over the 3-D view: ten-entry blend table, four skin programs, top-left fill, bottom-up rows) and the inventory and journal windows draw through their constructors' words, without a title bar. Next: the item renderer, the Rayform fonts if they are found, the control callbacks, the character view and preview bars, item icons, the tab strip, the journal list, typing, the loot window and the technique selector.

Done (change `model-the-retail-screens`; Phase 7 UI and Phase 4 item windows): about 61% of the 39 kB screens pack is modelled as tested records and rules: tooltips, the modal and transition screens, class select, dialogue choices, the pickers, popups, dialogs, help screen, task stack, game tips, colour swatch, item renderer, loot window, inventory window, arsenal and the script class and role models, with the save family, physique wait and emblem steps corrected.
