Nothing is painted or wired yet; the creator's 3-D preview and the running of role scripts are not in.

Still open:

- The other emblem controls. A candidate's listed price is the rank square plus four fifths of the gear. A swept season grants its four item ids, and day 134 repeats the first row. A shop line prices at 1, 12, 144 or 1440 copper, and a sale is a quarter of the basket. A tier multiplies a match stake by one more than the tier. A recruitment costs 12 copper. Overnight rest and a trainer's lesson are in.
- Story side levels, scripted encounters and their live triggers (the role interpreter and dialogue engine are in, `run-the-role-scripts`), map discovery.
- The other powers and their meshes. A blast charge, a barrier ripple, a wave front, a shell ripple, a shell vertex, a shell index list, a skinned section filter, a compacted vertex map, a section slot, an index room, a fade by the stored size of 800, a light that follows that fade, a second light pair, an index upload, a play draw that submits that upload, a torrent mix and a torrent stream are in. The Bevy render graph does not issue the original's GL calls.
- Character creation's form, model preview and voice playback, and wiring the creator, the match board and the save slots into the running game (their rules are in).
- Full UI using the original GUI skin, icons and manual pages.

**M7 "arena season", M8 "story act 1", M9 "full story".**

Done (change `review-save-unit-third-pass`; savegames3 lane): the save unit's scene presets (the eased day draw, sums in double, thunder from the engine's shared seed), the level transition's placement (followers rotated by the spawn matrix, the controller re-attached, scene-content lists), the scene character flush (worn items, both hand pairs, then carried items), the arena table writer (the merchant chunk skipped only when no employee is a merchant, the arsenal written shelf by shelf) and the settings-chunk hook on an exported level follow the instructions as tested functions. Not wired yet: a live level export/import bridge, an arena scene load calling the presets and the landing plan, and filling the arena tables from the live arsenal, staff and tournament. XA_SaveGames about 42% → 48%.

Done (change `match-the-game-flows-menus-and-story-gear`; gamemain2 lane, reviewed): the main menu's rows read their own save managers, the in-game page shows NEW GAME in practice and the arena pause row reads EXIT once the session is won or ended; a new story clears the player's roles and adds 7, 1 and the background's own (the unknown background carries item 0x10c with the thaumaturge's power word; the knight and proctor hold and carry item 0x1f); the get-up's and the revival's animator writes, the practice start, the boot order, the settings keys' kinds, session begin, continue and leave, back to the title with its title logo, the story end-menu exit, the level exit's fade bit and the unit's menu text shader are tested rules. Live in the game: only the page state and F1's hold of the dialogue and hook. Not wired: applying the flow's steps, drawing the end menu, the shader in the row painter, reloading the arena on a practice next fight. SG_GameMain about 45% → 50%.

Done (change `wire-the-arena-hub`; wire-arena-hub lane, reviewed): the arena hub runs in the game: entering `arenahub` draws its music, installs the click and fall hooks and places the manager; a dead manager ends the session and the slot is wiped. A non-drag click goes to the click hook (gate order, the 0x1a character ids, the 0xac0 condition test, the merchant nibble, the two object names compared on their stored bytes), which opens the match board, Manage Company or the trade window on a GUI task drawn through the GUI path; the board and Manage Company set and clear the hub's busy flag as the original does, and a board pick restarts the game on the drawn arena with `--arena-match`. On the RTX 3090 `play arenahub` loads with the manager standing and an arena duel still starts. Not yet: the notice boards and shop counter are static scenery with no node-aware collider, so clicks on them reach the level (a static collider to node map is needed); the ArenaMach side field; the shop's first stock; the launched match using the spawn plans; the session end and the return to the hub. XA_Arena about 67% → 69%.

Done (change `pick-static-scene-nodes`; wire-hub-pick lane, reviewed): the arena hub's notice boards and shop counter answer clicks in the running game. Each static collider remembers the scene node it was built under, and the hand's pick returns that node (`Hit::SceneNode`), whose stored name the hub hook compares, as the original's scene pick returns the node; every other consumer of the pick treats it as the level. A model's `_null` groups are pick-only colliders (the shop counter is only a `_null` box); whether the original's solver collides with `_null` faces is untraced, so collision is unchanged. `play arenahub` counts as arena mode for the hook. On the RTX 3090 a click on a notice board opens Manage Company or the match board and a click on the counter opens the trade window. Still open: the side field, the shop's first stock, the return from a match, the hub windows' close controls, pictures and items.

Done (change `end-the-arena-match-and-launch-from-the-hub`; arena3 lane, reviewed): the match's end is reproduced as tested rules in `arena_session`: the round transition (states 8/5/4, screens 3/2), the end screen's Continue (main menu, next round or bracket fight, settle and return with the saved placement), the forfeit guard, the next round's bodies (the reserve entrant meets the opponent who won; no spare), the dead-body pass (health widened to double), the return's health write-back, booking and tournament-day checks, the participant list and the hub arrival fixups. The hub's Begin now charges the entry fee (flooring the purse at 0), warns once on the board when the manager is entered, lets the first unhealthy specialist leave, and restarts into the match carrying the event's rank, gear tier and one drawn weapon per opponent. The hub placements, the roster gauge, the fighter panel, the quality markers and the preview modes are tested rules. Next: a scene body generator for arena fighters and hub NPCs, so the next round and the return to the hub run in game. XA_Arena about 69% → 74%.

Done (change `wire-the-save-level-bridge`; wire-save-level lane, reviewed): level loads apply the save unit's scene presets as the arena set-up decides: `arena1`–`7` and the story levels take the indoor preset (every shipped indoor level's lighting is unchanged), `arenao1`–`5` the clock-seeded outdoor preset, and only `arenao1`–`4` may draw a night. The world clock's saved base is restored at a session start (`exa_game::world_clock`) and `Session` can restore and stamp it; the arena tables are filled from and applied to the live company, staff and arsenal (`exa_game::arena_tables`) and are read back on load only when they write back byte for byte, so retail chunks survive a re-save. The level change is a tested plan (`exa_game::level_transition`) with a pending landing the play start honours. Not wired: a live save and load path, a live level export and import with an in-process level change, and the day record track (night, rain and cloud dimming are not drawn, so a drawn night on an outdoor arena renders lit under the moon's direction). XA_SaveGames about 48% → 55%.

Done (change `wire-the-game-flow`; wire-game-flow lane, reviewed): the game flow's steps now run in the game. The main menu is drawn through the GUI path with the unit's menu text shader on the selected row; the end menu is drawn over the game and its rows follow the original's end-menu routine (mode, then row, then menu: practice RESTART back to the menu form, returns to the title behind the transition). Resume, practice start, BEGIN (unknown background) and leave to the title are live, a story death opens the end menu and its exit returns to the title, and the splash plays BME Critters and the menu theme. The review fixed a NEXT FIGHT overlay the lane raised over every launched level (exanima01's corpses counted as practice deaths), kept the session begin from hiding the menu, and wrote the logo flag only on the return to the title. Next: an in-process level reload for practice next fight, teleporters and the story respawn; live saves; the world and camera steps; the arena session behind the arena end menu; the transition screen. SG_GameMain about 50% → 52%, TSplashScreen 72% → 74%.

Done (change `draw-speech-and-drain-crystals`; dialogue3 lane, reviewed): topic memory has the original's layout (a count word, then node n at slot n, with the shifted load from the stored count word). Speech bubbles are measured, placed and turned into draw batches (`exa_script::bubbles`, `exa_ui::speech`): wrap at 320, the narrator 75 % down, a character's head lifted 120 cm and projected, 4-unit margins, rounded boxes at alpha 128 drawn before white text at alpha 160. The reply panel measures its entries and animates its height (`exa_ui::dialogue_choices`); spoken lines reach both contacts as hearing stimuli; `ContextObj` names a weapon "a " plus its lowered name. Glow tasks and the crystal drain walks are tested functions (`exa_script::objects`). The review reverted the lane's calm-class-0 acceptance and its removal of the six-candidate cap, and fixed the hearing NaN compares, the glow's final zero write, `ContextObj`'s case and the projection's behind-camera NaN edges. Not wired: painting bubbles and the panel, AI triggers, glow writes reaching meshes and lights, a drain caller, topic-memory persistence. RFG_Dialogue about 68% → 76%, SG_GameFunctions 38% → 64%.

Done (change `wire-the-arena-bodies`; wire-arena-bodies lane, reviewed): an in-process scene body generator (`exa_game::scene_bodies`) builds arena opponents, the Beast, company entrants and the hub's shopkeeper and specialists from `arena_spawn` plans on the placed characters' spawn path. `arena_live` stands the first round, runs Continue (Enter or Space until the end screen is drawn), the next round (the winner stays; the reserve or a fresh opponent comes in) and the settle-and-return; `arena_handoff` carries the career across the match restart (an in-process level change is still blocked). The review fixed the generator parsing the object database every frame, a physics capsule left behind on despawn, a double turn on the return, and the hub entry never running under the play view's practice mode (so the manager was never placed and the shopkeeper never stood behind the counter, also on main). Next: generated records and outfits instead of actor templates, the hire forced outfit, the Beast body, the bracket's next fight, the end screen and forfeit button. XA_Arena about 74% → 76%.

Done (change `finish-the-creator-routines`; charcreate2 lane, reviewed): the character screen's remaining routines are modelled in `exa_game::create`: the preview icon list, material pass and dummy head, panel binding, the power tree's open, close and deferred close, the name warning and the reset-to-portal and delete dialogs, the continue page's character view and save steps, the icon sheet at eight GUI units, the creator form's opening and full health including the save seed. The review corrected the new-character mark (set only when the screen makes a record), the equipment page's back switch (only the first built form is hidden, then the versus forms, and the appearance panels return with tab words 0x110), the recruit-or-menu exit (`leave_creator`), the material loop (highest index + 1) and the continue view's flag-0x100 item refresh, and dropped the lane's duplicate underwear rule for `CharacterData::ensure_underwear`. Nothing is wired yet, since there is no creator screen. SG_Character_Create about 65% → 72%.

Done (change `draw-the-skill-screen-and-control-plans`; skillui2 lane, reviewed): `exa_ui::skill_draw` builds the skill screen's full draw list from its draw body (page-2 background, the 0.001 opening gate, dim, plates, textured spokes, node and group icons, orbit bars, ring tooltip, learning row and forget dialog) with the original's single- and double-precision sums, the engine's packed sin/cos and the exact inks; `skill_gui` holds both shader sources, the full 4×4 `MPrj` and the reveal sound; `controls` gains draw plans for every extended control plus the spinner's set word, drag capture and hot halves. Remaining: mount the skill screen and the extended controls in a Bevy form (`Hotkey::Skills`), feed the skin's grey, spoke ink and atlas handles, and add the glyph pass. TSkillUI about 70% → 82%, RF_GUI_Controls_Ex 60% → 78%.

Done (change `wire-the-arena-end`; wire-arena-end lane, reviewed): the arena's end screen draws through the GUI path (`NEXT FIGHT` / `EXIT;`, Enter or a pointer release is the only Continue), the pause page's FORFEIT settles the match, a tournament's Continue resolves the bracket and raises the next fight, and generic and hire-list opponents are built from their own generated records and outfits on the merged-mesh path, with the event weapon row travelling on the in-process launch. The review rebased it onto the in-process level change (no hand-off file), limited the next fight to the event's side count with the raised role flag (a duel event had become an 8-team free-for-all), gave hire opponents their −100/100/0 personality and added the `--arena-fell` debug flag; a GPU run checked the duel, the end screen and the return to the hub. Remaining: the Beast body item, saved company and specialist records, the rival company per team, the score board and round banner. XA_Arena about 76% → 79%.

Done (change `wire-the-level-reload`; wire-level-reload lane, reviewed): `exa_world::level_lifecycle` changes level in process: it tags every level-owned root, despawns those subtrees, empties the physics world (new capsules for survivors) and drops the GI, navigation, room, sky and fog state; the viewer then loads the destination through the staged `LevelLoad`, keeping the player entity, record and possessions, and the flow's `LoadLevel`, `TeardownWorld` and `PlacePlayer` steps are applied. The hub match launch and return no longer restart the process (the hand-off file is gone), and the practice next fight reloads the arena and runs the practice filter as the original's re-import does. The review paused the game clock while the new collision builds (the player used to fall to its death on every change) and added `--level-change` with per-level counts; on the RTX 3090 a round trip exanima01 → arenahub → arena1 → exanima01 returns to the same physics and asset counts and GPU memory. Next: level export and import into saves, followers, a respawn that rebuilds the player from its record, resetting the camera and torch on a change. SG_GameMain about 52% → 54%, XA_SaveGames 55% → 56%.

Done (change `draw-the-forms-through-their-shaders`; forms2 lane, reviewed): the arena loadout's 27 control records are decoded exactly from the executable, and the divination card, company emblem and destination symbol shaders run per fragment into images the GUI draw path shows. The review made turning cards use the engine's axis-angle matrix (the lane's was transposed) with the light dimmed by the turn and the turned shadow drawn, mirrored held faces back upright, kept the shaders' multiply order, coloured the destination symbol base + add·mix instead of a blend, and stopped the divination screen clearing other screens' frames. Remaining: an opener for readings, loading the symbol atlas, callers for the emblem preview and loadout controls, the loadout drop handler, the forms' own paint and the skill pickers' primitives. TLoadoutForm about 35% → 40%, TDivCardForm 80% → 86%, TEmblemForm 75% → 80%, TSelectionUI 80% → 85%.

Done (change `draw-the-compass-and-form-spans`; gamegui3 lane, reviewed): the HUD draws the compass from `compass.rfi` while the player carries it, in the original's frame (the camera's flat backward axis with x negated, RSQRTSS-normalised, crossed with up and shifted 3 px), upright and turning clockwise with the yaw; the form's client spans, set-controls plan, control passes, fade draw plan, held-record move and title drag, the horizontal scrollbar's fixed smallest thumb of 9, the counter's backs and shades, and the bar and gauge frames are tested rules. The review fixed a mirrored, backwards-turning compass, removed a duplicate curtain draw (the death fade already draws it), and corrected the ease order, the border/width mix-up in the spans, an invented thumb rule, the HUD clocks (signed 64-bit, not a 32-bit wrap) and the counter step. Remaining: the status marker and alert icons, the power bar on the HUD, frame dragging of live screens, the compass's interface fade. TGUIForm about 79% → 82%, SG_GameGUI 65% → 70%.

Done (change `review-save-unit-fourth-pass`; savegames4 lane, reviewed): the save unit has the three level-load entry points and the import order (GI reset; a terrain entry that was read counts as loaded), the game and menu scene set-up, the fighters' kit preparation and equip passes, the world character and object database imports, the old arena save's repair of the player record, the transition's ordered flow, the outdoor preset's view value and migration lists without duplicates, all checked against the instructions. The review corrected the record repair (the player record's worn, loadout and carried lists, not a manager list), the transition flow (the save-section path always lands, the re-attach pass always runs, the portal word takes the previous level id) and several mislabelled scene steps. Remaining: wiring it into the running game, the placement and world-database exports. XA_SaveGames about 56% → 62%.

Done (change `generate-the-arena-beast-and-rivals`; arena4 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the Beast spawns from its own Ogre record with its tier's hand item and worn pieces, a smoothed voice pitch and its experience grant; every generated body reseeds the shared sequence from the byte-reversed performance counter; a tournament side draws its event-row weapon inside the generation and wears the rival company the career's saved weekly field names for its team; the hub launch's hire level byte is the event's tier; and the random NPC's voice number, pitch and undead voice are corrected. Remaining: the company entrants' and hub specialists' saved records, the item instances' generated quality, the score board and round banner, the tournament week's board and reseed in the live hub. XA_Arena about 79% → 81%.

Done (change `draw-the-hud-status-marker`; gamegui4 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the HUD draws its power marker (live while the player knows powers) and skill-ready alert through the GUI draw path in halo-sized canvases, both from the skill atlas's first icon on the skin's rounded halo with one shared pulse clock, and the compass fades behind the open power tree; TGUIForm gained the close cascade, the maximise and restore steps and the control-table copy as tested models. Remaining: live frame dragging (needs the skin's frame hit test), the HUD power bar, the dialogue layer, a live skill record for the alert. SG_GameGUI about 70% → 76%, TGUIForm 82% → 85%.

Done (change `run-the-transition-screen`; gamemain3 pass by a Claude agent while the DeepSeek provider was down): the game flow's transitions run through the live transition screen: the action fires when the screen reaches black (600 ms, or 2000 ms with the exit fade bit's long hold) instead of after a fixed 100 ms that was really a GUI task priority; each caller's music-fade argument, the music, transition-bus and world fades and a black hold while a level change loads are applied, and the screen draws as a black form. Remaining: a respawn that rebuilds the player from its record, the camera, character and motion steps, live saves, the screen's close-time engine words and the "TO BE CONTINUED" hold. SG_GameMain about 54% → 57%, TTransitionScreen 75% → 82%.

Done (change `save-unit-fifth-pass`; savegames5 pass by a Claude agent): the save unit's writers cover every section the original writes (the world character and object databases with their count gates, the player's placement and the player data), the load side regenerates the arena's item lists, and the in-process level change records the transition flow. The running game still writes no save: it holds no live object or character database and its scenes have no export character. Next: keep the world databases as live resources, build the player's export character from the body, and save to a new slot only, so no existing save is overwritten. XA_SaveGames about 62% → 66%.

Done (change `build-the-hub-shops-first-stock`; arena5probe lane, DeepSeek, stalled after one commit, reviewed): the hub's machine side comes from the clicked node's world bounding centre, and the shop's and merchant's first stock is built when their shelf is empty from a live object-database world (each table entry's flags word is the kinds tested; copies are instance ids that remember their source; the merchant draws with its saved stock type and specialisation, which the merchant chunk now loads and writes), so clicking the hub's shop counter lists 18–20 named items. The review replaced the lane's section-tag walk with the table's flags word, used the merchant's own words and removed wrappers. Remaining: item bodies for the copies, basket moves, the trade window's icons and preview. SG_TradeSystem about 60% → 63%.

Done (change `add-hud-show-rule-and-control-messages`; gamegui5 lane, DeepSeek, stalled after one commit, reviewed): the HUD bars' show rule is one tested function the HUD draw calls every frame (it shows on a changed or NaN health, a strictly dropped focus, the power bar being open, combat mode, or an unsigned hold clock still ahead), and the gauge shows with a shield in combat mode; the counter's and bar control's message handlers, the inventory fill's page states and plan, the journal's list state, the technique selector's open plan and the deferred inventory fill (which retries on the GUI job queue until the generator has the node) are tested rules. The review fixed the lane's misreading of the power-bar flag as a combat word and its inverted queue answer, and removed duplicated or rule-less helpers. SG_GameGUI about 76% → 77%.

Done (change `return-items-to-the-loadout-arsenal`; forms3 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the loadout form's largest handler is the company stock's drop handler (taking an item back from the kit), not an equip as the lane ported it; the review ported it from the assembly (seven corrections: the gate count, a duplicated match release, the category test, the drag's sort, an inverted off-hand branch, the weapon branch, the real counts). Nothing opens the loadout screen yet.

Done (change `review-the-game-flows-start-and-respawn-rules`; gamemain4 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the story and practice starts record the original's start point, views and start record, the first wake-up ends on the player's animator word, the revive and story respawn follow the original's order, a NaN health takes the alive tip and the boot applies the settings store's unlock words; the review restored the exit nudge's sign test and removed a wrong controls-off step that left a respawned player without controls. SG_GameMain 57% → 58%.

Done (change `review-the-arena-return-and-draws`; arena6 lane, DeepSeek, reviewed): the return to the hub writes each company body's health back into the manager's and roster's records with the original's participant ids (the manager is 0, so a manager's death now resolves as in the original), a lesson crossing a rank copies the previous rank's kit, a rival week records the champion team's company, and the match experience award is reproduced as tested functions; the review corrected the scene draw's forfeit rule, the 64-bit weight conversions and the experience rounding, and reverted the lane's square health gauge and single-precision bracket angle. XA_Arena 81% → 82%.

Done (change `run-the-story-wake-and-combat-mode`; gamemain5 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): the game flow's combat-mode setter, the revive's order, the story respawn giving the controls back, the first wake-up ending when the body is up (the original's wake tick now runs) and the camera turning to each start's shot follow the original. SG_GameMain 58% → 60%.

Done (savegames8 lane, Claude, after the DeepSeek provider failed): saves now run in the game: a story or arena session saves to a new slot and an arena slot loads back as the original's full load does (tables, list regeneration with the item classification, clock, player reset), on the original's cached slot list; the scan's repeat rule was corrected. GPU runs wrote and reloaded both kinds of slot. XA_SaveGames 67% → 70%.

Done (change `score-the-arena-rounds-and-close-the-bracket`; arena7 lane, Claude): arena fighters keep the original's combat score and each round's experience is now credited live to the company's records, tournaments draw their winners from the scene and close the bracket as the original does, the roster shows its health gauges, and a slot load resets the arena stores first. XA_Arena 82% → 84%.

Done (change `build-the-hub-windows-from-their-tables`; arenawin lane, Claude): the arena hub's company windows are built from the original's control tables and wired to the arena logic (a draw-order bug that left them blank was fixed). XA_Arena 84% → 85%.

Done (change `run-the-character-creator`; charcreate5 lane, Claude): the original's character creator now runs from the main menu, with its forms read from the constructors, its preview on the creation floor and its commit starting the game with the created record (spawned with its physique, skin, hair and name). The background picker shows its four silhouette cards. Not yet: the saves' screen, the arena and versus commits.

Done (change `reproduce-the-dormant-gui-controls`; dormantgui lane, Claude): the editor-only popup menu, tree view and the game-object editor's handlers are reproduced from the assembly as tested types with no live caller, as the retail game never builds them. TGUITreeView and TGUIMenu 0% → 90%, the dormant object editor 0% → 40%.

Done (matchboard3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed; merged with the hub windows): the arena match board is drawn from its decoded template records like the other hub windows, with the original's day-end rule and actions; the review restored three lane errors. TMatchBoard 83% → 90%.

Done (change `continue-from-the-saves-screen`; savescreen lane, Claude): the menu's CONTINUE opens the original's saves' character screen and loads a story save into the running game with its player record, clock and position; story saves now hold the player and the flow's save steps write the slot. A same-frame double teardown that crashed the terrain build on load was fixed. XA_SaveGames 70% → 73%.

Round 38 intersection3: published light records and scene-node alias bookkeeping; the spread is a no-op until the room flood links sectors.

Round 38 mbfix: the match board and Manage Company draw tables at the table view's title and cell pens, built at the live unit; headers no longer overlap their first row.

Round 38 teleporters3: the reconstruction device's scan aura and reconstruction word; review added the missing identity write.

Done (change `read-technique-bits-and-the-trade-basket`; xptrade3 lane, DeepSeek with REA, reviewed): the experience unit's account write, level-start hook and hook order, the technique word on a level rise and the shop's basket transaction, quality share and stock binding follow the original; the review fixed a one-skill shift in the technique bits and reverted a signed reading of the power progress.

Round 39 arena7: the arena's weapon variant draw indexes the row's three slots as they stand, counting non-zero slots with the original's unsigned compare, a drawn empty slot meaning no weapon (live through the hire spawn); review corrected the lane's signed count.

Round 39 gamemain6: the expert ladder unlock write and the flow's motions through the unforced action switch; review reverted a wrong practice spawn move.

Round 39 savegames9: the save-disable flag, checkpoint save-then-copy and retail save restore live; review made the readers drop the header camera pair and kept the checkpoint copy under the disable flag.

Round 40 blastfx2: the force blast and torrent checked against the assembly; review replaced duplicate min/max helpers, restored the double texture walk and rebuilt the torrent ribbon as the original draws it.

Round 40 dialogue4: dialogue keys and the script cast mirror wired; review made scripts read red health and kept their role changes.

Round 42 arena8: the arena's day end, company records and live hire list; review moved the rolls onto the library's Mersenne twister, fixed the day-end order, sent match deaths to the career and packed the coin records in saves.

Round 42 dialogue: Talk starts a conversation, the reply panel and speech bubbles are painted and replies pick by click or digit; Derrin, the unknown background's companion, now stands in the first level so there is someone to talk to.

Round 42 fxwire: the force blast, torrent and teleporter bubble register their GI lights live (the blast lights the struck opponent blue on the GPU), and a blast or torrent hit writes the pin gain and sets the balance's landing momentum to 5; the effect frame's inverse transform was fixed (it applied the frame twice).

Round 43 guimain2: the GUI core's message boxes, double click and viewport rules, and the main menu's title art and cues live; review removed a duplicated helper, fixed the double click, the viewport precision and routes, and played the cues at the original's gain.

Round 43 savehealth: a story save now keeps the player's wounds (yellow, red, focus, heal pool, potion) and a load restores them, as the original's story writer does; new story characters start with focus 0.

Round 43 thaummind2: the Mind sight draw ported (no anchor creator yet); review dropped a duplicate force-light registration, fixed a transposed matrix and a reinvented sphere, and stopped an idle per-frame noise upload.

Round 44 sweep-modes: the loadout's equipped marks, the tip banner and the story experience write-back live; review fixed the loadout record states, the arsenal return and release and the card culling.

Round 44 sweep-thaum-a: the Barrier, force Bubble and barricade GI lights run live in their node frames; review restored the Bubble's own hum and fixed the electric opacity, flame wobble, spirit spawn height and wave gain, and removed invented or duplicated pieces.

Round 44 sweep-thaum-b (reviewed, change correct-the-thaumaturgy-sweep-b): sixteen effect components checked against the assembly; the shock burst, the Barrier and Bubble GI lights and the barricade hazard are live; the lane's invented vanish rule and duplicate classes were removed; its overlap with sweep-thaum-a was folded into a's GI and Hazard code at merge.

Round 44 sweep-ui-a: a UI sweep found most rules present; review fixed the lane's three changes (end pages' forfeit word, compact pip v range and node alpha order) and put the creation pickers on the form's client.

Round 44 sweep-ui-b: control draws reuse the existing skin ports and the destination screen's skin colours are live; review fixed nine wrong or duplicated rules (physique NaN, colour picker callbacks, edit-page preview, drop-down band, picture corners, choice list).

Round 46 auras (E3): the displacement shell and the teleporter scan aura draw; the shell only darkens, so a retail side-by-side would confirm its strength.

### Phase 8 — Polish and release

Performance (streaming, culling, GPU timings), settings, controller
support, Linux/Windows/macOS packaging that auto-detects Steam
installations, crash reporting, a modding hook (loose-file overrides that
take precedence over packs), regression tests based on recorded inputs.

Done (change `add-map-panel`): F9 opens a map panel in the level and play
views with the current map, live switches (point shadows, every room, the
navigation grid, the body overlay) and the installation's 29 levels;
choosing one restarts Exarust on it with the same flags, less those tied to
a place or a script. The mouse stays in the panel while it is open, and
Cursor Look no longer leaves the system pointer visible
([DEBUGGING.md](DEBUGGING.md), Map panel).

Done (change `add-frame-profiler`): `--profiler` (F10) and `--perf-log`
split each frame by main schedule, render thread and GPU pass; the
`trace-chrome`/`trace-tracy` features give a full timeline. Play mode in
exanima01 runs at 26–32 FPS, bound by Bevy's per-frame point-light shadow
views on the render thread and the body solver on the main thread; the
order of work is in [PERFORMANCE.md](PERFORMANCE.md).
Done (change `cache-character-shadows`): characters cast into the cached
cube maps, posed by their joints in the caster shader; each light keeps its
casters' face masks, a moved caster dirties only the faces it was and is
in, and Bevy's per-frame point shadow maps are off. The render thread in
exanima01's play mode fell from 28.9 ms to 7.6 ms a frame.
Done (change `step-bodies-in-parallel`): the body solver's per-body work
runs in parallel on the compute pool; bodies no pair touches run their 20
iterations and 8 friction passes at once, the others keep the original's
interleaving, with identical results. Play mode in exanima01: 34 → 51–54
FPS.
Done (change `relink-lights-by-cell`): a moved light is relinked only
against the casters filed in the 6 m cells it reaches, the wide ones and
those linked to it, as the original relinks a light against its sectors'
nodes. Play mode in exanima01: 68 FPS.
Done (change `follow-the-light-in-cube-faces`): a moved light works out its
casters' cube faces again, so the carried torch's shadows are no longer cut
at face edges. Done (change `look-ahead-on-the-grid`): the player's
look-ahead walks the navigation grid as the original's does and stops in
front of furniture; pushing into it had slowed play mode to 10–15 FPS.

Done (change `read-the-settings-files`, new spec `user-settings`): the launcher reads `Exanima.ini` and the `.set` store as the original does (the reader's split and trim rules, the 41 registrations, the setters' clamps and defaults, KeyDef parsing, the game's one-setting save, the store's get/set/integrity check and the exit save), on top of RF_DataIO's stream, folder table, file references, search path and format registry. Graphics & key settings 80%, RF_StoreSettings 90%, RF_DataIO 70%: the hotkeys parse but do nothing, nothing calls the store's set at run time, and the existing readers do not use the DataIO code yet. Corrections for the map: the bindings are a payload in the .set store at tag path 0x6440A401/0x6440CB01, and `exa_game::bindings::Bindings::decode` differs from the original's reader (version < 101 shape, the payload's count, slot 21 cleared); a positive GUISize works only on an 800-pixel-tall display mode; the launcher loads the store before the ini.

Done (change `reproduce-the-hardware-configuration`, new spec `hardware-config`): the settings screen's unit: `exa_formats::hw_config` has the options, defaults, slider steps and labels (with their scales), the page's three copies (live, draft and saved; opening rereads the settings file), Apply's lines in order, Cancel and Defaults per page, the GPU benchmark's protocol, score and quality tiers, the Deck preset, key names, and the startup handlers (`Volume` × 0.01, `ShowGameTips`, `CheckPoints`); `exa_ui::settings` drives the rows (live versus draft effects, the anti-aliasing row's rule, the aim-speed row, the 22 Controls rows' key cells and binding slots); `exa_game::hw_config` runs open, Apply, Cancel, Defaults, rebinding and the benchmark (`--benchmark`) and pushes the live options into shadow-map sizes, MSAA, super-sampling, the audio, the frame-rate readout and the window. The launcher seeds the options from `Exanima.ini` with its own flags winning, and super-sampling stays on when the file has no `Supersample` line. On the RTX 3090 `play exanima01` starts with the same picture and super-sampling as `gpu-launch-92`, with the user's settings, with no settings file (it is created) and with a full retail-style file (`target/verify/gpu-launch-94{,-noini,-ini}.{log,png}`). Not in: drawing the Settings and Controls forms, the key-read screen, Steam Deck and Steam Input, the shading, texture and shadow-detail renderer features (the texture loader keeps the retail user's values), the aim-speed scaling and the first-run auto-detection at launch.

Done (change `reproduce-the-frame-loop`, new spec `frame-loop`): the original's frame and platform layers as rule models with tests, wired where they have an effect. `exa_game::frame` has the frame-rate cap (`FramePacer`: automatic, vertical-sync and explicit caps, periods rounded half to even, 32 ms unfocused), the real and game clocks with the paused-time accumulator, the redraw-flag idle wait, the periodic timer table, the 128-slot deferred-call ring, the status history, the timed overlay and the logger's presentation levels; `exa_game::window` the display-mode list, the refresh rate (unknown below 30 Hz), window fitting, the 640 × 480 minimum, the full-screen rule (borderless over the desktop, exclusive only for a listed `ScreenWidth`/`ScreenHeight` other than the desktop's), the present mode and Alt+Enter; `exa_render::passes` the scene pass's order with its depth states and the blended and opaque list orders; `exa_render::view` the super-sampling level table and jitters, the separate render-scale mode, the projection, point projection, rectangle clip and framebuffer viewport and region stacks. `exarust::frame_loop` applies the cap only when `--max-fps` or `FPSLimit` asks, releases held keys and buttons on a focus change, binds Alt+Enter, and a background window runs on winit's low-power 32 ms update (unattended runs stay at full speed). The branch presented with plain Fifo, the original's swap interval 1; on the RTX 3090 `play exanima01` (`--perf-log 5 --frames 1800`, load average about 8–13) then averaged 35.1, 45.9 and 51.1 ms a frame against 28.2, 28.1 and 36.5 ms for the tree before it (`target/verify/perf/frameloop-{new,base}-{a,b,c}.log`), because Fifo holds each late frame to the next blank. Exarust now keeps the adaptive `AutoVsync` while its frames are slower than the display (`ADAPTIVE_VSYNC`, ad3a0263): 33.0 ms against 36.7 ms with Fifo over three interleaved runs each (`frameloop-head{auto,fifo}-{a,b,c}.log`), within the 32.0–35.5 ms of the last measurement above. The game starts and keeps its picture (`target/verify/gpu-launch-97.{log,png}`, mean colour (29.9, 19.0, 12.1) against (30.8, 19.4, 12.3) for `gpu-launch-96`; this session's window opened at 2400 × 1350 for both trees, a display-scale difference, not the change). Not in: the OpenGL/Win32 glue, Steam Input, the log file, drawing the status line and overlay, stereo, the jittered four-sub-view drawing (the ordered 2 × 2 grid stays), the decal bias and particle pre-pass as render nodes, the hook lists.

Done (change `reproduce-the-gamepad-action-sets`; steaminput lane): the original's Steam Input gamepad is in `exa_game::gamepad` on Bevy's gilrs backend (`bevy_gilrs`); Steam is not linked. Reproduced and checked against the listing: the menu, interact and combat action sets with their manifest names and kinds and a documented default pad table; the set table with the gameplay layer that the last form's pop restores; the menu frame, with its 0.25 dead zone, its 0.1 double-precision analog bits and dt-scaled cursors every frame, its 400/150/30 ms repeats including the fast quirk, edge-only keys and 3 ms priming; the directional pick at the GUI unit; the gameplay frame, with its camera-frame move and run flag, the power-bar and dialogue step modes, the look routine at the orbit rate (stick up steeper), the stepped zoom (Zoom_In −20 a frame), the 500 ms power-tree hold, the command word, the Alt and crouch key bits and sprint; and rumble (computed; the original's vibration call is a stub). Wired: in controller mode (the Controls page's `InputMode`, or for the session once a pad is used) the pad drives movement (keys translated by `pad_keys`), combat steps, aim, attack, stab, crouch, sprint and the chase camera's look and zoom; at integration its weapon swap and its R3 combat toggle (Exarust's own) went through the key handler as their bound keys, so the handler's gates apply. Keyboard and mouse are unchanged. Not in: a drawn form toolkit to receive menu keys and picks; the consumers for the inventory, power, dialogue, pause and camera-mode bits; AimSpeed's aim-cursor ease; a hit caller for rumble; the raw XInput fallback as a live path; Steam's binding panel, glyphs, floating keyboard and Deck detection.

Done (fixups-1021): names instead of retail addresses in the living docs, the archived changes and `exa_ui::menu` (a `MenuStep` enum); textures decoded at the live texture quality, detail and super-sampling (`load-textures-at-the-live-options`); the bindings payload read and written as the original's saved key table of 23 Controls rows in scan codes (`read-the-saved-key-table`); the head-tracking yaw and pitch through the engine's planar angle and arc sine; and a test that composes and validates the surface shader with Bevy's modules.

Done (fixups2-1021): fields that were named after object offsets (and one after a retail address) are named by meaning in `exa_combat::damage`, `exa_game::create` and `thaum`, `exa_physics::cloth` and `motile`, and `exa_ui::menu`, `skill` and `skin`, with the guesses labelled; the mouse codes follow the original's table, so the middle button saves as Mouse 3 and Back and Forward as Mouse 4 and 5 (and a rebound extra button now works); the viewer's level-load line counts hung props among the solver's bodies.

## 5. Method: how the original is studied

| tool | use |
|---|---|
| `exa-tool` + hex inspection | format structure, corpus statistics |
| REA (Ghidra provider) on `Exanima.exe` — see `RE-TOOLING.md` | readers (`TRFCReader`, image loader, packed-image decompressor), constants (physics, damage), rules. FPC RTTI names classes and virtual tables. Decompiled code is never copied: rules and constants are rewritten in Exarust's own terms |
| RenderDoc / apitrace on the running game (Proton/Wine) | GL state, plain GLSL as compiled, draw order, light counts, shadow resolutions |
| Side-by-side capture | identical scenes and camera paths in both runtimes; screenshot and video diffs |
| Official sources | [baremettle.com](https://www.baremettle.com/) news archive (`/news/`), forums and Steam devlogs: release notes, combat demo videos and design statements, used as behavioural reference |
| Community references | the community format spec Structs.txt; the Rust [Exanima Modding Toolkit](https://codeberg.org/ExanimaModding/Toolkit); [ExanimaREP](https://github.com/Horse4Horse/ExanimaREP) (IDA database with ~330 named classes); translation projects that solved the image RLE; Jango's Blender RFC editor. All collected in `docs/research/online-research.md`. They show where to look; they are not evidence. Respect each licence: some are source-available with no redistribution, so read them for facts only. |

Facts, offsets and dumps stay in `context/` (untracked). The tracked tree
holds the runtime and the documentation of formats and behaviour.

## 6. Risks

| risk | impact | mitigation |
|---|---|---|
| Physics feel diverges from the original | the game stops being Exanima | own character solver; fixed timestep; tuning harness that replays recorded inputs; compare against videos of the original |
| Scripts or shader libraries resist analysis | missing story logic or exact shading | RenderDoc capture for shaders; story logic can be re-authored from observed behaviour; the community spec covers part of the script language |
| Format drift: the game went 64-bit in 0.9.5 and community specs predate it | wrong field layouts | every claim re-checked by a corpus parse of the installed build |
| GI and many shadowed lights cost too much on wgpu | looks flatter | baked GI per level as a first step; budgeted shadow updates |
| Level sections only partly understood | missing props or lights | corpus statistics plus Ghidra on the level reader |
| Bevy API churn | upgrade cost | pinned version, thin integration crates |
| Legal | takedown | no assets, no decompiled code, no keys; the user must own the game |

## 7. Immediate next steps

[`COMPONENT-MAP.md`](COMPONENT-MAP.md) sets every component of the original
against Exarust and lists the largest missing ones. In order:

1. Gait walking, clip blending, and parts' spheres fitted to their
   meshes (Phase 3). The standing leg bend, recovery steps, the
   support pins, the run overlay on the walk, the sprint overlay
   weight, the sprint clock, the sprint row's thrust, gait plants,
   the backpedal (Walk played backwards), knockback steps, and the
   tighter run cap (1 − 0.4·encumbrance while the sprint latch is kept,
   1 − 0.3·encumbrance on the walk keys) are in. So are the locomotion request, the
   body tick's movement decision, turn table and alignment, and the clip
   clocks, and the locomotion routine itself (run blend, gait table and
   columns, walk start, stride clocks), the torso twist and the head
   look; the walking drive they compute is not applied yet (the capsule
   stands in; C18 needs the feet's lag factor). The hand takes hold of
   props and corpses, the idle camera turns and the key handler
   dispatches; lifting, doors and wheels wait on an item cursor.
   The sphere fit itself is in (1060 of 1503 shipped bodies bit for
   bit), and so are the limb, root and factory-weapon builders over it;
   calling them from character generation and the item factory is not.
   Hung props hang from the world by their stored links, the simulation
   sector's grid, meet tests and pair stage follow the original, and the
   dynamics world unit's grids, purges and placement query are records
   the game does not drive yet.
2. Inventory, equipment and containers, then blocking and parrying
   (Phase 4). Equipping by occupancy, container transfers, a parry that
   stops a blow and a shield's yellow leak are in; every blow now runs
   the original's damage routine in full, with the shield block, the
   parry and the turned parry, but nothing in the running game raises a
   guard yet. An inventory pane places
   icons, checks bulk and size, and hits the top-most one. A marked flag, a
   missing required mark, and an unlisted id are refused. A limit of 0
   does not refuse. An item card chooses a weapon, apparel, shield, or plain
   page and quarters its coin word. A weapon row is shown only above 5,
   at that byte times 0.05. Apparel coverage is always that byte times
   0.05, and its other rows appear only above 0.25. A plain card places
   its icon by dividing the span minus the icon size by 2. A stats card
   places it from the unit, and a picture wider than eleven units shifts
   it. A shield's weight is that byte times 0.01 and its impact is that
   byte times 0.05. Encumbrance uses that scale unless the byte is 0.
   Flags sharing a bit with 0x06000000 store weight as 1 and encumbrance
   as 8. Arena turns rank and points on. Story and a fresh practice
   turn them off. A shown rank reads the top three skill bits, and points
   are the low five bits times one half. A description phrase is chosen
   from the rows whose range holds the value, and a repeated long word
   adds nothing. A weapon sentence wraps that phrase with the name,
   "It is", ", but" or " and", and "A " or "An ". When the id word is
   -1, a 0x10 byte and the byte after it are deleted. An apparel sentence
   drops four letters from a bracket, says "They are " or "It is", joins
   the same side of 50 with ", but", strips a leading "A ", and keeps a
   non-final dot as a tail. Quality reads 14 made rows. A material from 1
   through 8 uppercases a side prefix, and a 0x10 there is replaced by
   the material name and a space. A record's possessions, weapon-set
   swaps and the item windows' rules are in (the panel's layouts, codecs,
   drops and card request, the card's condition blocks and pronouns, and
   the character view's camera, background and drags, checked instruction
   by instruction), and so are the character record's live rules (equip
   lock, trade, loadouts, skills); drawing the windows, the cards and the
   3D figure, and re-attaching held models, are not. A weapon part tree
   redistributes its masses in two passes (`exa_physics::weapon_mass`): a
   saved mass of zero is latched from the live mass, each live mass becomes
   1.25 times that saved mass, and both the mass word and the inverse word
   become the reciprocal of a half-even, half-by-inverse share of the
   caller's scale. A part's shield stat block (`ShieldBlock`) is marked
   `0x80000000` only when it is created; the three floats are the shield
   section's bytes 4, 5 and 6 times 0.01 in double, or those bytes eight
   bytes into a looked-up section object, and a missed lookup leaves them
   unwritten. Injuries beyond damage are not.
3. The original's NPC brain: perception, targets and the combat state that
   drives the same inputs as the player (Phase 6). A path on the grid, the
   look-ahead, and an order that writes the player's heading and velocity
   are in. A contact's closeness, hostility and the notice, watch, engage
   or flee choice are in. Once the fight has started, the blow is an
   overhead, a thrust or a side, and energy under eight tenths of the
   maximum refuses it. A disturbance above 0.3 is walked to, and an
   alert of 0.2 looks around or faces a target more than 45° off. The
   roam, patrol and sentry cycles are in. Roles, relations and the
   attitude now set each placed character's rating of the player, and the
   role and dialogue scripts run in `exa_script`, which shares the role
   list with the AI and runs the role init scripts; nothing consumes the
   scripts' effects in the running game yet. The combatant's
   perception pass, the corrected position score and the 18 KB combat
   state drive the duellist; next are the brain's live triggers for them,
   the role database feeding the pass, NPCs planning and following paths
   in the fight (for flight, follow and the chase route) with the planner,
   which now follows the original's instructions (`--debug-path` shows
   it), the swing release and the cast chooser.
4. Portal clipping and doors' open state, and level transitions
   (Phase 2) are in. The lid in the lights' cube faces is not. The
   original's scene-node visibility (sector classes, portal scissor,
   cut-away probe and fade) is modelled in `exa_world::scene_nodes`; putting it on
   the draw path is next, then driving the content stream and the terrain
   stream from the level loader (textures already stream in the game).
   The rooms' door portals, light reach and light spill are in but not
   called; per-room light
   aliases and the occluder/lid segment tests come next.
5. `add-static-mesh-decoding` is still open, waiting on a visual mirroring
   check against the running game.
6. Wire the reproduced mode units into the running game (Phases 7 and 8):
   the object database's tiers into the level loader and the save
   package, the save slots behind the menus, and forms that draw the
   character creator, the arena match board, the skill screen and
   technique selector, the loadout and emblem editors, the divination
   reading, the teleporter's destination screen and the Settings and
   Controls pages from their rule models (their rules, the extended
   controls and the GUI core's window manager are in; the F1 menu already
   routes through the window manager). The base draw skin's batch is
   complete (`exa_ui::skin`); submitting it to a render pass is the first
   step.
   The game-main flow and the splash are modelled (`exa_game::flow` logs
   its steps; `--splash` shows the splash), I and J open the inventory and
   journal tasks without drawing them, and `exa-tool saves` reads the
   retail saves and rebuilds them byte for byte; applying the flow's
   steps and loading a save into a session come next.
   The arena's match, staff, report, save chunks and company windows'
   rules are in. A fresh tournament match (`match_layout::raise_fresh_match`)
   places that bracket: a side stops at the first body the generator
   refuses and reports "Failed to generate match motile."; the bodies
   created on that side take familiarity 100 toward each other, both ways,
   and both of their AI clocks wait 3000 ms; fewer than three formed sides
   are hostile and three or more are a free-for-all; the player's side is
   stood from the company list and the player's own entry is not spawned
   again. A rematch (`match_layout::stand_rematch`) drops bodies in the
   dead mode, stores a tracked fighter's health on the full scale, and
   stands the survivors on the player's spread with a 10000 ms grace.
   Creating the scene bodies is not in. A hub
   screen to draw and wire the windows comes next. The gamepad drives the
   player in controller mode; its menu keys and picks wait for those drawn
   forms.
7. Thaumaturgy in play (Phase 7): the casts' gates and prices, the active
   power classes and the force and environment effect nodes are tested
   rules; a power bar or cast dispatcher that creates the classes from the
   casts comes first, then `exa_combat` reading the shell's damping and
   the strikes' factor, drawing the force effects with their own programs,
   GI injection of the emissives, and registering the force entities with
   the AI's sight.
8. What remains after batch 18. Most of the new units are tested models
   that nothing in the running game calls: the rest of the unwired rules
   (cast effect entities, Mind casts, the character decomposition passes,
   the cloth helpers, camera planes, shadow sizes, the splines caller, the
   ray-query caller, the screens' painting and callbacks) need their
   callers. The small components still unpacked or unrated are the
   scene-node, material and object helpers at 0% in the component map,
   and the largest-missing table of [`COMPONENT-MAP.md`](COMPONENT-MAP.md)
   is the worklist. There is no fixups list under `docs/reviews/`; the
   open questions each change notes ("Open", "Not wired") are the list.

Done (change `reproduce-the-base-library-helpers`; osmain2 lane, reviewed): the base library's fixed 16-byte names (ellipsis past sixteen, offset writes, appends, byte/word/ANSI compares) are in `exa_formats::text`, the packed RGBA8 store in `exa_formats::colour`, the vector lengths, SSE-NaN component min/max, unit projection, XZ heading and yaw/elevation directions in `exa_physics::engine_math`, the priority hook lists in `exa_game::hooks`, and the tagged deferred-call cancellation and the performance-counter millisecond clock in `exa_game::frame`. The review corrected the case-insensitive ANSI compare (it falls back on the length difference), the ANSI lowering (it never reaches the last byte) and the projection's sum order, and folded duplicate converters, frac and heading into existing code. Nothing is wired into the frame loop yet. FLX_System about 63% → 72%.

Round 38 settingsio3: the engine's memory blocks, whole-file reads and the settings exit save; review fixed the search-path miss logging, the block copy and a duplicated KeyDef counter.

Round 40 gamegui6: the form base's client-area refresh and freed-form slot sweep; review removed a duplicate wheel rule and rewrote the sweep over real slot state.

Round 40 osmain4: the super-sampled pass schedule, the renderer's list walk, the particle pass and the overlay layout as tested models; review fixed the scissor bit, the particle masks and the overlay box height.

Round 44 sweep-render (reviewed): mostly tested models with no caller; the lane's invented sphere shadow mode, interleaved layout, upload copy and prepass change were removed.

Round 44 weather (E1, cl/weather): rain and thunder are live, GPU-checked on arenao3 with night storm, day storm and dry seeds.
